`AGENTS.md`:

```md
# AGENTS.md

Guidance for AI coding agents and human contributors working in this repository.

## Project

Eigen is an open-source, machine-learning combat anti-cheat for Minecraft servers running
Paper or Folia. This repository contains only the server plugin (the "client" of the Eigen
backend). It observes each player through network packets, models what that player's game
client sees, turns every client tick into a row of features, cuts those rows into windows and
streams them to the backend for dataset collection and inference. Training code, models and
the backend live elsewhere and are not part of this repository.

The wire contract with the backend is `docs/protocol.md`. It is the source of truth: if code
and document disagree, the code is wrong, or the document must be changed deliberately and the
protocol version bumped.

## Stack

- Scala 3 (LTS line), compiled for Java 25. sbt 2 builds the project.
- PacketEvents is a runtime plugin dependency (`depend` in `plugin.yml`); it is never shaded.
- Third-party runtime libraries (`scala3-library`, `jsoniter-scala-core`, `lettuce-core`) are
  bundled into the fat jar by `sbt paper/assembly` so the distributed jar is self-contained.
- Compile-time-only libraries (macros) are `Provided`: MacWire for wiring the object graph,
  jsoniter-scala macros for JSON codecs.

## Commands

```bash
sbt testFull            # all tests (plain `sbt test` is incremental in sbt 2)
sbt paper/assembly      # the plugin jar
```

Golden protocol fixtures live in `docs/fixtures` and are shared with the backend's tests. After
an intentional wire change, regenerate them (the sbt server must be restarted to see the
variable):

```bash
sbt shutdown && EIGEN_WRITE_FIXTURES=1 sbt core/testFull
```

`paper/src/test/.../StreamLiveSuite` runs against a live backend when `EIGEN_TEST_BACKEND` and
`EIGEN_TEST_TOKEN` are set, and is skipped otherwise.

## Layout

- `core` — platform-independent model: feature schema, per-player simulation, lag
  compensation, tick history, windows and the wire codec. It must never depend on Bukkit,
  Paper or PacketEvents, so it can be tested in isolation and reused offline.
- `config` — a small declarative DSL for typed configuration reads: each key has a type, a
  default and a check, and a section is a `for` comprehension over its keys. No platform code.
- `commands` — a small Scala DSL over Paper's Brigadier command API.
- `paper` — the adapter: packet listeners, transport, dataset collection, commands, wiring.
- `docs` — the protocol specification and its byte-level fixtures.

## Architecture

- **Components and systems.** Per-player state is split into small mutable components with
  primitive fields. Packet handlers only record facts into components. Once per client tick a
  fixed schedule of stateless systems derives features and writes one row. Keep new logic in
  this shape: a component for state, a system for derivation, a clear place in the schedule.
- **Column-major history.** Rows are stored in a ring buffer of per-column arrays. Windows are
  copied out with bulk array copies, matching the column-major wire format.
- **Thread confinement.** A player's simulation is touched only by that connection's network
  thread. Other threads hand work over through the player's mailbox or through transaction
  tasks; never mutate simulation state from another thread.
- **Lag compensation.** Outbound packets that change what the client sees are bracketed with
  transactions; their effect is applied when the client acknowledges them. Our transaction ids
  are positive; GrimAC uses non-positive ids, so both can run on one server. Never consume or
  cancel a pong that is not ours.
- **Client versions.** Old clients connect through ViaVersion. Version-specific behaviour is
  captured once in `ClientProfile`; simulation code reads the profile, never the version.

## Engineering rules

- Correctness first, then clarity, then performance. The per-tick path must not allocate:
  reuse scratch objects, prefer primitive arrays and `while` loops, use `inline` and opaque
  types for zero-cost abstractions. Ordinary Scala is fine off the hot path.
- Features must be invariant to things that identify a server, map, kit or connection
  (absolute positions, ping, client version, brand). Such values may only appear as metadata
  columns, which are never model inputs.
- The schema, mask reasons, item kinds and window kinds are part of the dataset format.
  Append only; never renumber or reorder existing entries without a schema version bump.
- Validate everything read from the network; never let malformed input throw on the network
  thread.
- No player names or raw UUIDs leave the server; players are identified by a salted HMAC.
- Every source file starts with the GPL-3.0 header. Files derived from GrimAC or Shard keep the
  attribution block naming those projects and their authors; keep it when editing.
- Tests accompany behaviour: pure logic in `core` gets unit tests, wire changes get fixture
  updates.
- Comments explain intent and invariants, not what the next line does.

## Working style for agents

- Read `docs/protocol.md` and the code you are about to change first.
- Make the smallest change that fully solves the task; do not refactor unrelated code.
- Keep the build warning-free and run `sbt testFull` before finishing.
- If a requirement is ambiguous or conflicts with these rules, stop and ask.

```

`README.md`:

```md
# Eigen

Open-source machine-learning combat anti-cheat for Paper and Folia servers.

Eigen models each player's client from the packets it exchanges with the server: mouse
movement in sensitivity-independent counts, movement keys, clicks, and the geometry of the
player's aim at their target, with lag compensation for what the client actually sees. Every
client tick becomes a row of features; rows are cut into combat windows (around hits and the
start of fights) and calm segments (the player's own baseline), and streamed to the Eigen
backend for dataset collection and inference.

## Requirements

- Paper or Folia 26.1+ on Java 25
- [PacketEvents](https://github.com/retrooper/packetevents) installed as a plugin
- Clients of any version: 1.21.2+ clients are observed tick by tick; older clients connecting
  through ViaVersion are supported with version-specific handling

## Commands

| Command | Permission | |
|---|---|---|
| `/eigen status` | `eigen.command` | Backend connection and collection overview |
| `/eigen alerts` | `eigen.alerts` | Toggle staff alerts for backend verdicts |
| `/eigen vl <player>` | `eigen.alerts` | Inspect a player's violation count and latest verdict |
| `/eigen debug <player>` | `eigen.admin` | Live simulation state of a player |
| `/eigen exempt <player>` | `eigen.admin` | Stop streaming a player |
| `/eigen collect start <player> <legit\|cheat\|unknown> [key=value…] [note]` | `eigen.collect` | Record a labelled session |
| `/eigen collect mark <player> <on\|off> [note]` | `eigen.collect` | Mark a cheat toggle inside a recording |
| `/eigen collect stop <player>` | `eigen.collect` | Finish a recording |
| `/eigen collect list` | `eigen.collect` | Recordings in progress |
| `/eigen record start [note]` | `eigen.record` | Self-record your own gameplay as `legit` |
| `/eigen record stop` | `eigen.record` | Stop your own self-service recording |
| `/eigen record status` | `eigen.record` | Check your mouse calibration and recording status |

Recordings go to the backend, to `plugins/Eigen/collect/` or both (`collect.sink` in
`config.yml`). The local format matches what the backend stores.

## Build

```bash
sbt testFull paper/assembly
```

The jar is written to `target/out/jvm/scala-3.9.0/eigen-paper/Eigen-<version>.jar`. It bundles
the Scala runtime and all required libraries (except PacketEvents, which is loaded as a plugin).

## Protocol

The plugin talks to the backend over a single authenticated WebSocket stream, described in
[docs/protocol.md](docs/protocol.md).

## Credits

Eigen stands on the work of two open-source anti-cheats, and parts of it are derived from
their code (each such file says so in its header):

- [GrimAC](https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim) by DefineOutside and contributors — the
  transaction-based lag compensation and entity interpolation model.
- [Shard](https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard) by KaelusAI — the mouse sensitivity estimation,
  aim geometry and the overall approach of ML detection on per-tick packet data.

## License

GPL-3.0-only, see [LICENSE](LICENSE).
```

`build.sbt`:

```sbt
ThisBuild / organization := "eigen"
ThisBuild / version := "0.1.0-SNAPSHOT"
ThisBuild / scalaVersion := "3.9.0"
ThisBuild / licenses := Seq("GPL-3.0-only" -> url("https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-3.0.html"))

// Paper 26.1+ runs only on Java 25.
ThisBuild / scalacOptions ++= Seq("-release", "25", "-deprecation", "-feature", "-Wunused:all")

val paperApiVersion = "26.2.build.134-stable"
val packetEventsVersion = "2.14.0"
val jsoniterVersion = "2.41.5"
val lettuceVersion = "6.5.5.RELEASE"
val macwireVersion = "2.6.7"
val munitVersion = "1.3.6"

val paperRepos = Seq(
  "papermc" at "https://repo.papermc.io/repository/maven-public/",
  "codemc-releases" at "https://repo.codemc.io/repository/maven-releases/",
)

lazy val root = (project in file("."))
  .aggregate(core, config, commands, shard, paper)
  .settings(
    name := "eigen",
    publish / skip := true,
  )

// Simulation, features, windows and the wire codec. No Bukkit, Paper or PacketEvents types,
// so the same code runs on the server, in tests and in offline replay.
lazy val core = (project in file("core"))
  .settings(
    name := "eigen-core",
    libraryDependencies ++= Seq(
      "com.github.plokhotnyuk.jsoniter-scala" %% "jsoniter-scala-core" % jsoniterVersion,
      "com.github.plokhotnyuk.jsoniter-scala" %% "jsoniter-scala-macros" % jsoniterVersion % Provided,
      "org.scalameta" %% "munit" % munitVersion % Test,
    ),
  )

// Declarative configuration reads: typed keys with defaults and validation. No platform code.
lazy val config = (project in file("config"))
  .settings(
    name := "eigen-config",
    libraryDependencies += "org.scalameta" %% "munit" % munitVersion % Test,
  )

// A small Scala 3 DSL over Paper's Brigadier command API.
lazy val commands = (project in file("commands"))
  .settings(
    name := "eigen-commands",
    resolvers ++= paperRepos,
    libraryDependencies += "io.papermc.paper" % "paper-api" % paperApiVersion % Provided,
  )

// Optional Shard 2 (KaelusAI/Shard) 100%-equivalent dataset collector for head-to-head benchmarks.
// Loaded via ServiceLoader only when `collect.shard-compat.enabled: true`.
lazy val shard = (project in file("shard"))
  .settings(
    name := "eigen-shard",
    resolvers ++= paperRepos,
    libraryDependencies ++= Seq(
      "io.papermc.paper" % "paper-api" % paperApiVersion % Provided,
      "com.github.retrooper" % "packetevents-spigot" % packetEventsVersion % Provided,
      "org.scalameta" %% "munit" % munitVersion % Test,
    ),
  )

// Paper/Folia adapter: packet listeners, transport, collection, commands.
lazy val paper = (project in file("paper"))
  .dependsOn(core, commands, config, shard)
  .settings(
    name := "eigen-paper",
    resolvers ++= paperRepos,
    libraryDependencies ++= Seq(
      "io.papermc.paper" % "paper-api" % paperApiVersion % Provided,
      // Supplied at runtime by the PacketEvents plugin (see `depend` in plugin.yml).
      "com.github.retrooper" % "packetevents-spigot" % packetEventsVersion % Provided,
      // Lettuce's netty modules are all bundled by Paper itself (the plugin classloader
      // resolves io.netty from the server first), so they stay out of the fat jar.
      ("io.lettuce" % "lettuce-core" % lettuceVersion).excludeAll(
        ExclusionRule(organization = "io.netty", name = "netty-buffer"),
        ExclusionRule(organization = "io.netty", name = "netty-codec"),
        ExclusionRule(organization = "io.netty", name = "netty-common"),
        ExclusionRule(organization = "io.netty", name = "netty-handler"),
        ExclusionRule(organization = "io.netty", name = "netty-resolver"),
        ExclusionRule(organization = "io.netty", name = "netty-transport"),
        ExclusionRule(organization = "io.netty", name = "netty-transport-native-unix-common"),
      ),
      // Compile-time only: autowire expands to plain constructor calls.
      "com.softwaremill.macwire" %% "macros" % macwireVersion % Provided,
      "com.github.plokhotnyuk.jsoniter-scala" %% "jsoniter-scala-macros" % jsoniterVersion % Provided,
      "org.scalameta" %% "munit" % munitVersion % Test,
    ),
    Compile / resourceGenerators += Def.task {
      val out = (Compile / resourceManaged).value / "plugin.yml"
      IO.write(out, pluginYml(version.value))
      Seq(out)
    }.taskValue,
    // Bundle Scala and third-party runtime dependencies directly into the plugin jar so server
    // operators only need PacketEvents installed.
    assembly / assemblyMergeStrategy := {
      case PathList("META-INF", "versions", _, "module-info.class") => MergeStrategy.discard
      case PathList("META-INF", "io.netty.versions.properties") => MergeStrategy.first
      case PathList("META-INF", "INDEX.LIST") => MergeStrategy.discard
      case "module-info.class" => MergeStrategy.discard
      case other => (assembly / assemblyMergeStrategy).value(other)
    },
    assembly / assemblyJarName := s"Eigen-${version.value}.jar",
  )

def pluginYml(version: String): String =
  s"""name: Eigen
     |version: '$version'
     |main: eigen.paper.EigenPlugin
     |description: Open-source ML combat anti-cheat
     |api-version: '1.13'
     |folia-supported: true
     |depend: [packetevents]
     |permissions:
     |  eigen.command:
     |    default: op
     |  eigen.alerts:
     |    default: op
     |  eigen.collect:
     |    default: op
     |  eigen.record:
     |    default: op
     |  eigen.admin:
     |    default: op
     |""".stripMargin

```

`commands/src/main/scala/eigen/commands/Arg.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.commands

import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*

import com.mojang.brigadier.arguments.{ArgumentType, IntegerArgumentType, StringArgumentType}
import com.mojang.brigadier.context.CommandContext
import io.papermc.paper.command.brigadier.CommandSourceStack
import io.papermc.paper.command.brigadier.argument.ArgumentTypes
import io.papermc.paper.command.brigadier.argument.resolvers.selector.PlayerSelectorArgumentResolver
import org.bukkit.entity.Player

/**
 * A typed command argument: how Brigadier parses it, how to read the parsed value back, and
 * optional tab completions. Declare arguments once as values and read them with `ctx(arg)`.
 */
final class Arg[A] private (
    val name: String,
    private[commands] val brigadier: ArgumentType[?],
    private[commands] val read: (CommandContext[CommandSourceStack], String) => A,
    private[commands] val completions: Option[CommandSourceStack => Iterable[String]],
):
  /** Adds tab completions computed from the command source. */
  def suggest(f: CommandSourceStack => Iterable[String]): Arg[A] = Arg(name, brigadier, read, Some(f))

  /** Adds fixed tab completions. */
  def suggest(values: String*): Arg[A] = suggest(_ => values)

object Arg:
  /** A single word without spaces. */
  def word(name: String): Arg[String] =
    Arg(name, StringArgumentType.word(), StringArgumentType.getString, None)

  /** The rest of the input, spaces included. Must be the last argument. */
  def text(name: String): Arg[String] =
    Arg(name, StringArgumentType.greedyString(), StringArgumentType.getString, None)

  def int(name: String, min: Int = Int.MinValue, max: Int = Int.MaxValue): Arg[Int] =
    Arg(name, IntegerArgumentType.integer(min, max), (c, n) => IntegerArgumentType.getInteger(c, n), None)

  /** One online player, by name or selector. */
  def player(name: String): Arg[Player] =
    Arg(
      name,
      ArgumentTypes.player(),
      (c, n) =>
        val players = c.getArgument(n, classOf[PlayerSelectorArgumentResolver]).resolve(c.getSource).asScala
        players.headOption.getOrElse(throw CommandFailure("No player matched."))
      ,
      None,
    )

  /** A word restricted to `values`, completed from them. */
  def oneOf(name: String, values: String*): Arg[String] =
    val allowed = values.toSet
    Arg(
      name,
      StringArgumentType.word(),
      (c, n) =>
        val v = StringArgumentType.getString(c, n)
        if allowed(v) then v else throw CommandFailure(s"Expected one of: ${values.mkString(", ")}."),
      Some(_ => values),
    )

```

`commands/src/main/scala/eigen/commands/CommandTree.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.commands

import java.util.logging.Level

import scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer
import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*

import com.mojang.brigadier.Command
import com.mojang.brigadier.arguments.ArgumentType
import com.mojang.brigadier.builder.ArgumentBuilder
import com.mojang.brigadier.context.CommandContext
import com.mojang.brigadier.exceptions.CommandSyntaxException
import com.mojang.brigadier.tree.LiteralCommandNode
import io.papermc.paper.command.brigadier.{CommandSourceStack, Commands}
import io.papermc.paper.plugin.lifecycle.event.types.LifecycleEvents
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component
import net.kyori.adventure.text.format.NamedTextColor
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin

/** Thrown from an executor to stop with a message to the sender. */
final class CommandFailure(message: String) extends RuntimeException(message, null, false, false)

/** What an executor sees: parsed arguments and the sender. */
final class Ctx private[commands] (val raw: CommandContext[CommandSourceStack]):
  def apply[A](arg: Arg[A]): A = arg.read(raw, arg.name)
  def sender: CommandSender = raw.getSource.getSender
  def reply(message: Component): Unit = sender.sendMessage(message)
  def reply(message: String): Unit = reply(Component.text(message, NamedTextColor.GRAY))
  def fail(message: String): Nothing = throw CommandFailure(message)

/** A node of the command tree under construction. */
final class Node private[commands] (private[commands] val label: Either[String, Arg[?]]):
  private[commands] val children = ArrayBuffer[Node]()
  private[commands] var permissions: Seq[String] = Nil
  private[commands] var executor: Option[Ctx => Unit] = None
  private[commands] var help: String = ""

/** A complete top-level command, ready to register. */
final class CommandSpec private[commands] (val name: String, val description: String, val aliases: Seq[String], root: Node):

  /** Builds the Brigadier tree. `onError` reports unexpected executor failures. */
  def build(onError: (String, Throwable) => Unit): LiteralCommandNode[CommandSourceStack] =
    convert(root, onError).asInstanceOf[com.mojang.brigadier.builder.LiteralArgumentBuilder[CommandSourceStack]].build()

  /** One line per runnable path, for help output. */
  def usage: Seq[String] =
    val out = ArrayBuffer[String]()
    def walk(n: Node, prefix: String): Unit =
      val here = n.label match
        case Left(literal) => s"$prefix $literal".trim
        case Right(arg) => s"$prefix <${arg.name}>"
      if n.executor.isDefined then out += (if n.help.isEmpty then here else s"$here — ${n.help}")
      n.children.foreach(walk(_, here))
    walk(root, "/")
    out.toSeq.map(_.replaceFirst("^/ ", "/"))

  private def convert(n: Node, onError: (String, Throwable) => Unit): ArgumentBuilder[CommandSourceStack, ?] =
    val builder: ArgumentBuilder[CommandSourceStack, ?] = n.label match
      case Left(literal) => Commands.literal(literal)
      case Right(arg) =>
        val b = Commands.argument(arg.name, arg.brigadier.asInstanceOf[ArgumentType[Any]])
        arg.completions.foreach: complete =>
          b.suggests: (ctx, sb) =>
            val typed = sb.getRemainingLowerCase
            complete(ctx.getSource).iterator.filter(_.toLowerCase.startsWith(typed)).foreach(sb.suggest)
            sb.buildFuture()
        b
    if n.permissions.nonEmpty then
      val perms = n.permissions
      builder.requires(src => perms.exists(src.getSender.hasPermission))
    n.executor.foreach: run =>
      builder.executes: (ctx: CommandContext[CommandSourceStack]) =>
        val c = Ctx(ctx)
        try
          run(c)
          Command.SINGLE_SUCCESS
        catch
          case f: CommandFailure =>
            c.reply(Component.text(f.getMessage, NamedTextColor.RED))
            0
          // Parse and resolve errors (unknown player, bad selector): Brigadier reports them.
          case e: CommandSyntaxException => throw e
          case e: Exception =>
            onError(s"/$name", e)
            c.reply(Component.text("Internal error, see the server log.", NamedTextColor.RED))
            0
    n.children.foreach(child => builder.`then`(convert(child, onError)))
    builder

/**
 * A small DSL for Paper's Brigadier commands. Blocks nest with context functions, so the code
 * mirrors the command tree:
 *
 * {{{
 * val Target = Arg.player("player")
 * command("eigen", "Eigen anti-cheat"):
 *   requires("eigen.command")
 *   literal("status"):
 *     run(ctx => ctx.reply("ok"))
 *   literal("debug"):
 *     arg(Target):
 *       run(ctx => ctx.reply(ctx(Target).getName))
 * }}}
 */
object CommandTree:

  def command(name: String, description: String, aliases: String*)(body: Node ?=> Unit): CommandSpec =
    val root = Node(Left(name))
    body(using root)
    CommandSpec(name, description, aliases, root)

  def literal(name: String)(body: Node ?=> Unit)(using parent: Node): Unit =
    val n = Node(Left(name))
    body(using n)
    parent.children += n

  def arg[A](a: Arg[A])(body: Node ?=> Unit)(using parent: Node): Unit =
    val n = Node(Right(a))
    body(using n)
    parent.children += n

  /** Restricts this node and everything below it to senders with `permission`. */
  def requires(permission: String)(using n: Node): Unit = n.permissions = Seq(permission)

  /** Restricts this node and everything below it to senders with any of `permissions`. */
  def requiresAny(permissions: String*)(using n: Node): Unit = n.permissions = permissions

  /** Makes this node runnable. `help` shows up in [[CommandSpec.usage]]. */
  def run(f: Ctx => Unit)(using n: Node): Unit = n.executor = Some(f)

  def run(help: String)(f: Ctx => Unit)(using n: Node): Unit =
    n.help = help
    n.executor = Some(f)

  /** Registers commands with Paper's lifecycle so they survive `/reload` of the datapacks. */
  def register(plugin: JavaPlugin, specs: CommandSpec*): Unit =
    val onError = (where: String, e: Throwable) => plugin.getLogger.log(Level.SEVERE, s"Command $where failed", e)
    plugin.getLifecycleManager.registerEventHandler(
      LifecycleEvents.COMMANDS,
      event => specs.foreach(s => event.registrar().register(s.build(onError), s.description, s.aliases.asJava)),
    )
```

`config/src/main/scala/eigen/config/Read.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.config

/**
 * A typed read from a [[Source]]. Each key carries its default and its validation, so a schema is
 * a single `for` comprehension over reads:
 *
 * {{{
 * val backend = Read.section("backend"):
 *   for
 *     enabled <- Read.bool("enabled", default = false)
 *     retries <- Read.int("retries", default = 3, min = 0)
 *   yield Backend(enabled, retries)
 * }}}
 *
 * A missing key yields its default. The first problem stops the read, and its message names the
 * dotted path of the offending key, for example `backend.retries: must be at least 0`.
 */
final class Read[A] private (private[config] val run: Source => Either[String, A]):
  def map[B](f: A => B): Read[B] = new Read(source => run(source).map(f))

  def flatMap[B](f: A => Read[B]): Read[B] = new Read(source => run(source).flatMap(a => f(a).run(source)))

  /** Reads the whole document. */
  def from(source: Source): Either[String, A] = run(source)

object Read:
  def pure[A](value: A): Read[A] = new Read(_ => Right(value))

  def fail[A](message: String): Read[A] = new Read(_ => Left(message))

  /** Fails with `key: message` unless `condition` holds; for checks that relate several keys. */
  def ensure(condition: Boolean, key: String, message: String): Read[Unit] =
    if condition then pure(()) else fail(s"$key: $message")

  /** A scalar key with a default; `parse` validates and converts the raw value. */
  def key[A](name: String, default: A)(parse: Any => Either[String, A]): Read[A] =
    new Read(source =>
      source.value(name) match
        case None => Right(default)
        case Some(raw) => parse(raw).left.map(problem => s"$name: $problem"))

  def text(name: String, default: String): Read[String] =
    key(name, default) {
      case s: String => Right(s)
      case other => typeError(other, "text")
    }

  def bool(name: String, default: Boolean): Read[Boolean] =
    key(name, default) {
      case b: Boolean => Right(b)
      case other => typeError(other, "true or false")
    }

  def int(name: String, default: Int, min: Int = Int.MinValue, max: Int = Int.MaxValue): Read[Int] =
    key(name, default) {
      case n: Number if n.doubleValue % 1 == 0 =>
        val v = n.longValue
        if v < min || v > max then Left(s"must be between $min and $max, got $v") else Right(v.toInt)
      case other => typeError(other, "a whole number")
    }

  def double(name: String, default: Double): Read[Double] =
    key(name, default) {
      case n: Number => Right(n.doubleValue)
      case other => typeError(other, "a number")
    }

  /** One of `options`, written as text; `default` must be one of them. */
  def choice[A](name: String, default: String)(options: (String, A)*): Read[A] =
    val table = options.toMap
    key(name, table(default)) {
      case s: String => table.get(s.toLowerCase).toRight(s"must be one of ${table.keys.mkString(", ")}, got '$s'")
      case other => typeError(other, "text")
    }

  /** A nested section. A missing section reads every key from its default; a scalar is an error. */
  def section[A](name: String)(inner: Read[A]): Read[A] =
    new Read(source =>
      val child = source.section(name)
      if child.isEmpty && source.value(name).isDefined then Left(s"$name: must be a section")
      else inner.run(child.getOrElse(Source.Empty)).left.map(problem => s"$name.$problem"))

  private def typeError(got: Any, expected: String): Left[String, Nothing] =
    Left(s"must be $expected, got ${describe(got)}")

  private def describe(value: Any): String = value match
    case s: String => s"'$s'"
    case other => other.getClass.getSimpleName

```

`config/src/main/scala/eigen/config/Source.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.config

/** One level of a configuration document: scalars by key, and nested sections. */
trait Source:
  /** The scalar stored under `key`, or `None` when absent. */
  def value(key: String): Option[Any]

  /** The nested section under `key`, or `None` when absent or not a section. */
  def section(key: String): Option[Source]

object Source:
  /** A document with no keys: every read falls back to its default. */
  val Empty: Source = of(Map.empty)

  /** A source over nested Scala maps; used for in-memory documents and tests. */
  def of(entries: Map[String, Any]): Source = new MapSource(entries)

  private final class MapSource(entries: Map[String, Any]) extends Source:
    def value(key: String): Option[Any] = entries.get(key).filterNot(_.isInstanceOf[Map[?, ?]])

    def section(key: String): Option[Source] = entries.get(key) match
      case Some(nested: Map[?, ?]) => Some(new MapSource(nested.asInstanceOf[Map[String, Any]]))
      case _ => None

```

`config/src/test/scala/eigen/config/ReadSuite.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.config

class ReadSuite extends munit.FunSuite:

  private case class Backend(enabled: Boolean, retries: Int, mode: String)

  private val backend: Read[Backend] = Read.section("backend"):
    for
      enabled <- Read.bool("enabled", default = false)
      retries <- Read.int("retries", default = 3, min = 0, max = 10)
      mode <- Read.choice("mode", "fast")("fast" -> "fast", "slow" -> "slow")
    yield Backend(enabled, retries, mode)

  private def read(entries: Map[String, Any]): Either[String, Backend] =
    backend.from(Source.of(entries))

  test("missing keys and sections fall back to their defaults"):
    assertEquals(read(Map.empty), Right(Backend(false, 3, "fast")))
    assertEquals(read(Map("backend" -> Map("retries" -> 5))), Right(Backend(false, 5, "fast")))

  test("present values are read with their types"):
    val doc = Map("backend" -> Map("enabled" -> true, "retries" -> 7, "mode" -> "slow"))
    assertEquals(read(doc), Right(Backend(true, 7, "slow")))

  test("errors name the dotted path of the offending key"):
    assertEquals(read(Map("backend" -> Map("retries" -> 11))), Left("backend.retries: must be between 0 and 10, got 11"))
    assertEquals(read(Map("backend" -> Map("enabled" -> "yes"))), Left("backend.enabled: must be true or false, got 'yes'"))
    assertEquals(read(Map("backend" -> Map("mode" -> "turbo"))), Left("backend.mode: must be one of fast, slow, got 'turbo'"))

  test("a section given as a scalar is an error, not silently defaulted"):
    assertEquals(read(Map("backend" -> 5)), Left("backend: must be a section"))

  test("numbers are accepted as whole numbers only when they are whole"):
    assertEquals(read(Map("backend" -> Map("retries" -> 2.0))), Right(Backend(false, 2, "fast")))
    assertEquals(read(Map("backend" -> Map("retries" -> 2.5))), Left("backend.retries: must be a whole number, got Double"))

  test("reads compose with map and flatMap"):
    val doubled = Read.int("n", default = 1).map(_ * 2)
    val chained = Read.int("n", default = 1).flatMap(n => Read.pure(n + 1))
    assertEquals(doubled.from(Source.of(Map("n" -> 4))), Right(8))
    assertEquals(chained.from(Source.of(Map.empty)), Right(2))

  test("ensure fails with the given key when a cross-key check does not hold"):
    val ordered: Read[(Int, Int)] = for
      low <- Read.int("low", 1)
      high <- Read.int("high", 2)
      _ <- Read.ensure(high >= low, "high", "must be at least low")
    yield (low, high)
    assertEquals(ordered.from(Source.of(Map("low" -> 5, "high" -> 3))), Left("high: must be at least low"))
    assertEquals(ordered.from(Source.of(Map("low" -> 1, "high" -> 3))), Right((1, 3)))

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/aim/RunningMode.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.aim

import eigen.core.wire.ByteSink

/**
 * Mode of the last `window` samples, where samples closer than a bucket width count as equal.
 * Flat arrays instead of a map: the number of distinct buckets is small and bounded by `window`.
 */
final class RunningMode(window: Int):
  private val history = new Array[Int](window) // bucket index of each sample, ring
  private var head = 0
  private var filled = 0

  private val keys = new Array[Double](window)
  private val counts = new Array[Int](window)
  private var buckets = 0

  def size: Int = filled

  def add(value: Double, width: Double): Unit =
    if filled == window then evict()
    var b = 0
    while b < buckets && math.abs(keys(b) - value) >= width do b += 1
    if b == buckets then
      keys(b) = value
      counts(b) = 0
      buckets += 1
    counts(b) += 1
    history((head + filled) % window) = b
    filled += 1

  /** The most popular bucket's value and count; `(0, 0)` when empty. */
  def mode: Double =
    val b = best
    if b < 0 then 0.0 else keys(b)

  def modeCount: Int =
    val b = best
    if b < 0 then 0 else counts(b)

  def clear(): Unit =
    head = 0; filled = 0; buckets = 0

  def writeTo(sink: ByteSink): Unit =
    sink.u16(filled)
    sink.u16(buckets)
    var b = 0
    while b < buckets do
      sink.f64(keys(b))
      sink.u16(counts(b))
      b += 1
    var i = 0
    while i < filled do
      sink.u8(history((head + i) % window))
      i += 1

  def readFrom(bytes: Array[Byte], at: Int): Int =
    if at + 4 > bytes.length then return -1
    val f = ByteSink.readU16(bytes, at)
    val bCount = ByteSink.readU16(bytes, at + 2)
    if f > window || bCount > window || (f == 0) != (bCount == 0) then return -1
    val end = at + 4 + bCount * 10 + f
    if end > bytes.length then return -1
    var pos = at + 4
    var sum = 0
    var b = 0
    while b < bCount do
      val k = ByteSink.readF64(bytes, pos)
      val c = ByteSink.readU16(bytes, pos + 8)
      if !java.lang.Double.isFinite(k) || k <= 0.0 || c <= 0 || c > f then return -1
      keys(b) = k
      counts(b) = c
      sum += c
      pos += 10
      b += 1
    if sum != f then return -1
    var i = 0
    while i < f do
      val idx = bytes(pos + i) & 0xff
      if idx >= bCount then return -1
      history(i) = idx
      i += 1
    head = 0
    filled = f
    buckets = bCount
    end

  private def best: Int =
    var b = -1
    var i = 0
    while i < buckets do
      if b < 0 || counts(i) > counts(b) then b = i
      i += 1
    b

  private def evict(): Unit =
    val b = history(head)
    head = (head + 1) % window
    filled -= 1
    counts(b) -= 1
    if counts(b) == 0 then
      // Move the last bucket into the hole and repoint its samples.
      val last = buckets - 1
      if b != last then
        keys(b) = keys(last)
        counts(b) = counts(last)
        var i = 0
        while i < filled do
          val s = (head + i) % window
          if history(s) == last then history(s) = b
          i += 1
      buckets -= 1

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/aim/Sensitivity.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.aim

import eigen.core.wire.ByteSink

/**
 * Estimates one axis' rotation step — the angle a single mouse count turns the camera.
 *
 * The client turns by whole mouse counts times a sensitivity-dependent step, so consecutive
 * per-tick deltas share that step as a common divisor. We take the GCD of consecutive deltas
 * and keep the running mode of it. Once stable, `delta / step` gives raw mouse counts
 * ("dots"), which do not depend on the player's sensitivity, and the distance of dots from a
 * whole number exposes rotations that did not come from a mouse.
 */
final class AxisEstimator:
  import AxisEstimator.*

  private val modes = RunningMode(Window)
  private var lastAbs = 0.0
  private var step = 0.0
  private var stable = false

  // Recent residual quality, to notice a sensitivity change and start over.
  private val recentOff = new Array[Boolean](DriftWindow)
  private var recentHead = 0
  private var recentCount = 0
  private var offCount = 0

  def converged: Boolean = stable
  def currentStep: Double = step
  def sampleCount: Int = modes.size

  /**
   * Feeds the rotation of one tick. `ulp` is the float precision of the absolute angle, which
   * grows with |yaw| and limits how small a step can still be resolved.
   */
  def observe(delta: Double, ulp: Double): Unit =
    val abs = math.abs(delta)
    if abs <= 0 || abs >= MaxSampleDelta then return
    val divisor = gcd(abs, lastAbs)
    if divisor > MinDivisor && ulp < divisor * UlpFraction then
      if lastAbs != 0 then modes.add(divisor, math.max(BucketWidth, ulp * 2))
      lastAbs = abs
      if modes.size > Significant && modes.modeCount > Significant then
        step = modes.mode
        stable = true
    if stable then trackDrift(abs)

  /** Signed mouse counts for `delta`, or NaN until the step is known. */
  def dots(delta: Double): Double = if stable then delta / step else Double.NaN

  def reset(): Unit =
    modes.clear()
    lastAbs = 0; step = 0; stable = false
    recentHead = 0; recentCount = 0; offCount = 0

  def writeTo(sink: ByteSink): Unit =
    sink.f64(lastAbs)
    sink.f64(step)
    sink.u8(if stable then 1 else 0)
    sink.u8(recentHead)
    sink.u8(recentCount)
    sink.u8(offCount)
    var i = 0
    while i < DriftWindow do
      sink.u8(if recentOff(i) then 1 else 0)
      i += 1
    modes.writeTo(sink)

  def readFrom(bytes: Array[Byte], at: Int): Int =
    val headerLen = 20 + DriftWindow
    if at + headerLen > bytes.length then return -1
    val lAbs = ByteSink.readF64(bytes, at)
    val stp = ByteSink.readF64(bytes, at + 8)
    val st = (bytes(at + 16) & 0xff) != 0
    val rHead = bytes(at + 17) & 0xff
    val rCount = bytes(at + 18) & 0xff
    val oCount = bytes(at + 19) & 0xff
    if !java.lang.Double.isFinite(lAbs) || lAbs < 0.0 || !java.lang.Double.isFinite(stp) || stp < 0.0 then return -1
    if st && stp <= MinDivisor then return -1
    if rHead >= DriftWindow || rCount > DriftWindow || oCount > rCount then return -1
    val next = modes.readFrom(bytes, at + headerLen)
    if next < 0 then return -1
    var i = 0
    while i < DriftWindow do
      recentOff(i) = (bytes(at + 20 + i) & 0xff) != 0
      i += 1
    lastAbs = lAbs
    step = stp
    stable = st
    recentHead = rHead
    recentCount = rCount
    offCount = oCount
    next

  private def trackDrift(abs: Double): Unit =
    val d = abs / step
    val off = math.abs(d - math.rint(d)) > DriftResidual
    if recentCount == DriftWindow then
      if recentOff(recentHead) then offCount -= 1
    else recentCount += 1
    recentOff(recentHead) = off
    if off then offCount += 1
    recentHead = (recentHead + 1) % DriftWindow
    if recentCount == DriftWindow && offCount * 2 > DriftWindow then reset()

object AxisEstimator:
  private inline val Window = 80
  private inline val Significant = 15
  private inline val MaxSampleDelta = 5.0
  private inline val BucketWidth = 1e-3
  private inline val UlpFraction = 1.0 / 16.0
  private inline val DriftWindow = 40
  private inline val DriftResidual = 0.2

  /** Smallest step the client can produce: sensitivity 0 → (0.2^3 * 8) * 0.15. */
  val MinDivisor: Double = math.pow(0.2, 3) * 8 * 0.15 - 1e-3

  /** Euclid on doubles, stopping at the smallest possible step. */
  def gcd(a0: Double, b0: Double): Double =
    if a0 == 0 then return 0
    var a = math.max(a0, b0)
    var b = math.min(a0, b0)
    while b > MinDivisor do
      val t = a - math.floor(a / b) * b
      a = b
      b = t
    a

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/entity/ClientProfile.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.entity

/**
 * Behaviour of a player's client version that the simulation must mirror. Built once per
 * player by the platform adapter from the protocol version; the simulation never inspects the
 * version itself.
 *
 * Old clients reach a modern server through ViaVersion, which translates packets but not the
 * client's own physics, timing and rounding — those are what this profile describes.
 */
final case class ClientProfile(
    protocol: Int,
    /** 1.21.2+: the client sends `CLIENT_TICK_END`, so every client tick is observed. */
    tickEnd: Boolean,
    /** 1.21.2+: `PLAYER_INPUT` carries the real movement keys. */
    realInput: Boolean,
    /** 1.9+ without tick-end packets: the client may skip a movement packet on idle ticks. */
    skipsIdleTicks: Boolean,
    /** 1.17–1.20.x: using an item sends an extra position-and-rotation packet mid-tick. */
    duplicateFlying: Boolean,
    /** Below 1.19.3 relative moves are packed with `floor`, not `round`. */
    floorPacking: Boolean,
    /** 1.8: positions are 1/32-block fixed point, so boxes carry that rounding as jitter. */
    coarsePositions: Boolean,
    /** 1.14.4 < v < 1.20.2 and 1.21.4 < v < 1.21.9: rotation-only packets freeze interpolation. */
    rotationFreezesInterpolation: Boolean,
    /** 1.21.11+: the trigonometry table is indexed in double precision. */
    doubleIndexTrig: Boolean,
    /** 1.8 checks two look vectors for a hit, 1.9+ three (a tick can lag behind). */
    lookCandidates: Int,
    /** Eye height while sneaking: 1.54 before 1.14, 1.27 since. */
    sneakEyeHeight: Double,
    /** Hitbox height of a crouching player as this client sees it: 1.8 before 1.14, 1.5 since. */
    crouchHeight: Float,
    /** Smallest position change the client reports: 0.03 before 1.18.2, 0.0002 since. */
    movementThreshold: Double,
)

object ClientProfile:
  /** The profile of a current client: everything observed, nothing legacy. */
  val Modern: ClientProfile = ClientProfile(
    protocol = Int.MaxValue,
    tickEnd = true,
    realInput = true,
    skipsIdleTicks = false,
    duplicateFlying = false,
    floorPacking = false,
    coarsePositions = false,
    rotationFreezesInterpolation = false,
    doubleIndexTrig = true,
    lookCandidates = 3,
    sneakEyeHeight = 1.27,
    crouchHeight = 1.5f,
    movementThreshold = 0.0002,
  )

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/entity/Interpolation.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.entity

import eigen.core.geom.Box

/**
 * Where the client may currently render an entity that the server moved, while the client
 * interpolates from the old position to the new one over `steps` ticks.
 *
 * The client's progress is only known within bounds: `low` is the fewest interpolation ticks it
 * can have run, `high` the most. The possible location is the union of the intermediate boxes
 * between those bounds. Instances are recycled by [[TrackedEntity]]; nothing here allocates.
 * Derived from GrimAC's reach interpolation (GPL-3.0).
 */
final class Interpolation:
  val start: Box = Box()
  val target: Box = Box()
  private var steps = 1
  private var low = 0
  private var high = 0
  private var unobservedTicks = false

  /**
   * Starts interpolating from `from` towards the point `(x, y, z)` over `steps` ticks.
   * `unobservedTicks` is true for clients that can tick without sending anything; their upper
   * bound is then unknown and runs straight to the end.
   */
  def reset(from: Box, x: Double, y: Double, z: Double, steps: Int, unobservedTicks: Boolean): Interpolation =
    start.copyFrom(from)
    target.setPoint(x, y, z)
    this.steps = math.max(1, steps)
    this.unobservedTicks = unobservedTicks
    low = 0
    high = if unobservedTicks then this.steps else 0
    this

  /** A move that does not interpolate (the entity stays wherever it possibly was). */
  def hold(from: Box): Interpolation =
    start.copyFrom(from)
    target.copyFrom(from)
    steps = 1
    unobservedTicks = false
    low = 0
    high = 0
    this

  /** Advances one client tick. `lowAdvances` is false while an older move is still settling. */
  def tick(lowAdvances: Boolean): Unit =
    if lowAdvances then low = math.min(low + 1, steps)
    high = if unobservedTicks then steps else math.min(high + 1, steps)

  /** Union of the positions the entity's feet can be at, written into `out`. */
  def possibleLocations(out: Box): Box =
    val inv = 1.0 / steps
    val sMinX = (target.minX - start.minX) * inv
    val sMaxX = (target.maxX - start.maxX) * inv
    val sMinY = (target.minY - start.minY) * inv
    val sMaxY = (target.maxY - start.maxY) * inv
    val sMinZ = (target.minZ - start.minZ) * inv
    val sMaxZ = (target.maxZ - start.maxZ) * inv
    // Intermediate boxes move linearly, so the union over steps low..high is spanned by its ends.
    out.minX = math.min(start.minX + low * sMinX, start.minX + high * sMinX)
    out.minY = math.min(start.minY + low * sMinY, start.minY + high * sMinY)
    out.minZ = math.min(start.minZ + low * sMinZ, start.minZ + high * sMinZ)
    out.maxX = math.max(start.maxX + low * sMaxX, start.maxX + high * sMaxX)
    out.maxY = math.max(start.maxY + low * sMaxY, start.maxY + high * sMaxY)
    out.maxZ = math.max(start.maxZ + low * sMaxZ, start.maxZ + high * sMaxZ)
    out

  /**
   * Bounds the start box around the target: an entity can not have been further away than
   * `steps` times its observed speed, plus jitter. Keeps an old, wide uncertainty from leaking
   * into every later move.
   */
  def clampStart(offX: Double, offY: Double, offZ: Double, teleport: Boolean): Unit =
    inline def lo(min: Double, max: Double, c: Double, off: Double): Double =
      val a = math.max(min, c - off)
      if a <= math.min(max, c + off) then a else if teleport then min else c
    inline def hi(min: Double, max: Double, c: Double, off: Double): Double =
      val b = math.min(max, c + off)
      if math.max(min, c - off) <= b then b else if teleport then max else c
    val cx = target.centerX
    val cy = target.centerY
    val cz = target.centerZ
    val x0 = lo(start.minX, start.maxX, cx, offX); val x1 = hi(start.minX, start.maxX, cx, offX)
    val y0 = lo(start.minY, start.maxY, cy, offY); val y1 = hi(start.minY, start.maxY, cy, offY)
    val z0 = lo(start.minZ, start.maxZ, cz, offZ); val z1 = hi(start.minZ, start.maxZ, cz, offZ)
    start.minX = x0; start.maxX = x1
    start.minY = y0; start.maxY = y1
    start.minZ = z0; start.maxZ = z1

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/entity/TrackedEntity.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.entity

import eigen.core.geom.Box

/** Coarse entity class, as reported in the `target_kind` meta column (0 = none). */
enum EntityKind(val code: Int):
  case Player extends EntityKind(1)
  case Living extends EntityKind(2)
  case Crystal extends EntityKind(3)
  case Other extends EntityKind(4)

/**
 * An entity as one particular client sees it: the server's latest position plus the window of
 * positions the client may still be interpolating through.
 *
 * Moves are applied in two transaction-bracketed steps, exactly when the client acknowledges
 * them (see [[eigen.core.latency.TransactionClock]]): [[onFirstTransaction]] when the move may
 * have been received, [[onSecondTransaction]] when it certainly was. Three interpolation slots
 * rotate so that steady movement allocates nothing.
 */
final class TrackedEntity(val id: Int, val kind: EntityKind, val steps: Int, profile: ClientProfile):
  import TrackedEntity.*

  /** Hitbox size as the client sees it; kept current by the platform adapter. */
  var width: Float = 0.6f
  var height: Float = 1.8f

  var dead = false

  /** Last transaction a move of this entity was bracketed with; used to force a fresh one. */
  var lastTransactionHung = 0

  // Latest server position, in the protocol's 1/4096-block fixed point.
  private var px = 0L
  private var py = 0L
  private var pz = 0L

  private var current: Interpolation | Null = null
  private var previous: Interpolation | Null = null
  private var spare: Interpolation | Null = null

  // Per-axis speed estimate (blocks per server update) bounding how far back a start can lie.
  private val speed = new Array[Double](3)
  private var hasLastTarget = false
  private var lastTargetX = 0.0
  private var lastTargetY = 0.0
  private var lastTargetZ = 0.0
  private var jump: Array[Double] | Null = null

  private val scratch = Box()

  def x: Double = px / Scale
  def y: Double = py / Scale
  def z: Double = pz / Scale

  def spawnAt(x: Double, y: Double, z: Double): Unit =
    if profile.coarsePositions then
      px = pack(math.floor(x * 32) / 32); py = pack(math.floor(y * 32) / 32); pz = pack(math.floor(z * 32) / 32)
    else
      px = pack(x); py = pack(y); pz = pack(z)

  /** The move may have reached the client: widen the possible area to cover it. */
  def onFirstTransaction(relative: Boolean, hasPos: Boolean, dx: Double, dy: Double, dz: Double): Unit =
    val preX = x
    val preY = y
    val preZ = z
    val old = current
    val startBox = if old != null then old.possibleLocations(scratch) else scratch.setPoint(x, y, z)

    if hasPos then
      if relative then
        if profile.floorPacking then
          if dx != 0 then px = math.floor(px + dx * Scale).toLong
          if dy != 0 then py = math.floor(py + dy * Scale).toLong
          if dz != 0 then pz = math.floor(pz + dz * Scale).toLong
        else
          px += math.round(dx * Scale); py += math.round(dy * Scale); pz += math.round(dz * Scale)
      else spawnAt(dx, dy, dz)
      updateSpeed(relative, preX, preY, preZ)

    // Retire the slot of the move before `old`; it can no longer matter.
    val recycled = previous
    previous = old
    val next = if spare != null then spare.nn else Interpolation()
    spare = recycled
    current =
      if !hasPos && profile.rotationFreezesInterpolation then next.hold(startBox)
      else
        next.reset(startBox, x, y, z, steps, profile.skipsIdleTicks)
        clampToSpeed(next)
        next

  /** The move certainly reached the client: the move before it has fully settled. */
  def onSecondTransaction(): Unit =
    if previous != null then
      if spare == null then spare = previous
      previous = null

  /** One client tick of interpolation. */
  def tick(): Unit =
    val cur = current
    if cur == null then return
    cur.tick(lowAdvances = previous == null)
    val prev = previous
    if prev != null then
      prev.tick(lowAdvances = true)
      cur.start.include(prev.possibleLocations(scratch))
      clampToSpeed(cur)

  /** Union of every hitbox the client may currently see, written into `out`. */
  def possibleHitbox(out: Box): Box =
    possibleLocations(out).growByDimensions(width, height)
    if profile.coarsePositions then out.expand(CoarseJitter, CoarseJitterY, CoarseJitter)
    out

  /** Union of the feet positions the client may currently see, written into `out`. */
  def possibleLocations(out: Box): Box =
    val cur = current
    if cur == null then out.setPoint(x, y, z)
    else
      cur.possibleLocations(out)
      val prev = previous
      if prev != null then out.include(prev.possibleLocations(scratch))
      out

  private def updateSpeed(relative: Boolean, preX: Double, preY: Double, preZ: Double): Unit =
    val nx = x
    val ny = y
    val nz = z
    if hasLastTarget then
      val decay = if steps <= 1 then 0.0 else (steps - 1.0) / steps
      decayInto(0, math.abs(nx - lastTargetX), decay)
      decayInto(1, math.abs(ny - lastTargetY), decay)
      decayInto(2, math.abs(nz - lastTargetZ), decay)
    hasLastTarget = true
    lastTargetX = nx; lastTargetY = ny; lastTargetZ = nz
    val jx = math.abs(nx - preX)
    val jy = math.abs(ny - preY)
    val jz = math.abs(nz - preZ)
    val lerped = jx < TeleportSnap && jy < TeleportSnap && jz < TeleportSnap
    jump = if relative || !lerped then null else Array(jx, jy, jz)

  private def decayInto(axis: Int, delta: Double, decay: Double): Unit =
    val decayed = speed(axis) * decay
    speed(axis) = if delta < SpeedSampleCap then math.max(decayed, delta) else decayed

  private def clampToSpeed(interp: Interpolation): Unit =
    val jitterXZ = if profile.coarsePositions then CoarseJitter else Jitter
    val jitterY = if profile.coarsePositions then CoarseJitterY else Jitter
    var ox = steps * math.max(speed(0), SpeedFloorXZ) * Safety + jitterXZ
    var oy = steps * math.max(speed(1), SpeedFloorY) * Safety + jitterY
    var oz = steps * math.max(speed(2), SpeedFloorXZ) * Safety + jitterXZ
    val j = jump
    if j != null then
      ox = math.max(ox, j(0) + jitterXZ)
      oy = math.max(oy, j(1) + jitterY)
      oz = math.max(oz, j(2) + jitterXZ)
    interp.clampStart(ox, oy, oz, teleport = j != null)

object TrackedEntity:
  private inline val Scale = 4096.0
  private inline def pack(v: Double): Long = math.round(v * Scale)

  private inline val SpeedFloorXZ = 0.05
  private inline val SpeedFloorY = 0.08
  private inline val Safety = 1.1
  private inline val Jitter = 1e-3
  private inline val CoarseJitter = 0.03125
  private inline val CoarseJitterY = 0.015625
  private inline val SpeedSampleCap = 8.0
  private inline val TeleportSnap = 64.0

  /** Interpolation steps the client uses for an entity type. */
  inline val LivingSteps = 3
  inline val MinecartSteps = 5
  inline val LegacyBoatSteps = 10

```

package eigen.core.geom

object Angles:

  inline val DegToRad = 0.017453292519943295
  inline val RadToDeg = 57.29577951308232

  /** Wraps an angle in degrees into [-180, 180). */
  inline def wrapDegrees(deg: Double): Double =
    val r = deg % 360.0
    if r >= 180.0 then r - 360.0
    else if r < -180.0 then r + 360.0
    else r

  /** Signed shortest turn from `from` to `to`, in degrees. */
  inline def turn(from: Double, to: Double): Double = wrapDegrees(to - from)
