`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/geom/Box.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.geom

/** Mutable axis-aligned box. Used as scratch space on the hot path to avoid allocation. */
final class Box(
    var minX: Double = 0,
    var minY: Double = 0,
    var minZ: Double = 0,
    var maxX: Double = 0,
    var maxY: Double = 0,
    var maxZ: Double = 0,
):

  def set(x0: Double, y0: Double, z0: Double, x1: Double, y1: Double, z1: Double): Box =
    minX = math.min(x0, x1); maxX = math.max(x0, x1)
    minY = math.min(y0, y1); maxY = math.max(y0, y1)
    minZ = math.min(z0, z1); maxZ = math.max(z0, z1)
    this

  def setPoint(x: Double, y: Double, z: Double): Box = set(x, y, z, x, y, z)

  def copyFrom(o: Box): Box = set(o.minX, o.minY, o.minZ, o.maxX, o.maxY, o.maxZ)

  /** Grows this box to also cover `o`. */
  def include(o: Box): Box =
    minX = math.min(minX, o.minX); maxX = math.max(maxX, o.maxX)
    minY = math.min(minY, o.minY); maxY = math.max(maxY, o.maxY)
    minZ = math.min(minZ, o.minZ); maxZ = math.max(maxZ, o.maxZ)
    this

  def expand(x: Double, y: Double, z: Double): Box =
    minX -= x; maxX += x
    minY -= y; maxY += y
    minZ -= z; maxZ += z
    this

  /** Turns a box of possible feet positions into a box of possible hitboxes. */
  def growByDimensions(width: Double, height: Double): Box =
    val half = width / 2
    minX -= half; maxX += half
    minZ -= half; maxZ += half
    maxY += height
    this

  inline def centerX: Double = (minX + maxX) * 0.5
  inline def centerY: Double = (minY + maxY) * 0.5
  inline def centerZ: Double = (minZ + maxZ) * 0.5
  inline def sizeX: Double = maxX - minX
  inline def sizeY: Double = maxY - minY
  inline def sizeZ: Double = maxZ - minZ

  /** Distance from a point to the closest point of the box (0 when inside). */
  def distanceTo(x: Double, y: Double, z: Double): Double =
    import Box.clamp
    val dx = x - clamp(x, minX, maxX)
    val dy = y - clamp(y, minY, maxY)
    val dz = z - clamp(z, minZ, maxZ)
    math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)

  override def toString: String = f"Box($minX%.3f,$minY%.3f,$minZ%.3f -> $maxX%.3f,$maxY%.3f,$maxZ%.3f)"

object Box:
  inline def clamp(v: Double, lo: Double, hi: Double): Double = if v < lo then lo else if v > hi then hi else v

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/geom/VanillaMath.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.geom

/** The client's lookup-table trigonometry, so look vectors match what the client computes. */
object VanillaMath:

  private inline val TableSize = 65536
  private inline val TableMask = 65535
  private inline val CosOffset = 16384.0
  private inline val Scale = 10430.378350470453
  private inline val LegacyScale = 10430.378f

  // sin(i * 2pi / 65536), identical to the client's table in float32.
  private val Sin: Array[Float] = Array.tabulate(TableSize)(i => math.sin(i / Scale).toFloat)

  /** @param doubleIndex clients 1.21.11+ index the table in double precision. */
  def sin(radians: Float, doubleIndex: Boolean): Float = Sin(index(radians, 0.0, doubleIndex))

  def cos(radians: Float, doubleIndex: Boolean): Float = Sin(index(radians, CosOffset, doubleIndex))

  private inline def index(radians: Float, offset: Double, doubleIndex: Boolean): Int =
    if doubleIndex then ((radians * Scale + offset).toLong & TableMask).toInt
    else (radians * LegacyScale + offset.toFloat).toInt & TableMask

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/history/CalmTrack.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.history

import eigen.core.schema.Schema
import eigen.core.wire.ByteSink

/**
 * Accumulates one contiguous run of calm (non-combat) ticks in the low-dimensional calm layout.
 *
 * Memory is bounded by `maxRows`: a run that reaches it is emitted and a new one starts, so a
 * player who never fights produces a sequence of segments rather than an ever-growing buffer.
 * Runs shorter than `minRows`, or with too few ticks of real mouse movement, are discarded.
 */
final class CalmTrack(minRows: Int, maxRows: Int, minActive: Float):
  require(minRows >= 1 && maxRows >= minRows)

  private val layout = Schema.Calm
  private val cols: Array[Array[Float]] = Array.fill(layout.cols.length)(new Array[Float](maxRows))
  // A tick counts as active when the mouse actually moved, not merely when rotation is unmasked.
  private val yawCol = layout.cols.indexOf(Schema.DotsYaw)
  private val pitchCol = layout.cols.indexOf(Schema.DotsPitch)

  private var rows = 0
  private var active = 0
  private var firstTick = 0
  private var epoch = 0
  private var engagement = 0
  private var lastSeq = -1L

  def length: Int = rows

  /**
   * Appends committed row `seq`. Returns a finished segment when this row closes one (the
   * buffer is full); a row that does not continue the current run starts a new one, so call
   * [[flush]] first when the caller knows the run ended.
   */
  def append(history: TickHistory, seq: Long, engagementNow: Int): Window | Null =
    val handedOff = rows > 0 && lastSeq == -1L
    val continues = rows > 0 && (handedOff || (seq == lastSeq + 1 && history.epochAt(seq) == epoch))
    val closed = if rows > 0 && !continues then flush() else null
    if rows == 0 then
      firstTick = history.tickAt(seq)
      epoch = history.epochAt(seq)
      engagement = engagementNow
    else if handedOff then
      firstTick = math.max(0, history.tickAt(seq) - rows)
      epoch = history.epochAt(seq)
    var i = 0
    while i < cols.length do
      cols(i)(rows) = history(layout.cols(i), seq)
      i += 1
    if cols(yawCol)(rows) != 0f || cols(pitchCol)(rows) != 0f then active += 1
    rows += 1
    lastSeq = seq
    if closed != null then closed
    else if rows == maxRows then flush()
    else null

  /** Ends the current run; returns it when it is long and active enough to keep. */
  def flush(): Window | Null =
    val n = rows
    val keep = n >= minRows && active >= minActive * n
    val out =
      if !keep then null
      else
        val data = Array.ofDim[Float](cols.length, n)
        var i = 0
        while i < cols.length do
          System.arraycopy(cols(i), 0, data(i), 0, n)
          i += 1
        Window(WindowKind.Calm, layout, epoch, engagement, firstTick, n, -1, data)
    rows = 0
    active = 0
    lastSeq = -1
    out

  def writeTo(sink: ByteSink): Unit =
    sink.u16(rows)
    sink.u16(active)
    sink.u32(engagement.toLong & 0xffffffffL)
    var c = 0
    while c < cols.length do
      var r = 0
      while r < rows do
        sink.f32(cols(c)(r))
        r += 1
      c += 1

  def readFrom(bytes: Array[Byte], at: Int): Int =
    if at + 8 > bytes.length then return -1
    val savedRows = ByteSink.readU16(bytes, at)
    val savedActive = ByteSink.readU16(bytes, at + 2)
    val savedEngagement = ByteSink.readI32(bytes, at + 4)
    if savedRows > 4096 || savedActive > savedRows || savedEngagement < 0 then return -1
    val end = at + 8 + savedRows * cols.length * 4
    if end > bytes.length then return -1
    if rows == 0 && savedRows > 0 then
      val keep = math.min(savedRows, maxRows)
      val skip = savedRows - keep
      var c = 0
      while c < cols.length do
        val colOff = at + 8 + (c * savedRows + skip) * 4
        var r = 0
        while r < keep do
          val v = ByteSink.readF32(bytes, colOff + r * 4)
          if !java.lang.Float.isFinite(v) then return -1
          cols(c)(r) = v
          r += 1
        c += 1
      var act = 0
      var r = 0
      while r < keep do
        if cols(yawCol)(r) != 0f || cols(pitchCol)(r) != 0f then act += 1
        r += 1
      rows = keep
      active = act
      engagement = savedEngagement
      firstTick = 0
      epoch = 1
      lastSeq = -1L
    end

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/history/TickHistory.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.history

import eigen.core.schema.Schema
import eigen.core.schema.Schema.Col

/**
 * Recent per-tick frames of one player, stored column-major (structure of arrays) in a ring.
 *
 * A row is written in place through [[update]] and published by [[commit]]; nothing is
 * allocated per tick. Rows are addressed by a monotonically increasing sequence number, so
 * windows can be cut by sequence range and copied out with at most two `arraycopy` calls
 * per column — the layout the wire format uses, too.
 */
final class TickHistory(val capacity: Int):
  require(capacity >= 2 && Integer.bitCount(capacity) == 1, "capacity must be a power of two")

  private val mask = capacity - 1
  private val cols: Array[Array[Float]] = Array.fill(Schema.width)(new Array[Float](capacity))
  private val ticks = new Array[Int](capacity)
  private val epochs = new Array[Int](capacity)

  /** Sequence number of the row being written. Committed rows are `[oldest, next)`. */
  private var next: Long = 0

  inline def nextSeq: Long = next
  // The slot of the row being built still holds the oldest row, so only capacity-1 rows are resident.
  def oldestSeq: Long = math.max(0L, next - capacity + 1)

  /** Writes `value` into column `col` of the row being built. */
  inline def update(col: Col, value: Float): Unit = cols(col.index)((next & mask).toInt) = value

  inline def update(col: Col, value: Boolean): Unit = update(col, if value then 1f else 0f)

  /** Publishes the row being built under the given client tick and epoch. */
  def commit(tick: Int, epoch: Int): Long =
    val slot = (next & mask).toInt
    ticks(slot) = tick
    epochs(slot) = epoch
    val seq = next
    next += 1
    // Clear the next slot so a column a system forgets to write reads as 0, never as stale data.
    val n = (next & mask).toInt
    var c = 0
    while c < cols.length do
      cols(c)(n) = 0f
      c += 1
    seq

  def holds(seq: Long): Boolean = seq >= oldestSeq && seq < next

  def apply(col: Col, seq: Long): Float =
    require(holds(seq), s"row $seq is not resident")
    cols(col.index)((seq & mask).toInt)

  /** Overwrites a committed value; used when a later system refines an earlier row. */
  def set(col: Col, seq: Long, value: Float): Unit =
    require(holds(seq), s"row $seq is not resident")
    cols(col.index)((seq & mask).toInt) = value

  def tickAt(seq: Long): Int = ticks((seq & mask).toInt)
  def epochAt(seq: Long): Int = epochs((seq & mask).toInt)

  /** True when `[from, from + rows)` is resident and lies inside a single epoch. */
  def contiguous(from: Long, rows: Int): Boolean =
    rows > 0 && from >= oldestSeq && from + rows <= next && epochAt(from) == epochAt(from + rows - 1)

  /** Copies `rows` values of `col` starting at sequence `from` into `dst` at `offset`. */
  def copyColumn(col: Col, from: Long, rows: Int, dst: Array[Float], offset: Int): Unit =
    val src = cols(col.index)
    val start = (from & mask).toInt
    val first = math.min(rows, capacity - start)
    System.arraycopy(src, start, dst, offset, first)
    if first < rows then System.arraycopy(src, 0, dst, offset + first, rows - first)

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/history/Window.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.history

import eigen.core.schema.Layout

/** Window kinds, as on the wire (protocol §5.1). */
enum WindowKind(val code: Int):
  case Attack extends WindowKind(1)
  case Engage extends WindowKind(2)
  case Calm extends WindowKind(3)

/**
 * A cut of a player's timeline, ready to encode. `data(i)` holds the rows of the i-th column of
 * `layout`. Routing fields (`handle`, `session`, `purpose`, `id`) are filled in by the transport.
 */
final class Window(
    val kind: WindowKind,
    val layout: Layout,
    val epoch: Int,
    val engagement: Int,
    val firstTick: Int,
    val rows: Int,
    val anchor: Int,
    val data: Array[Array[Float]],
):
  var handle: Int = 0
  var session: Int = 0
  var purpose: Int = 0
  var id: Int = 0

  def wireBytes: Int = Window.HeaderBytes + rows * layout.rowBytes

object Window:
  /** Size of the per-window header in a `WINDOWS` payload. */
  inline val HeaderBytes = 32

  inline val PurposeCollect = 1
  inline val PurposeInfer = 2

  /** Cuts `rows` rows starting at `from` out of `history` in the given layout. */
  def cut(
      history: TickHistory,
      kind: WindowKind,
      layout: Layout,
      from: Long,
      rows: Int,
      anchor: Int,
      engagement: Int,
  ): Window =
    val data = Array.ofDim[Float](layout.cols.length, rows)
    var i = 0
    while i < data.length do
      history.copyColumn(layout.cols(i), from, rows, data(i), 0)
      i += 1
    Window(kind, layout, history.epochAt(from), engagement, history.tickAt(from), rows, anchor, data)

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/latency/TransactionClock.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.latency

import java.util.ArrayDeque
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicIntegerArray

/**
 * Orders server-side knowledge by what the client has actually received.
 *
 * The server brackets interesting outbound packets with ping packets ("transactions") and the
 * client answers each with a pong in order. A task registered against transaction `n` runs once
 * the pong for `n` arrives, i.e. once the client has certainly processed everything sent before
 * it. Tasks are produced on the send path and executed on the receive path — a command buffer —
 * so all simulation state is touched from the receive side only.
 *
 * Ids are positive shorts. GrimAC uses ids `<= 0`, so both anti-cheats can run side by side, each
 * consuming only its own pongs.
 */
final class TransactionClock:
  import TransactionClock.*

  private final class Stamp(val id: Short, val sentNanos: Long)
  private final class Task(val transaction: Int, val run: Runnable)

  private val counter = AtomicInteger(0)
  // Id membership as bit sets: pending marks allocated-but-unsent ids, in-flight marks
  // sent-but-unanswered ones. Two fixed 4 KiB tables replace the per-id `java.lang.Short`
  // boxing and the per-pong stream scan this class used to pay. Ids wrap only after 32768
  // outstanding transactions, which no live connection reaches.
  private val pendingBits = AtomicIntegerArray(Bits)
  private val inFlightBits = AtomicIntegerArray(Bits)
  private val inFlight = ConcurrentLinkedQueue[Stamp]()

  /** Transactions the client has been sent / has answered. Task ids compare against these. */
  private val sent = AtomicInteger(0)
  private val received = AtomicInteger(0)

  private val tasks = ArrayDeque[Task]()

  @volatile private var rttMillis = 0.0

  def lastSent: Int = sent.get
  def lastReceived: Int = received.get

  /** Smoothed round-trip time of transactions, in milliseconds (0 until the first answer). */
  def rtt: Double = rttMillis

  /** Allocates the id for the next ping we send. The caller writes the packet. */
  def nextId(): Short =
    val raw = counter.getAndIncrement() & 0x7fff
    val id = (if raw == 0 then 0x7fff else raw).toShort
    setBit(pendingBits, id)
    id

  /** Called on the send path when a ping leaves; ignores pings that are not ours. */
  def onPingSent(id: Int): Unit =
    if isOurs(id) then
      val sid = id.toShort
      if testAndClearBit(pendingBits, sid) then
        setBit(inFlightBits, sid)
        inFlight.add(Stamp(sid, System.nanoTime()))
        sent.incrementAndGet()

  /**
   * Called on the receive path for every pong. Returns true when the pong was ours, so the
   * caller can cancel it; due tasks run immediately on the calling thread.
   */
  def onPong(id: Int): Boolean =
    if !isOurs(id) || !testBit(inFlightBits, id.toShort) then return false
    var stamp: Stamp | Null = null
    while
      stamp = inFlight.poll()
      stamp != null && { received.incrementAndGet(); clearBit(inFlightBits, stamp.nn.id); stamp.nn.id != id.toShort }
    do ()
    val s = stamp
    if s != null then
      val sample = (System.nanoTime() - s.sentNanos) / 1e6
      rttMillis = if rttMillis == 0 then sample else rttMillis + (sample - rttMillis) * RttWeight
    runDue()
    true

  /** Schedules `task` for when the client has answered transaction `transaction`. */
  def at(transaction: Int)(task: Runnable): Unit =
    tasks.synchronized(tasks.addLast(Task(transaction, task)))

  /**
   * Runs every task whose transaction is already answered (receive path only).
   *
   * Two listeners (e.g. Eigen and ShardCompat on `ENTITY_RELATIVE_MOVE` or `DESTROY_ENTITIES`)
   * each schedule a `t` task and a `t + 1` task, producing `[t, t + 1, t, t + 1]`. Scanning the
   * queue rather than stopping at the first `t + 1` head ensures every `<= done` task runs as
   * soon as `t` is acknowledged, matching GrimAC and Shard.
   */
  def runDue(): Unit =
    var keepGoing = true
    while keepGoing do
      val done = received.get
      val due = ArrayDeque[Runnable]()
      tasks.synchronized {
        val it = tasks.iterator()
        while it.hasNext do
          val t = it.next()
          if t.transaction <= done then
            due.addLast(t.run)
            it.remove()
      }
      if due.isEmpty then keepGoing = false
      else
        var r = due.pollFirst()
        while r != null do
          r.run()
          r = due.pollFirst()

  def clear(): Unit =
    tasks.synchronized(tasks.clear())

object TransactionClock:
  private inline val RttWeight = 0.125

  /** One bit per positive short id: 32768 ids in 1024 ints. */
  private inline val Bits = 1024

  /** Ours are positive shorts; GrimAC's are `<= 0`; anything wider than a short belongs to neither. */
  inline def isOurs(id: Int): Boolean = id > 0 && id == id.toShort

  // Bit twiddles as CAS loops: contention is one writer per id, so they run uncontended
  // and allocate nothing.
  private def setBit(bits: AtomicIntegerArray, id: Short): Unit =
    val b = id.toInt & 0x7fff
    val w = b >>> 5
    val m = 1 << (b & 31)
    var prev = bits.get(w)
    while (prev & m) == 0 && !bits.compareAndSet(w, prev, prev | m) do prev = bits.get(w)

  private def clearBit(bits: AtomicIntegerArray, id: Short): Unit =
    val b = id.toInt & 0x7fff
    val w = b >>> 5
    val m = 1 << (b & 31)
    var prev = bits.get(w)
    while (prev & m) != 0 && !bits.compareAndSet(w, prev, prev & ~m) do prev = bits.get(w)

  private def testBit(bits: AtomicIntegerArray, id: Short): Boolean =
    val b = id.toInt & 0x7fff
    (bits.get(b >>> 5) & (1 << (b & 31))) != 0

  private def testAndClearBit(bits: AtomicIntegerArray, id: Short): Boolean =
    val b = id.toInt & 0x7fff
    val w = b >>> 5
    val m = 1 << (b & 31)
    var prev = bits.get(w)
    while (prev & m) != 0 && !bits.compareAndSet(w, prev, prev & ~m) do prev = bits.get(w)
    (prev & m) != 0

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/schema/Manifest.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.schema

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
import java.util.zip.CRC32

/** Canonical manifest JSON of the schema (protocol §4) and its CRC-32. */
object Manifest:

  val json: String =
    val sb = StringBuilder()
    sb.append("{\"version\":").append(Schema.Version).append(",\"layouts\":[")
    for (layout, li) <- Schema.layouts.zipWithIndex do
      if li > 0 then sb.append(',')
      sb.append("{\"id\":").append(layout.id)
      sb.append(",\"name\":\"").append(layout.name).append("\",\"columns\":[")
      for (col, ci) <- layout.cols.zipWithIndex do
        if ci > 0 then sb.append(',')
        sb.append("[\"").append(col.name).append("\",\"").append(col.tpe.wire)
        sb.append("\",\"").append(col.stream.wire).append("\"]")
      sb.append("]}")
    sb.append("]}")
    sb.result()

  val bytes: Array[Byte] = json.getBytes(UTF_8)

  val hash: String =
    val crc = CRC32()
    crc.update(bytes)
    f"${crc.getValue}%08x"

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/schema/Schema.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.schema

import scala.collection.immutable.ArraySeq

/** Wire type of a column. In memory every column is a `Float`; the type only decides encoding. */
enum ColType(val size: Int, val wire: String):
  case U8 extends ColType(1, "u8")
  case I8 extends ColType(1, "i8")
  case U16 extends ColType(2, "u16")
  case F32 extends ColType(4, "f32")

/** Logical stream a column belongs to; `Mask` and `Meta` are never model inputs. */
enum Stream(val wire: String):
  case Rot extends Stream("rot")
  case Inp extends Stream("inp")
  case Tgt extends Stream("tgt")
  case Mask extends Stream("mask")
  case Meta extends Stream("meta")

/** A subset of columns sent together; see the manifest in `docs/protocol.md` §4. */
final class Layout(val id: Int, val name: String, val cols: ArraySeq[Schema.Col]):
  /** Bytes one row of this layout occupies on the wire. */
  val rowBytes: Int = cols.iterator.map(_.tpe.size).sum

/**
 * The per-tick feature frame. Order here is the wire order; append new columns at the end of
 * their layout and bump [[Schema.Version]].
 */
object Schema:
  import ColType.*
  import Stream.*

  inline val Version = 1

  /** A column of the per-tick frame: a zero-cost index into the frame's column arrays. */
  opaque type Col = Int

  object Col:
    extension (c: Col)
      inline def index: Int = c
      def name: String = names(c)
      def tpe: ColType = types(c)
      def stream: Stream = streams(c)

    /** The column with the given index; for codecs that walk columns generically. */
    def at(index: Int): Col =
      require(index >= 0 && index < width, s"no column $index")
      index

  private val nameBuf = Array.newBuilder[String]
  private val typeBuf = Array.newBuilder[ColType]
  private val streamBuf = Array.newBuilder[Stream]
  private var next = 0

  private def col(name: String, tpe: ColType, stream: Stream): Col =
    nameBuf += name; typeBuf += tpe; streamBuf += stream
    next += 1
    next - 1

  // Mouse: sensitivity-normalised counts and the residual from whole counts.
  val DotsYaw: Col = col("dots_yaw", F32, Rot)
  val DotsPitch: Col = col("dots_pitch", F32, Rot)
  val ResidYaw: Col = col("resid_yaw", F32, Rot)
  val ResidPitch: Col = col("resid_pitch", F32, Rot)

  // Keys and clicks.
  val Strafe: Col = col("strafe", I8, Inp)
  val Forward: Col = col("forward", I8, Inp)
  val Jump: Col = col("jump", U8, Inp)
  val Sprint: Col = col("sprint", U8, Inp)
  val Sneak: Col = col("sneak", U8, Inp)
  val Swing: Col = col("swing", U8, Inp)
  val Use: Col = col("use", U8, Inp)

  // Target geometry, in the player's view frame.
  val AimYaw: Col = col("aim_yaw", F32, Tgt)
  val AimPitch: Col = col("aim_pitch", F32, Tgt)
  val AimAngle: Col = col("aim_angle", F32, Tgt)
  val Distance: Col = col("distance", F32, Tgt)
  val RelFwd: Col = col("rel_fwd", F32, Tgt)
  val RelSide: Col = col("rel_side", F32, Tgt)
  val RelUp: Col = col("rel_up", F32, Tgt)
  val Attack: Col = col("attack", U8, Tgt)
  val Hit: Col = col("hit", U8, Tgt)
  val HitX: Col = col("hit_x", F32, Tgt)
  val HitY: Col = col("hit_y", F32, Tgt)
  val HitZ: Col = col("hit_z", F32, Tgt)
  val Miss: Col = col("miss", F32, Tgt)
  val Cooldown: Col = col("cooldown", F32, Tgt)
  val Crit: Col = col("crit", U8, Tgt)
  val SprintHit: Col = col("sprint_hit", U8, Tgt)
  val SameTarget: Col = col("same_target", U8, Tgt)

  // Validity of each model stream for this tick (1 = valid).
  val MaskRot: Col = col("m_rot", U8, Mask)
  val MaskInp: Col = col("m_inp", U8, Mask)
  val MaskTgt: Col = col("m_tgt", U8, Mask)

  // Context for balancing and audits. Never a model input.
  val Reasons: Col = col("reasons", U16, Meta)
  val Gate: Col = col("gate", U8, Meta)
  val Activity: Col = col("activity", U8, Meta)
  val Inflation: Col = col("inflation", F32, Meta)
  val TargetKind: Col = col("target_kind", U8, Meta)
  val ItemKind: Col = col("item_kind", U8, Meta)
  val PingBucket: Col = col("ping_bucket", U8, Meta)

  val names: Array[String] = nameBuf.result()
  val types: Array[ColType] = typeBuf.result()
  val streams: Array[Stream] = streamBuf.result()

  /** Number of columns in a full frame. */
  val width: Int = names.length

  val all: ArraySeq[Col] = ArraySeq.range(0, width)

  val Combat: Layout = Layout(1, "combat", all)

  val Calm: Layout = Layout(
    2,
    "calm",
    ArraySeq(
      DotsYaw, DotsPitch, ResidYaw, ResidPitch,
      Strafe, Forward, Jump, Sprint, Sneak, Swing, Use,
      MaskRot, MaskInp,
      Reasons, Activity, ItemKind,
    ),
  )

  val layouts: ArraySeq[Layout] = ArraySeq(Combat, Calm)

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/sim/Components.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.sim

import eigen.core.aim.AxisEstimator
import eigen.core.entity.TrackedEntity

/*
 * Components: plain mutable state with primitive fields, one concern each. Packet events write
 * them; systems (see Systems.scala) read and derive from them once per tick. All of it is
 * confined to the player's network thread.
 */

/** Camera rotation and the per-axis mouse-step estimators. */
final class Rotation:
  var yaw = 0f
  var pitch = 0f
  var lastYaw = 0f
  var lastPitch = 0f
  /** Rotation accumulated over the current tick. */
  var tickYaw = 0.0
  var tickPitch = 0.0
  var rotated = false
  /** The camera was set by the server this tick; its delta is not mouse movement. */
  var forced = false
  /** The delta crossed a jump no mouse can produce (lost packets, desync). */
  var discontinuity = false

  val yawAxis = AxisEstimator()
  val pitchAxis = AxisEstimator()

  val look = Look3()
  val lastYawLook = Look3()
  val lastLook = Look3()

  // Derived each tick by the rotation system.
  var dotsYaw = 0f
  var dotsPitch = 0f
  var residYaw = 0f
  var residPitch = 0f

/** Position and movement state of the player's body. */
final class Body:
  var x = 0.0
  var y = 0.0
  var z = 0.0
  var lastX = 0.0
  var lastY = 0.0
  var lastZ = 0.0
  var onGround = false
  var wasOnGround = false
  var fallDistance = 0.0
  var hasPosition = false

  var sprinting = false
  var sneaking = false
  var swimming = false
  var gliding = false
  var riptide = false
  var inVehicle = false
  var inWater = false

  var scale = 1f
  var interactionRange = 3.0

/** Movement keys. Reported by 1.21.2+ clients, inferred from motion for older ones. */
final class Keys:
  var forward = false
  var backward = false
  var left = false
  var right = false
  var jump = false
  var shift = false
  var sprint = false

  def any: Boolean = forward || backward || left || right || jump

  inline def strafe: Int = (if right then 1 else 0) - (if left then 1 else 0)
  inline def ahead: Int = (if forward then 1 else 0) - (if backward then 1 else 0)

/** Clicks of the current tick and the melee timing state. */
final class Clicks:
  var swing = false
  var use = false
  var attack = false
  var attackTarget = -1
  var prevAttackTarget = -1
  var lastAttackTick = Int.MinValue / 2
  var lastPlaceTick = Int.MinValue / 2
  var lastDigTick = Int.MinValue / 2

  /** Ticks since the attack strength was last reset (vanilla `attackStrengthTicker`). */
  var strengthTicks = 0
  var attackSpeed = 4.0

  // Captured at the moment of the attack, before the reset.
  var strengthAtAttack = 1f
  var sprintAtAttack = false

  def strength(ticks: Int): Float =
    val period = 20.0 / attackSpeed
    math.max(0.0, math.min(1.0, (ticks + 0.5) / period)).toFloat

/** Conditions that invalidate parts of a tick, with their expiry ticks. */
final class Conditions:
  var teleportUntil = Int.MinValue / 2
  var knockbackUntil = Int.MinValue / 2
  var guiOpen = false
  var idleRun = 0

/** Melee target selection and the combat gate. */
final class Combat:
  var target: TrackedEntity | Null = null
  var gate = false
  var lastCombatTick = Int.MinValue / 2
  /** Increments at every engagement; see protocol §5.3. */
  var engagement = 0
  var engagedThisTick = false

  // Target features of the current tick, derived by the target system.
  var aimYaw = 0f
  var aimPitch = 0f
  var aimAngle = 0f
  var distance = 0f
  var relFwd = 0f
  var relSide = 0f
  var relUp = 0f
  var hit = false
  var hitX = 0f
  var hitY = 0f
  var hitZ = 0f
  var miss = 0f
  var crit = false
  var inflation = 0f

/** Item kinds in the hotbar and offhand, for the `item_kind` meta column. */
final class Inventory:
  val hotbar = new Array[Byte](9)
  var offhand: Byte = 0
  var slot = 0

  def held: Byte = hotbar(slot)

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/sim/Geometry.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.sim

import eigen.core.geom.{Angles, Box, VanillaMath}

/** A unit look vector, mutable so it can live in a component and be reused every tick. */
final class Look3:
  var x = 0.0
  var y = 0.0
  var z = 1.0

  /** Sets this to the client's look vector for the given rotation (vanilla table trigonometry). */
  def fromRotation(yaw: Float, pitch: Float, doubleIndex: Boolean): Look3 =
    val pitchRad = (pitch * Angles.DegToRad).toFloat
    val yawRad = (-yaw * Angles.DegToRad).toFloat
    val cosPitch = VanillaMath.cos(pitchRad, doubleIndex)
    x = VanillaMath.sin(yawRad, doubleIndex) * cosPitch
    y = -VanillaMath.sin(pitchRad, doubleIndex)
    z = VanillaMath.cos(yawRad, doubleIndex) * cosPitch
    // The table leaves sin²+cos² off unity by up to 8e-5; normalise so angles near 0 stay exact.
    val len = math.sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z)
    if len > 0 then { x /= len; y /= len; z /= len }
    this

/**
 * Aim geometry between an eye position and a target box. Pure functions over primitives and
 * caller-owned scratch objects: nothing allocates. Derived in part from Shard's ReachUtils
 * (GPL-3.0).
 */
object Geometry:

  /** Smallest angle, in degrees, between `look` and the directions to the box centre and corners. */
  def minAngleToBox(ex: Double, ey: Double, ez: Double, look: Look3, b: Box): Double =
    var best = angleTo(ex, ey, ez, look, b.centerX, b.centerY, b.centerZ)
    var corner = 0
    while corner < 8 do
      val px = if (corner & 1) == 0 then b.minX else b.maxX
      val py = if (corner & 2) == 0 then b.minY else b.maxY
      val pz = if (corner & 4) == 0 then b.minZ else b.maxZ
      best = math.min(best, angleTo(ex, ey, ez, look, px, py, pz))
      corner += 1
    // Looking at the box from inside or straight through its interior counts as 0.
    if rayHits(ex, ey, ez, look, 64.0, b) then 0.0 else best

  def angleTo(ex: Double, ey: Double, ez: Double, look: Look3, px: Double, py: Double, pz: Double): Double =
    val dx = px - ex
    val dy = py - ey
    val dz = pz - ez
    val len = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
    if len < 1e-3 then 0.0
    else
      val dot = (look.x * dx + look.y * dy + look.z * dz) / len
      math.acos(math.max(-1.0, math.min(1.0, dot))) * Angles.RadToDeg

  /**
   * Signed yaw and pitch error from the look direction to the box centre, each normalised by the
   * box's angular half-size, so ±1 is the edge of the target whatever its distance. Writes
   * `out(0)` (yaw) and `out(1)` (pitch).
   */
  def aimError(ex: Double, ey: Double, ez: Double, yaw: Float, pitch: Float, b: Box, out: Array[Double]): Unit =
    out(0) = 0; out(1) = 0
    val dx = b.centerX - ex
    val dy = b.centerY - ey
    val dz = b.centerZ - ez
    val horizontal = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)
    val distance = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
    if distance < 1e-3 then return
    val deltaYaw = Angles.wrapDegrees(yaw - math.atan2(-dx, dz) * Angles.RadToDeg)
    val deltaPitch = pitch - (-math.atan2(dy, horizontal) * Angles.RadToDeg)
    val halfWidth = math.max(b.sizeX, b.sizeZ) / 2
    val angularWidth = math.atan2(halfWidth, distance) * Angles.RadToDeg
    val angularHeight = math.atan2(b.sizeY / 2, distance) * Angles.RadToDeg
    if angularWidth > 1e-6 then out(0) = deltaYaw / angularWidth
    if angularHeight > 1e-6 then out(1) = deltaPitch / angularHeight

  /**
   * Intersects the ray `eye + t * look`, `0 <= t <= reach`, with `b`. On a hit writes the hit point
   * into `hit(0..2)` and returns the distance; returns -1 on a miss. Slab method.
   */
  def rayHit(ex: Double, ey: Double, ez: Double, look: Look3, reach: Double, b: Box, hit: Array[Double]): Double =
    val t = rayEntry(ex, ey, ez, look, reach, b)
    if t >= 0 then
      hit(0) = ex + look.x * t
      hit(1) = ey + look.y * t
      hit(2) = ez + look.z * t
    t

  def rayHits(ex: Double, ey: Double, ez: Double, look: Look3, reach: Double, b: Box): Boolean =
    rayEntry(ex, ey, ez, look, reach, b) >= 0

  /** Entry distance of the ray into `b` within `reach`, or -1 on a miss. Slab method, no allocation. */
  private def rayEntry(ex: Double, ey: Double, ez: Double, look: Look3, reach: Double, b: Box): Double =
    var tMin = 0.0
    var tMax = reach
    // Inline so the slab bounds stay locals instead of captured, heap-boxed vars.
    inline def slab(o: Double, d: Double, lo: Double, hi: Double): Boolean =
      if math.abs(d) < 1e-12 then o >= lo && o <= hi
      else
        val t1 = (lo - o) / d
        val t2 = (hi - o) / d
        tMin = math.max(tMin, math.min(t1, t2))
        tMax = math.min(tMax, math.max(t1, t2))
        tMin <= tMax
    if slab(ex, look.x, b.minX, b.maxX) && slab(ey, look.y, b.minY, b.maxY) && slab(ez, look.z, b.minZ, b.maxZ) then tMin
    else -1.0

  /** Closest approach between the ray segment and the box, sampled along the segment. */
  def missDistance(ex: Double, ey: Double, ez: Double, look: Look3, reach: Double, b: Box): Double =
    var best = Double.MaxValue
    var i = 0
    while i <= MissSamples do
      val t = reach * i / MissSamples
      best = math.min(best, b.distanceTo(ex + look.x * t, ey + look.y * t, ez + look.z * t))
      i += 1
    best

  private inline val MissSamples = 32

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/sim/Handoff.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.sim

import eigen.core.schema.Schema
import eigen.core.wire.ByteSink

/**
 * Binary snapshot of a player's per-session baseline state (mouse sensitivity estimator,
 * engagement counter, and in-progress calm track) for cross-instance handoff via Garnet.
 */
object Handoff:
  private val Magic = 0x4547484f // "EGHO"
  private val Version = 1

  def encode(sim: PlayerSim): Array[Byte] =
    val sink = ByteSink(512)
    encodeTo(sim, sink)
    sink.toArray

  def encodeTo(sim: PlayerSim, sink: ByteSink): Unit =
    sink.u32(Magic.toLong & 0xffffffffL)
    sink.u8(Version)
    sink.u8(Schema.Version)
    sink.u8(Schema.Calm.cols.size)
    sink.u32(sim.combat.engagement.toLong & 0xffffffffL)
    sim.rotation.yawAxis.writeTo(sink)
    sim.rotation.pitchAxis.writeTo(sink)
    sim.calm.writeTo(sink)

  def restore(sim: PlayerSim, bytes: Array[Byte]): Boolean =
    if bytes == null || bytes.length < 11 then return false
    if ByteSink.readI32(bytes, 0) != Magic then return false
    if (bytes(4) & 0xff) != Version then return false
    if (bytes(5) & 0xff) != Schema.Version then return false
    if (bytes(6) & 0xff) != Schema.Calm.cols.size then return false
    val savedEngagement = ByteSink.readI32(bytes, 7)
    if savedEngagement < 0 then return false
    // Do not overwrite if local calibration has already converged on the new server
    if sim.rotation.yawAxis.converged && sim.rotation.pitchAxis.converged then return false
    val afterYaw = sim.rotation.yawAxis.readFrom(bytes, 11)
    if afterYaw < 0 then
      sim.rotation.yawAxis.reset()
      return false
    val afterPitch = sim.rotation.pitchAxis.readFrom(bytes, afterYaw)
    if afterPitch < 0 then
      sim.rotation.yawAxis.reset()
      sim.rotation.pitchAxis.reset()
      return false
    val afterCalm = sim.calm.readFrom(bytes, afterPitch)
    if afterCalm != bytes.length then
      sim.calm.flush()
      return false
    if savedEngagement > sim.combat.engagement then
      sim.combat.engagement = savedEngagement
    true

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/sim/ItemKind.scala`:

/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.

 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
