```scala
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.sim

/** Coarse class of a held item, for the `item_kind` meta column. Codes are dataset format. */
object ItemKind:
  final val Empty: Byte = 0
  final val Sword: Byte = 1
  final val Axe: Byte = 2
  final val Mace: Byte = 3
  final val Trident: Byte = 4
  final val Spear: Byte = 5
  final val Bow: Byte = 6
  final val Crossbow: Byte = 7
  final val Crystal: Byte = 8
  final val Totem: Byte = 9
  final val Shield: Byte = 10
  final val Food: Byte = 11
  final val Other: Byte = 12

  /** Classifies a namespaced item id such as `minecraft:diamond_sword`. */
  def of(id: String): Byte =
    val name = id.substring(id.indexOf(':') + 1)
    if name.isEmpty || name == "air" then Empty
    else if name.endsWith("_sword") then Sword
    else if name.endsWith("_axe") then Axe
    else if name.endsWith("_spear") then Spear
    else
      name match
        case "mace" => Mace
        case "trident" => Trident
        case "bow" => Bow
        case "crossbow" => Crossbow
        case "end_crystal" => Crystal
        case "totem_of_undying" => Totem
        case "shield" => Shield
        case "golden_apple" | "enchanted_golden_apple" | "cooked_beef" | "bread" | "golden_carrot" => Food
        case _ => Other

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/sim/PlayerSim.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.sim

import java.util.ArrayDeque

import eigen.core.entity.{ClientProfile, EntityKind, TrackedEntity}
import eigen.core.history.{CalmTrack, TickHistory, Window}
import eigen.core.geom.Box
import eigen.core.util.IntMap

/**
 * The server's model of one player's client, advanced tick by tick.
 *
 * Packet handlers call the `on*` methods, which only record facts into components. At the end
 * of every client tick [[endTick]] runs the [[Schedule]] of systems, which derive features,
 * write one row into the [[history]] and cut windows that are passed to `emit`. Everything is
 * confined to the player's network thread; other threads talk to it through transaction tasks
 * or the owner's command queue.
 */
final class PlayerSim(val profile: ClientProfile, val config: SimConfig, emit: Window => Unit):
  import PlayerSim.*

  val rotation = Rotation()
  val body = Body()
  val keys = Keys()
  val clicks = Clicks()
  val conditions = Conditions()
  val combat = Combat()
  val inventory = Inventory()

  val entities = IntMap[TrackedEntity](64)
  var selfId: Int = -1

  val history = TickHistory(config.historyCapacity)
  val calm = CalmTrack(config.calmMinTicks, config.calmMaxTicks, config.calmMinActive)
  private[sim] val attackCut = PendingCut()
  private[sim] val engageCut = PendingCut()

  /** Client tick index of the tick being built; see protocol §5.2. */
  var tick: Int = 0
  var epoch: Int = 1
  var pingBucket: Int = 0

  /** When false, rows are still recorded but no windows are cut (nobody consumes them). */
  var windowsWanted: Boolean = false

  private val pendingTeleports = ArrayDeque[PendingTeleport]()
  private val pendingRotations = ArrayDeque[PendingRotation]()

  // Scratch space shared by systems; never escapes a call.
  private[sim] val boxA = Box()
  private[sim] val boxB = Box()
  private[sim] val boxC = Box()
  private[sim] val pair = new Array[Double](3)

  /** Whether this tick has seen a (non-duplicate) movement packet; read by `RecordSystem`. */
  private[sim] var flyingThisTick = false

  // ---- client → server events -------------------------------------------------------------

  /**
   * A movement packet. For clients without tick-end packets it also closes the tick, unless it
   * is the extra mid-tick packet 1.17–1.20 clients send when using an item.
   */
  def onFlying(hasPos: Boolean, x: Double, y: Double, z: Double, hasRot: Boolean, yaw: Float, pitch: Float, onGround: Boolean): Unit =
    val duplicate = profile.duplicateFlying && hasPos && hasRot && body.hasPosition &&
      onGround == body.onGround &&
      math.abs(x - body.x) < profile.movementThreshold &&
      math.abs(y - body.y) < profile.movementThreshold &&
      math.abs(z - body.z) < profile.movementThreshold

    if hasPos && hasRot && acceptTeleport(x, y, z, yaw, pitch) then
      setPosition(x, y, z, resetMotion = true)
      setRotation(yaw, pitch)
      rotation.forced = true
      conditions.teleportUntil = tick + config.teleportTicks
    else if hasRot && !hasPos && acceptServerRotation(yaw, pitch) then
      setRotation(yaw, pitch)
      rotation.forced = true
    else
      if hasPos then setPosition(x, y, z, resetMotion = false)
      if hasRot then rotate(yaw, pitch)

    body.onGround = onGround
    if !duplicate then flyingThisTick = true
    if !profile.tickEnd && !duplicate then endTick()

  /** `CLIENT_TICK_END` (1.21.2+). */
  def onTickEnd(): Unit = if profile.tickEnd then endTick()

  def onInput(forward: Boolean, backward: Boolean, left: Boolean, right: Boolean, jump: Boolean, shift: Boolean, sprint: Boolean): Unit =
    if profile.realInput then
      keys.forward = forward; keys.backward = backward
      keys.left = left; keys.right = right
      keys.jump = jump; keys.shift = shift; keys.sprint = sprint
      body.sneaking = shift

  def onSwing(): Unit = clicks.swing = true
  def onUse(): Unit = clicks.use = true

  def onPlace(): Unit =
    clicks.use = true
    clicks.lastPlaceTick = tick

  def onDig(): Unit = clicks.lastDigTick = tick

  def onAttack(entityId: Int): Unit =
    if entityId == selfId then return
    clicks.attack = true
    clicks.prevAttackTarget = if clicks.lastAttackTick > Int.MinValue / 2 then clicks.attackTarget else -1
    clicks.attackTarget = entityId
    clicks.lastAttackTick = tick
    clicks.strengthAtAttack = clicks.strength(clicks.strengthTicks)
    clicks.sprintAtAttack = body.sprinting
    clicks.strengthTicks = 0

  def onSlot(slot: Int): Unit =
    if slot >= 0 && slot < 9 && slot != inventory.slot then
      inventory.slot = slot
      clicks.strengthTicks = 0

  def onSprint(sprinting: Boolean): Unit = body.sprinting = sprinting
  def onSneak(sneaking: Boolean): Unit = if !profile.realInput then body.sneaking = sneaking
  def onGui(open: Boolean): Unit = conditions.guiOpen = open

  // ---- server → client facts, applied once the client acknowledged them -------------------

  def expectTeleport(t: PendingTeleport): Unit =
    if pendingTeleports.size >= MaxPending then pendingTeleports.pollFirst()
    pendingTeleports.addLast(t)

  def expectRotation(r: PendingRotation): Unit =
    if pendingRotations.size >= MaxPending then pendingRotations.pollFirst()
    pendingRotations.addLast(r)

  def onKnockback(): Unit = conditions.knockbackUntil = tick + config.knockbackTicks

  def onSelfFlags(gliding: Boolean, swimming: Boolean): Unit =
    body.gliding = gliding
    body.swimming = swimming

  def onSelfLivingFlags(riptide: Boolean): Unit = body.riptide = riptide
  def onVehicle(inVehicle: Boolean): Unit = body.inVehicle = inVehicle
  def onAttackSpeed(value: Double): Unit = clicks.attackSpeed = math.max(0.1, value)
  def onInteractionRange(value: Double): Unit = body.interactionRange = value
  def onSelfScale(value: Float): Unit = body.scale = value

  def addEntity(e: TrackedEntity): Unit = entities(e.id) = e
  def entity(id: Int): TrackedEntity | Null = entities.getOrNull(id)
  def removeEntity(id: Int): Unit =
    val e = entities.remove(id)
    if e != null && combat.target == e then combat.target = null

  /**
   * The player's timeline broke (join, respawn, world change): flush what was being built and
   * start a new epoch, so no window spans the break.
   */
  def breakEpoch(): Unit =
    WindowSystem.flushCalm(this)
    attackCut.active = false
    engageCut.active = false
    entities.clear()
    combat.target = null
    pendingTeleports.clear()
    pendingRotations.clear()
    body.hasPosition = false
    epoch += 1

  // ---- tick -------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  def endTick(): Unit =
    Schedule.run(this)
    flyingThisTick = false
    tick += 1

  private[sim] inline def emitWindow(w: Window): Unit = emit(w)

  // ---- internals --------------------------------------------------------------------------

  private def setPosition(x: Double, y: Double, z: Double, resetMotion: Boolean): Unit =
    if resetMotion || !body.hasPosition then
      body.lastX = x; body.lastY = y; body.lastZ = z
    body.x = x; body.y = y; body.z = z
    body.hasPosition = true

  private def setRotation(yaw: Float, pitch: Float): Unit =
    rotation.lastYaw = yaw; rotation.lastPitch = pitch
    rotation.yaw = yaw; rotation.pitch = pitch

  private def rotate(yaw: Float, pitch: Float): Unit =
    val dy = yaw.toDouble - rotation.yaw
    val dp = pitch.toDouble - rotation.pitch
    if math.abs(dy) > 180 || math.abs(dp) > 180 then rotation.discontinuity = true
    else
      rotation.tickYaw += dy
      rotation.tickPitch += dp
    rotation.rotated = rotation.rotated || dy != 0 || dp != 0
    rotation.lastYaw = rotation.yaw; rotation.lastPitch = rotation.pitch
    rotation.yaw = yaw; rotation.pitch = pitch

  private def acceptTeleport(x: Double, y: Double, z: Double, yaw: Float, pitch: Float): Boolean =
    val it = pendingTeleports.iterator()
    var matched = -1
    var i = 0
    while matched < 0 && it.hasNext do
      if it.next().matches(x, y, z, yaw, pitch, body, rotation, profile.movementThreshold) then matched = i
      i += 1
    if matched >= 0 then for _ <- 0 to matched do pendingTeleports.pollFirst()
    matched >= 0

  private def acceptServerRotation(yaw: Float, pitch: Float): Boolean =
    val head = pendingRotations.peekFirst()
    if head != null && head.matches(yaw, pitch) then
      pendingRotations.pollFirst()
      true
    else false

  /** Whether entities of this kind count as melee targets under the current configuration. */
  private[sim] def targetable(kind: EntityKind): Boolean =
    kind == EntityKind.Player || (config.targetLiving && kind == EntityKind.Living)

object PlayerSim:
  private inline val MaxPending = 8

/** A server teleport the client has acknowledged and should answer with a matching movement. */
final class PendingTeleport(
    x: Double, y: Double, z: Double, yaw: Float, pitch: Float,
    relX: Boolean, relY: Boolean, relZ: Boolean, relYaw: Boolean, relPitch: Boolean,
):
  def matches(ax: Double, ay: Double, az: Double, ayaw: Float, apitch: Float, body: Body, rot: Rotation, threshold: Double): Boolean =
    val ex = x + (if relX then body.x else 0.0)
    val ey = y + (if relY then body.y else 0.0)
    val ez = z + (if relZ then body.z else 0.0)
    val tol = (if relX || relY || relZ then threshold else 0.0) + 1e-6
    val eyaw = yaw + (if relYaw then rot.yaw else 0f)
    val epitch = pitch + (if relPitch then rot.pitch else 0f)
    math.abs(ax - ex) <= tol && math.abs(ay - ey) <= tol + 1e-7 && math.abs(az - ez) <= tol &&
      math.abs(ayaw - eyaw) <= 1e-3f && math.abs(apitch - math.max(-90f, math.min(90f, epitch))) <= 1e-3f

/** A rotation the server set (1.21.2+ `PLAYER_ROTATION`); relative axes accept any value. */
final class PendingRotation(yaw: Float, pitch: Float, relYaw: Boolean, relPitch: Boolean):
  def matches(ayaw: Float, apitch: Float): Boolean =
    (relYaw || ayaw == yaw) && (relPitch || apitch == pitch)

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/sim/Reason.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.sim

/**
 * Why a tick's streams are masked; the bit set goes to the `reasons` meta column. Bits are part
 * of the dataset format: never renumber, only append.
 */
object Reason:
  inline val Teleport = 1 << 0
  inline val ServerRotation = 1 << 1
  inline val Uncalibrated = 1 << 2
  inline val Gui = 1 << 3
  inline val Vehicle = 1 << 4
  inline val Gliding = 1 << 5
  inline val Riptide = 1 << 6
  inline val Knockback = 1 << 7
  inline val Idle = 1 << 8
  inline val Uncertain = 1 << 9
  inline val NoTarget = 1 << 10
  inline val Water = 1 << 11
  /** Movement keys were inferred from motion (client older than 1.21.2), not reported. */
  inline val LegacyInput = 1 << 12
  inline val Discontinuity = 1 << 13
  /** 1.21.2+ only: the tick was closed by `CLIENT_TICK_END` with no movement packet, so a
    * client without tick-end packets would not have produced this row. An observation marker,
    * deliberately outside the validity masks: training can filter on it to compare client
    * versions on equal terms. */
  inline val NoMoveTick = 1 << 14
  /** An attack input happened this tick, whether or not a target was matched; the `attack`
    * feature column stays target-gated, this bit marks the raw input. Last free bit of the
    * U16: the next reason needs a wider column. */
  inline val Attack = 1 << 15

  inline val MasksRot = Teleport | ServerRotation | Uncalibrated | Gui | Vehicle | Gliding | Riptide | Idle | Discontinuity
  inline val MasksInp = Teleport | Gui | Vehicle | Gliding | Riptide | Knockback | Idle | Water
  inline val MasksTgt = NoTarget | Uncertain | Teleport | ServerRotation | Vehicle | Gliding | Riptide | Discontinuity

/** Bits of the `activity` meta column. */
object ActivityBit:
  inline val Moving = 1 << 0
  inline val Mining = 1 << 1
  inline val Building = 1 << 2
  inline val Rotating = 1 << 3

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/sim/SimConfig.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.sim

/** Tuning of the per-player simulation; loaded from the plugin configuration. */
final case class SimConfig(
    /** Also treat non-player living entities as combat targets. */
    targetLiving: Boolean = false,
    /** Ticks after the last hit that still count as combat. Combat starts only with a hit. */
    combatTicks: Int = 60,
    /** Ticks around combat that are neither combat nor calm. */
    bufferTicks: Int = 60,
    /** Ticks of context before / after the hit; the attack window is 128 ticks. */
    attackPre: Int = 96,
    attackPost: Int = 32,
    /** Minimum ticks between two attack anchors. */
    attackMinGap: Int = 20,
    /** Ticks of context before / after the first tick of an engagement; 128 total. */
    engagePre: Int = 32,
    engagePost: Int = 96,
    calmMinTicks: Int = 100,
    calmMaxTicks: Int = 1200,
    /** Fraction of calm ticks that must carry real mouse movement for a segment to be kept. */
    calmMinActive: Float = 0.25f,
    /** Target geometry is masked when its position uncertainty exceeds this, in blocks. */
    maxInflation: Double = 2.0,
    idleTicks: Int = 20,
    knockbackTicks: Int = 10,
    teleportTicks: Int = 2,
):
  require(attackPre >= 0 && attackPost >= 1 && engagePre >= 0 && engagePost >= 1)
  require(calmMinTicks >= 1 && calmMaxTicks >= calmMinTicks)

  /** History must hold the delay line and the longest window. */
  def historyCapacity: Int =
    val need = math.max(bufferTicks, math.max(attackPre + attackPost, engagePre + engagePost)) + 8
    Integer.highestOneBit(need - 1) << 1

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/sim/Systems.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.sim

import eigen.core.entity.TrackedEntity
import eigen.core.history.{Window, WindowKind}
import eigen.core.geom.Box
import eigen.core.schema.Schema

/** One stage of the per-tick pipeline. Systems are stateless; all state lives in components. */
trait TickSystem:
  def run(s: PlayerSim): Unit

/**
 * The fixed order systems run in at the end of every client tick. Order matters: each system
 * reads what the previous ones derived, and [[RecordSystem]] snapshots the result.
 */
object Schedule:
  private val systems: Array[TickSystem] = Array(
    EntitySystem,
    RotationSystem,
    InputSystem,
    TargetSystem,
    GateSystem,
    RecordSystem,
    WindowSystem,
    CleanupSystem,
  )

  def run(s: PlayerSim): Unit =
    var i = 0
    while i < systems.length do
      systems(i).run(s)
      i += 1

/** Advances every tracked entity's interpolation by one client tick. */
object EntitySystem extends TickSystem:
  def run(s: PlayerSim): Unit = s.entities.foreachValue(_.tick())

/** Turns the tick's rotation into look vectors and sensitivity-normalised mouse counts. */
object RotationSystem extends TickSystem:
  def run(s: PlayerSim): Unit =
    val r = s.rotation
    val doubleIndex = s.profile.doubleIndexTrig
    r.look.fromRotation(r.yaw, r.pitch, doubleIndex)
    r.lastYawLook.fromRotation(r.lastYaw, r.pitch, doubleIndex)
    r.lastLook.fromRotation(r.lastYaw, r.lastPitch, doubleIndex)

    r.dotsYaw = 0; r.dotsPitch = 0; r.residYaw = 0; r.residPitch = 0
    if r.forced || r.discontinuity then return
    if r.tickYaw != 0 then
      val ulp = math.ulp(math.max(math.abs(r.yaw), math.abs(r.lastYaw))).toDouble
      r.yawAxis.observe(r.tickYaw, ulp)
    if r.tickPitch != 0 then r.pitchAxis.observe(r.tickPitch, math.ulp(90f).toDouble)
    // An axis that did not move contributes exactly zero counts, calibrated or not.
    if r.yawAxis.converged then
      val dy = r.yawAxis.dots(r.tickYaw)
      r.dotsYaw = dy.toFloat
      r.residYaw = (dy - math.rint(dy)).toFloat
    if r.pitchAxis.converged then
      val dp = r.pitchAxis.dots(r.tickPitch)
      r.dotsPitch = dp.toFloat
      r.residPitch = (dp - math.rint(dp)).toFloat

  /** True when this tick's mouse counts are trustworthy on both axes. */
  def calibrated(r: Rotation): Boolean =
    (r.tickYaw == 0 || r.yawAxis.converged) && (r.tickPitch == 0 || r.pitchAxis.converged) &&
      (r.yawAxis.converged || r.pitchAxis.converged)

/**
 * Body motion bookkeeping and, for clients older than 1.21.2, movement keys inferred from the
 * horizontal motion relative to the camera.
 */
object InputSystem extends TickSystem:
  private inline val KeyThreshold = 0.02
  private inline val JumpRise = 0.1

  def run(s: PlayerSim): Unit =
    val b = s.body
    val dy = b.y - b.lastY
    if b.onGround then b.fallDistance = 0
    else if dy < 0 then b.fallDistance -= dy

    if !s.profile.realInput then
      val k = s.keys
      val mx = b.x - b.lastX
      val mz = b.z - b.lastZ
      val yawRad = s.rotation.yaw * eigen.core.geom.Angles.DegToRad
      val sin = math.sin(yawRad)
      val cos = math.cos(yawRad)
      // Forward is (-sin, cos); right is (-cos, -sin) in Minecraft's x-east, z-south frame.
      val ahead = -sin * mx + cos * mz
      val side = -cos * mx - sin * mz
      k.forward = ahead > KeyThreshold; k.backward = ahead < -KeyThreshold
      k.right = side > KeyThreshold; k.left = side < -KeyThreshold
      k.jump = b.wasOnGround && !b.onGround && dy > JumpRise
      k.sprint = b.sprinting
      k.shift = b.sneaking

/** Picks the melee target and derives its geometry in the player's view frame. */
object TargetSystem extends TickSystem:
  private inline val StandingEye = 1.62
  private inline val LowEye = 0.4

  def run(s: PlayerSim): Unit =
    val c = s.combat
    val b = s.body
    val r = s.rotation
    val cfg = s.config
    val eyeHeight = (if b.swimming || b.gliding then LowEye else if b.sneaking then s.profile.sneakEyeHeight else StandingEye) * b.scale
    val ex = b.x
    val ey = b.y + eyeHeight
    val ez = b.z
    val look = r.look
    val union = s.boxA

    // Combat is defined by hits: the attacked entity is the target while the last hit is recent.
    // Nothing before the first hit of a fight is combat, so pre-hit aim is not modelled.
    val attacked = s.entity(s.clicks.attackTarget)
    val recent = s.tick - s.clicks.lastAttackTick <= cfg.combatTicks
    val target: TrackedEntity | Null =
      if recent && attacked != null && !attacked.dead && s.targetable(attacked.kind) then attacked else null
    c.target = target
    c.gate = target != null

    if target == null then
      clear(c)
      return

    // Best estimate of what the client renders: the centre of the possible area, true size.
    target.possibleHitbox(union)
    val tight = target.possibleLocations(s.boxB)
    val fx = tight.centerX
    val fy = tight.minY + (tight.maxY - tight.minY) * 0.5
    val fz = tight.centerZ
    tight.set(fx, fy, fz, fx, fy, fz).growByDimensions(target.width, target.height)
    c.inflation = ((union.sizeX - tight.sizeX) + (union.sizeY - tight.sizeY) + (union.sizeZ - tight.sizeZ)).toFloat

    Geometry.aimError(ex, ey, ez, r.yaw, r.pitch, tight, s.pair)
    c.aimYaw = s.pair(0).toFloat
    c.aimPitch = s.pair(1).toFloat
    c.aimAngle = Geometry.minAngleToBox(ex, ey, ez, look, union).toFloat
    c.distance = union.distanceTo(ex, ey, ez).toFloat

    // View frame: forward = look, right = horizontal right of yaw, up = right × forward.
    val dx = tight.centerX - ex
    val dy = tight.centerY - ey
    val dz = tight.centerZ - ez
    val yawRad = r.yaw * eigen.core.geom.Angles.DegToRad
    val rx = -math.cos(yawRad)
    val rz = -math.sin(yawRad)
    val ux = -rz * look.y
    val uy = rz * look.x - rx * look.z
    val uz = rx * look.y
    c.relFwd = (dx * look.x + dy * look.y + dz * look.z).toFloat
    c.relSide = (dx * rx + dz * rz).toFloat
    c.relUp = (dx * ux + dy * uy + dz * uz).toFloat

    if s.clicks.attack && s.clicks.attackTarget == target.id then hitTest(s, ex, ey, ez, tight)
    else
      c.hit = false; c.hitX = 0; c.hitY = 0; c.hitZ = 0; c.miss = 0; c.crit = false

  /** Ray test of the attack against the target, trying every look the client may have used. */
  private def hitTest(s: PlayerSim, ex: Double, ey: Double, ez: Double, box: Box): Unit =
    val c = s.combat
    val r = s.rotation
    val reach = s.body.interactionRange
    val point = s.pair
    var dist = Geometry.rayHit(ex, ey, ez, r.look, reach, box, point)
    if dist < 0 && s.profile.lookCandidates >= 2 then dist = Geometry.rayHit(ex, ey, ez, r.lastYawLook, reach, box, point)
    if dist < 0 && s.profile.lookCandidates >= 3 then dist = Geometry.rayHit(ex, ey, ez, r.lastLook, reach, box, point)
    c.hit = dist >= 0
    if c.hit then
      c.hitX = ((point(0) - box.centerX) / (box.sizeX * 0.5)).toFloat
      c.hitY = ((point(1) - box.centerY) / (box.sizeY * 0.5)).toFloat
      c.hitZ = ((point(2) - box.centerZ) / (box.sizeZ * 0.5)).toFloat
      c.miss = 0
    else
      c.hitX = 0; c.hitY = 0; c.hitZ = 0
      c.miss = Geometry.missDistance(ex, ey, ez, r.look, reach, box).toFloat
    val b = s.body
    c.crit = s.clicks.strengthAtAttack > 0.9f && b.fallDistance > 0 && !b.onGround &&
      !s.clicks.sprintAtAttack && !b.inVehicle && !b.gliding && !b.swimming

  private def clear(c: Combat): Unit =
    c.aimYaw = 0; c.aimPitch = 0; c.aimAngle = 0; c.distance = 0
    c.relFwd = 0; c.relSide = 0; c.relUp = 0
    c.hit = false; c.hitX = 0; c.hitY = 0; c.hitZ = 0; c.miss = 0; c.crit = false
    c.inflation = 0

/** Tracks engagements: entering combat after at least a buffer's worth of calm starts one. */
object GateSystem extends TickSystem:
  def run(s: PlayerSim): Unit =
    val c = s.combat
    c.engagedThisTick = false
    if c.gate then
      if s.tick - c.lastCombatTick > s.config.bufferTicks then
        c.engagement += 1
        c.engagedThisTick = true
      c.lastCombatTick = s.tick

/** Derives masks and meta, then writes the tick's row into the history. */
object RecordSystem extends TickSystem:
  import Schema.*

  def run(s: PlayerSim): Unit =
    val h = s.history
    val r = s.rotation
    val k = s.keys
    val b = s.body
    val cl = s.clicks
    val c = s.combat
    val cond = s.conditions

    // Idle: no mouse, keys or clicks for a while.
    if r.rotated || k.any || cl.swing || cl.use || cl.attack then cond.idleRun = 0 else cond.idleRun += 1

    val reasons = reasonsOf(s)
    val activity = activityOf(s)

    val attackOnTarget = cl.attack && c.target != null && cl.attackTarget == c.target.nn.id

    h(DotsYaw) = r.dotsYaw
    h(DotsPitch) = r.dotsPitch
    h(ResidYaw) = r.residYaw
    h(ResidPitch) = r.residPitch

    h(Strafe) = k.strafe.toFloat
    h(Forward) = k.ahead.toFloat
    h(Jump) = k.jump
    h(Sprint) = b.sprinting
    h(Sneak) = b.sneaking
    h(Swing) = cl.swing
    h(Use) = cl.use

    h(AimYaw) = c.aimYaw
    h(AimPitch) = c.aimPitch
    h(AimAngle) = c.aimAngle
    h(Distance) = c.distance
    h(RelFwd) = c.relFwd
    h(RelSide) = c.relSide
    h(RelUp) = c.relUp
    h(Attack) = attackOnTarget
    h(Hit) = attackOnTarget && c.hit
    h(HitX) = c.hitX
    h(HitY) = c.hitY
    h(HitZ) = c.hitZ
    h(Miss) = c.miss
    h(Cooldown) = if cl.attack then cl.strengthAtAttack else cl.strength(cl.strengthTicks)
    h(Crit) = attackOnTarget && c.crit
    h(SprintHit) = attackOnTarget && cl.sprintAtAttack
    h(SameTarget) = attackOnTarget && cl.prevAttackTarget == cl.attackTarget

    h(MaskRot) = (reasons & Reason.MasksRot) == 0
    h(MaskInp) = (reasons & Reason.MasksInp) == 0
    h(MaskTgt) = (reasons & Reason.MasksTgt) == 0

    h(Reasons) = reasons.toFloat
    h(Gate) = c.gate
    h(Activity) = activity.toFloat
    h(Inflation) = c.inflation
    h(TargetKind) = (if c.target == null then 0 else c.target.nn.kind.code).toFloat
    h(ItemKind) = s.inventory.held.toFloat
    h(PingBucket) = s.pingBucket.toFloat

    h.commit(s.tick, s.epoch)

  /** Why this tick's streams are masked, as a set of `Reason` bits. */
  private def reasonsOf(s: PlayerSim): Int =
    val r = s.rotation
    val b = s.body
    val cond = s.conditions
    val c = s.combat
    var reasons = 0
    if s.tick <= cond.teleportUntil then reasons |= Reason.Teleport
    if r.forced then reasons |= Reason.ServerRotation
    if !RotationSystem.calibrated(r) then reasons |= Reason.Uncalibrated
    if cond.guiOpen then reasons |= Reason.Gui
    if b.inVehicle then reasons |= Reason.Vehicle
    if b.gliding then reasons |= Reason.Gliding
    if b.riptide then reasons |= Reason.Riptide
    if s.tick <= cond.knockbackUntil then reasons |= Reason.Knockback
    if cond.idleRun >= s.config.idleTicks then reasons |= Reason.Idle
    if c.target == null then reasons |= Reason.NoTarget
    else if c.inflation > s.config.maxInflation then reasons |= Reason.Uncertain
    if b.swimming || b.inWater then reasons |= Reason.Water
    if !s.profile.realInput then reasons |= Reason.LegacyInput
    if r.discontinuity then reasons |= Reason.Discontinuity
    if !s.flyingThisTick then reasons |= Reason.NoMoveTick
    if s.clicks.attack then reasons |= Reason.Attack
    reasons

  /** What the player was doing this tick, as a set of `ActivityBit` bits. */
  private def activityOf(s: PlayerSim): Int =
    val cl = s.clicks
    var activity = 0
    if s.keys.any then activity |= ActivityBit.Moving
    if s.tick - cl.lastDigTick <= RecentTicks then activity |= ActivityBit.Mining
    if s.tick - cl.lastPlaceTick <= RecentTicks then activity |= ActivityBit.Building
    if s.rotation.rotated then activity |= ActivityBit.Rotating
    activity

  private inline val RecentTicks = 10

/** A window waiting for its post-anchor rows. */
final class PendingCut:
  var active = false
  var anchorSeq = 0L
  var anchorTick = 0
  var engagement = 0

/** Cuts attack, engage and calm windows out of the history as rows become final. */
object WindowSystem extends TickSystem:
  def run(s: PlayerSim): Unit =
    val h = s.history
    val seq = h.nextSeq - 1 // the row just recorded
    val cfg = s.config
    val c = s.combat

    if s.windowsWanted then
      val attacked = h(Schema.Attack, seq) != 0f
      if attacked && !s.attackCut.active && s.tick - s.attackCut.anchorTick >= cfg.attackMinGap then
        arm(s.attackCut, seq, s.tick, c.engagement)
      if c.engagedThisTick && !s.engageCut.active then arm(s.engageCut, seq, s.tick, c.engagement)
      close(s, s.attackCut, WindowKind.Attack, cfg.attackPre, cfg.attackPost)
      close(s, s.engageCut, WindowKind.Engage, cfg.engagePre, cfg.engagePost)

    // Calm classification runs `bufferTicks` behind, when combat on either side is known.
    val delayed = seq - cfg.bufferTicks
    if delayed >= 0 && h.holds(delayed) then
      val tickThen = h.tickAt(delayed)
      val calmRow = h(Schema.Gate, delayed) == 0f && tickThen - c.lastCombatTick > cfg.bufferTicks
      if !s.windowsWanted then s.calm.flush()
      else if calmRow then
        val done = s.calm.append(h, delayed, c.engagement)
        if done != null then s.emitWindow(done)
      else flushCalm(s)

  def flushCalm(s: PlayerSim): Unit =
    val done = s.calm.flush()
    if done != null && s.windowsWanted then s.emitWindow(done)

  private def arm(cut: PendingCut, seq: Long, tick: Int, engagement: Int): Unit =
    cut.active = true
    cut.anchorSeq = seq
    cut.anchorTick = tick
    cut.engagement = engagement

  private def close(s: PlayerSim, cut: PendingCut, kind: WindowKind, pre: Int, post: Int): Unit =
    if !cut.active || s.history.nextSeq < cut.anchorSeq + post then return
    cut.active = false
    val h = s.history
    val epoch = h.epochAt(cut.anchorSeq)
    var from = math.max(h.oldestSeq, cut.anchorSeq - pre)
    while from < cut.anchorSeq && h.epochAt(from) != epoch do from += 1
    val rows = (cut.anchorSeq + post - from).toInt
    if h.contiguous(from, rows) then
      s.emitWindow(Window.cut(h, kind, Schema.Combat, from, rows, (cut.anchorSeq - from).toInt, cut.engagement))

/** Clears per-tick event flags and advances tick-based timers. */
object CleanupSystem extends TickSystem:
  def run(s: PlayerSim): Unit =
    val r = s.rotation
    r.tickYaw = 0; r.tickPitch = 0
    r.rotated = false; r.forced = false; r.discontinuity = false
    val cl = s.clicks
    // A swing that hit nothing resets the attack strength like a hit does.
    if cl.swing && !cl.attack then cl.strengthTicks = 0 else cl.strengthTicks += 1
    cl.swing = false; cl.use = false; cl.attack = false
    val b = s.body
    b.lastX = b.x; b.lastY = b.y; b.lastZ = b.z
    b.wasOnGround = b.onGround

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/util/IntMap.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.util

import scala.reflect.ClassTag

/**
 * Open-addressing hash map from `Int` keys to references, with linear probing and
 * backward-shift deletion. No boxing of keys and no allocation on lookup; iteration walks
 * the backing arrays. Not thread-safe: owned by a single player's network thread.
 */
final class IntMap[A <: AnyRef: ClassTag](initialCapacity: Int = 16):
  private var keys = new Array[Int](IntMap.capacityFor(initialCapacity))
  private var vals = new Array[A](keys.length)
  private var used = 0

  def size: Int = used
  def isEmpty: Boolean = used == 0

  /** Returns the value for `key`, or `null` when absent. */
  def getOrNull(key: Int): A =
    val mask = keys.length - 1
    var i = IntMap.mix(key) & mask
    while vals(i) != null do
      if keys(i) == key then return vals(i)
      i = (i + 1) & mask
    null.asInstanceOf[A]

  def contains(key: Int): Boolean = getOrNull(key) != null

  def update(key: Int, value: A): Unit =
    require(value != null, "IntMap does not store nulls")
    if (used + 1) * 4 > keys.length * 3 then grow()
    val mask = keys.length - 1
    var i = IntMap.mix(key) & mask
    while vals(i) != null && keys(i) != key do i = (i + 1) & mask
    if vals(i) == null then used += 1
    keys(i) = key
    vals(i) = value

  def remove(key: Int): A =
    val mask = keys.length - 1
    var i = IntMap.mix(key) & mask
    while vals(i) != null && keys(i) != key do i = (i + 1) & mask
    val removed = vals(i)
    if removed == null then return removed
    used -= 1
    // Backward-shift deletion keeps probe chains intact without tombstones.
    var gap = i
    var j = (i + 1) & mask
    while vals(j) != null do
      val home = IntMap.mix(keys(j)) & mask
      val movable = if gap <= j then home <= gap || home > j else home <= gap && home > j
      if movable then
        keys(gap) = keys(j); vals(gap) = vals(j); gap = j
      j = (j + 1) & mask
    vals(gap) = null.asInstanceOf[A]
    removed

  def clear(): Unit =
    java.util.Arrays.fill(vals.asInstanceOf[Array[AnyRef]], null)
    used = 0

  inline def foreachValue(inline f: A => Unit): Unit =
    val vs = values
    var i = 0
    while i < vs.length do
      val v = vs(i)
      if v != null then f(v)
      i += 1

  /** Backing value array, for allocation-free iteration (`null` slots are empty). */
  def values: Array[A] = vals

  private def grow(): Unit =
    val oldKeys = keys
    val oldVals = vals
    keys = new Array[Int](oldKeys.length * 2)
    vals = new Array[A](keys.length)
    used = 0
    var i = 0
    while i < oldKeys.length do
      if oldVals(i) != null then update(oldKeys(i), oldVals(i))
      i += 1

object IntMap:
  private def capacityFor(n: Int): Int = Integer.highestOneBit(math.max(4, n * 4 / 3) * 2 - 1)

  /** Murmur3 finaliser: spreads sequential entity ids over the table. */
  private inline def mix(k: Int): Int =
    var h = k
    h ^= h >>> 16
    h *= 0x85ebca6b
    h ^= h >>> 13
    h *= 0xc2b2ae35
    h ^ (h >>> 16)

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/wire/ByteSink.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.wire

import java.lang.invoke.{MethodHandles, VarHandle}
import java.nio.ByteOrder

/** Growable little-endian byte buffer. Reused across frames by the single transport thread. */
final class ByteSink(initial: Int = 64 * 1024):
  import ByteSink.*

  private var buf = new Array[Byte](initial)
  private var pos = 0

  def size: Int = pos
  def array: Array[Byte] = buf
  def reset(): Unit = pos = 0
  def toArray: Array[Byte] = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(buf, pos)

  def u8(v: Int): Unit =
    ensure(1)
    buf(pos) = v.toByte
    pos += 1

  def u16(v: Int): Unit =
    ensure(2)
    Short16.set(buf, pos, v.toShort)
    pos += 2

  def u32(v: Long): Unit =
    ensure(4)
    Int32.set(buf, pos, v.toInt)
    pos += 4

  def f32(v: Float): Unit =
    ensure(4)
    Float32.set(buf, pos, v)
    pos += 4

  def f64(v: Double): Unit =
    ensure(8)
    Double64.set(buf, pos, v)
    pos += 8

  def bytes(src: Array[Byte], off: Int, len: Int): Unit =
    ensure(len)
    System.arraycopy(src, off, buf, pos, len)
    pos += len

  /** Overwrites a u32 at an earlier offset (for back-patched counts). */
  def patchU16(at: Int, v: Int): Unit = Short16.set(buf, at, v.toShort)

  def ensure(n: Int): Unit =
    if pos + n > buf.length then buf = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(buf, math.max(buf.length * 2, pos + n))

object ByteSink:
  private val Short16: VarHandle = MethodHandles.byteArrayViewVarHandle(classOf[Array[Short]], ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
  private val Int32: VarHandle = MethodHandles.byteArrayViewVarHandle(classOf[Array[Int]], ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
  private val Float32: VarHandle = MethodHandles.byteArrayViewVarHandle(classOf[Array[Float]], ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
  private val Double64: VarHandle = MethodHandles.byteArrayViewVarHandle(classOf[Array[Double]], ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)

  def readU16(a: Array[Byte], at: Int): Int = (Short16.get(a, at): Short) & 0xffff
  def readI16(a: Array[Byte], at: Int): Int = Short16.get(a, at): Short
  def readU32(a: Array[Byte], at: Int): Long = (Int32.get(a, at): Int) & 0xffffffffL
  def readI32(a: Array[Byte], at: Int): Int = Int32.get(a, at): Int
  def readF32(a: Array[Byte], at: Int): Float = Float32.get(a, at): Float
  def readF64(a: Array[Byte], at: Int): Double = Double64.get(a, at): Double

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/wire/Frames.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.wire

import java.util.zip.{DataFormatException, Deflater, Inflater}

import eigen.core.history.Window
import eigen.core.schema.ColType

/** Message types of protocol §3. */
object MessageType:
  inline val Hello = 0x01
  inline val PlayerJoin = 0x02
  inline val PlayerLeave = 0x03
  inline val CollectStart = 0x04
  inline val CollectStop = 0x05
  inline val Mark = 0x06
  inline val Windows = 0x10
  inline val Welcome = 0x81
  inline val Ack = 0x82
  inline val Verdict = 0x83
  inline val Error = 0x8f

/** A decoded server→client frame. `payload` is already decompressed. */
final case class InFrame(tpe: Int, seq: Long, payload: Array[Byte])

/**
 * Frame and payload codecs of protocol v1. One instance per transport thread: it owns reusable
 * buffers and a DEFLATE context, so encoding allocates only the finished frame.
 */
final class FrameCodec(compressAbove: Int = 1024):
  private val payload = ByteSink()
  private val frame = ByteSink()
  private val deflater = Deflater(Deflater.DEFAULT_COMPRESSION, true)
  private val chunk = new Array[Byte](64 * 1024)

  /** Encodes one frame; payloads above `compressAbove` bytes are raw-DEFLATE compressed. */
  def encode(tpe: Int, seq: Long, body: Array[Byte], len: Int): Array[Byte] =
    frame.reset()
    val compress = len > compressAbove
    frame.u8(tpe)
    frame.u8(if compress then 1 else 0)
    frame.u16(0)
    frame.u32(seq)
    if compress then
      deflater.reset()
      deflater.setInput(body, 0, len)
      deflater.finish()
      while !deflater.finished() do
        val n = deflater.deflate(chunk)
        frame.bytes(chunk, 0, n)
    else frame.bytes(body, 0, len)
    frame.toArray

  def encode(tpe: Int, seq: Long, body: Array[Byte]): Array[Byte] = encode(tpe, seq, body, body.length)

  /** Encodes a `WINDOWS` frame (protocol §5). */
  def encodeWindows(seq: Long, windows: collection.Seq[Window]): Array[Byte] =
    payload.reset()
    payload.u16(windows.length)
    windows.foreach(w => writeWindow(w, w.purpose, w.id))
    encode(MessageType.Windows, seq, payload.array, payload.size)

  /** One window exactly as it appears inside a `WINDOWS` payload, with the given purpose and id. */
  def windowRecord(w: Window, purpose: Int, id: Int): Array[Byte] =
    payload.reset()
    writeWindow(w, purpose, id)
    payload.toArray

  private def writeWindow(w: Window, purpose: Int, id: Int): Unit =
    val p = payload
    p.ensure(w.wireBytes)
    p.u32(w.handle.toLong & 0xffffffffL)
    p.u32(id.toLong & 0xffffffffL)
    p.u32(w.session.toLong & 0xffffffffL)
    p.u8(w.kind.code)
    p.u8(purpose)
    p.u8(w.layout.id)
    p.u8(0)
    p.u32(w.epoch.toLong & 0xffffffffL)
    p.u32(w.engagement.toLong & 0xffffffffL)
    p.u32(w.firstTick.toLong & 0xffffffffL)
    p.u16(w.rows)
    p.u16(w.anchor & 0xffff)
    var c = 0
    while c < w.data.length do
      val values = w.data(c)
      w.layout.cols(c).tpe match
        case ColType.F32 =>
          var i = 0
          while i < w.rows do { p.f32(values(i)); i += 1 }
        case ColType.U16 =>
          var i = 0
          while i < w.rows do { p.u16(values(i).toInt); i += 1 }
        case ColType.U8 | ColType.I8 =>
          var i = 0
          while i < w.rows do { p.u8(values(i).toInt); i += 1 }
      c += 1

object FrameCodec:
  inline val HeaderBytes = 8

  /** Parses a server frame, inflating a compressed payload up to `maxBytes`. */
  def decode(bytes: Array[Byte], maxBytes: Int): Either[String, InFrame] =
    if bytes.length < HeaderBytes then return Left("frame shorter than its header")
    val tpe = bytes(0) & 0xff
    val flags = bytes(1) & 0xff
    if (flags & ~1) != 0 || ByteSink.readU16(bytes, 2) != 0 then return Left("reserved bits set")
    val seq = ByteSink.readU32(bytes, 4)
    val body = java.util.Arrays.copyOfRange(bytes, HeaderBytes, bytes.length)
    if (flags & 1) == 0 then Right(InFrame(tpe, seq, body))
    else inflate(body, maxBytes).map(InFrame(tpe, seq, _))

  private def inflate(body: Array[Byte], maxBytes: Int): Either[String, Array[Byte]] =
    val inflater = Inflater(true)
    try
      inflater.setInput(body)
      val out = ByteSink(math.min(maxBytes, body.length * 4 + 64))
      val chunk = new Array[Byte](16 * 1024)
      while !inflater.finished() do
        val n = inflater.inflate(chunk)
        if n == 0 && (inflater.needsInput() || inflater.needsDictionary()) then return Left("truncated deflate stream")
        if out.size + n > maxBytes then return Left("frame exceeds max_frame_bytes")
        out.bytes(chunk, 0, n)
      Right(out.toArray)
    catch case e: DataFormatException => Left(s"bad deflate stream: ${e.getMessage}")
    finally inflater.end()

```

`core/src/main/scala/eigen/core/wire/Messages.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core.wire

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8

import com.github.plokhotnyuk.jsoniter_scala.core.*
import com.github.plokhotnyuk.jsoniter_scala.macros.*

import eigen.core.schema.Manifest

/** JSON control messages of protocol §3, with codecs generated at compile time by jsoniter. */
object Messages:

  final case class PlayerJoin(handle: Int, player: String, clientProtocol: Int, brand: String)
  final case class PlayerLeave(handle: Int)
  final case class CollectStart(
      session: Int,
      sessionUuid: String,
      handle: Int,
      label: String,
      tags: Map[String, String],
      note: String,
      startedAtMs: Long,
  )
  final case class CollectStop(session: Int, stoppedAtMs: Long)
  final case class Mark(session: Int, tick: Int, state: String, note: String)

  final case class Welcome(protocol: Int, resumeFrom: Long, maxFrameBytes: Int, serverTimeMs: Long, inference: Boolean)
  final case class Verdict(handle: Int, window: Long, model: String, scores: Map[String, Double])
  final case class ServerError(code: String, message: String, fatal: Boolean)

  private inline def codec[A]: JsonValueCodec[A] =
    JsonCodecMaker.make[A](CodecMakerConfig.withFieldNameMapper(JsonCodecMaker.enforce_snake_case).withTransientDefault(false).withTransientEmpty(false))

  given JsonValueCodec[PlayerJoin] = codec
  given JsonValueCodec[PlayerLeave] = codec
  given JsonValueCodec[CollectStart] = codec
  given JsonValueCodec[CollectStop] = codec
  given JsonValueCodec[Mark] = codec
  given JsonValueCodec[Welcome] = codec
  given JsonValueCodec[Verdict] = codec
  given JsonValueCodec[ServerError] = codec
  private given JsonValueCodec[String] = JsonCodecMaker.make[String]

  def encode[A: JsonValueCodec](a: A): Array[Byte] = writeToArray(a)

  def decode[A: JsonValueCodec](bytes: Array[Byte]): Either[String, A] =
    try Right(readFromArray[A](bytes))
    catch case e: JsonReaderException => Left(e.getMessage)

  /**
   * `HELLO` embeds the canonical manifest verbatim, so the server can hash exactly the bytes we
   * hashed. Built by hand because the manifest must not be re-serialised.
   */
  def hello(streamId: String, plugin: String, platform: String): Array[Byte] =
    val sb = StringBuilder(Manifest.json.length + 256)
    sb.append("{\"protocol\":1,\"stream_id\":").append(quote(streamId))
    sb.append(",\"plugin\":").append(quote(plugin))
    sb.append(",\"platform\":").append(quote(platform))
    sb.append(",\"schema\":").append(Manifest.json)
    sb.append(",\"schema_hash\":").append(quote(Manifest.hash)).append('}')
    sb.result().getBytes(UTF_8)

  private def quote(s: String): String = writeToString(s)

```

`core/src/test/scala/eigen/core/SimSuite.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core

import scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer

import eigen.core.entity.{ClientProfile, EntityKind, TrackedEntity}
import eigen.core.history.{Window, WindowKind}
import eigen.core.latency.TransactionClock
import eigen.core.schema.Schema
import eigen.core.sim.{Handoff, PlayerSim, Reason, RotationSystem, SimConfig, WindowSystem}

class SimSuite extends munit.FunSuite:

  private val step = { val f = 0.5 * 0.6 + 0.2; f * f * f * 8 * 0.15 }

  private final class Harness(cfg: SimConfig = SimConfig(calmMinTicks = 40, calmMaxTicks = 200)):
    val windows = ArrayBuffer[Window]()
    val sim = PlayerSim(ClientProfile.Modern, cfg, windows += _)
    sim.windowsWanted = true
    sim.selfId = 1
    private var yaw = 0f
    private var pitch = 0f
    private val rnd = scala.util.Random(11)

    /** One client tick with a random whole-count mouse movement. */
    def tick(attack: Int = -1, swing: Boolean = false): Unit =
      yaw += ((rnd.nextInt(9) - 4) * step).toFloat
      pitch = math.max(-30f, math.min(30f, pitch + ((rnd.nextInt(5) - 2) * step).toFloat))
      sim.onFlying(hasPos = true, 0, 64, 0, hasRot = true, yaw, pitch, onGround = true)
      if attack >= 0 then sim.onAttack(attack)
      if swing || attack >= 0 then sim.onSwing()
      sim.onTickEnd()

    def spawnTarget(id: Int, z: Double): TrackedEntity =
      val e = TrackedEntity(id, EntityKind.Player, TrackedEntity.LivingSteps, ClientProfile.Modern)
      e.spawnAt(0, 64, z)
      sim.addEntity(e)
      e

  test("calm play produces a calm segment linked to no engagement"):
    val h = Harness()
    for _ <- 0 until 400 do h.tick()
    val calm = h.windows.filter(_.kind == WindowKind.Calm)
    assert(calm.nonEmpty, "expected calm segments")
    calm.foreach: w =>
      assertEquals(w.layout, Schema.Calm)
      assertEquals(w.engagement, 0)
      assertEquals(w.anchor, -1)
      assert(w.rows <= 200)
    assert(h.windows.forall(_.kind == WindowKind.Calm))

  test("an attack cuts a fixed-length combat window anchored on the hit"):
    val cfg = SimConfig(calmMinTicks = 40, calmMaxTicks = 200)
    val h = Harness(cfg)
    for _ <- 0 until 150 do h.tick() // calibrate the mouse estimator
    h.spawnTarget(2, 2.5)
    for _ <- 0 until 30 do h.tick()
    h.tick(attack = 2)
    for _ <- 0 until cfg.engagePost do h.tick()

    val attack = h.windows.filter(_.kind == WindowKind.Attack)
    assertEquals(attack.size, 1)
    val w = attack.head
    assertEquals(w.rows, cfg.attackPre + cfg.attackPost)
    assertEquals(w.anchor, cfg.attackPre)
    assertEquals(w.layout, Schema.Combat)
    val attackCol = Schema.Combat.cols.indexOf(Schema.Attack)
    assertEquals(w.data(attackCol)(w.anchor), 1f)
    val maskTgt = Schema.Combat.cols.indexOf(Schema.MaskTgt)
    assertEquals(w.data(maskTgt)(w.anchor), 1f, "target geometry valid at the hit")
    val reasons = Schema.Combat.cols.indexOf(Schema.Reasons)
    assertEquals(w.data(reasons)(w.anchor).toInt & Reason.Uncalibrated, 0)

    val engage = h.windows.filter(_.kind == WindowKind.Engage)
    assertEquals(engage.size, 1)
    assertEquals(engage.head.engagement, 1)
    assertEquals(w.engagement, 1)

  test("calm after a fight carries the engagement that preceded it"):
    val h = Harness()
    for _ <- 0 until 150 do h.tick()
    val target = h.spawnTarget(2, 2.5)
    for _ <- 0 until 20 do h.tick(attack = 2)
    target.dead = true
    for _ <- 0 until 500 do h.tick()
    val before = h.windows.filter(w => w.kind == WindowKind.Calm && w.engagement == 0)
    val after = h.windows.filter(w => w.kind == WindowKind.Calm && w.engagement == 1)
    assert(before.nonEmpty && after.nonEmpty, s"calm before=${before.size} after=${after.size}")
    assert(after.forall(_.firstTick > before.map(_.firstTick).max))

  test("a broken epoch never ends up inside one window"):
    val h = Harness()
    for _ <- 0 until 120 do h.tick()
    h.sim.breakEpoch()
    for _ <- 0 until 300 do h.tick()
    val epochs = h.windows.map(_.epoch).toSet
    assert(epochs.contains(2))
    h.windows.foreach(w => assert(w.epoch == 1 || w.epoch == 2))

  test("TransactionClock runs tasks in order once their pong arrives and ignores foreign ids"):
    val clock = TransactionClock()
    val ran = ArrayBuffer[Int]()
    val a = clock.nextId(); clock.onPingSent(a)
    clock.at(clock.lastSent)(() => ran += 1)
    val b = clock.nextId(); clock.onPingSent(b)
    clock.at(clock.lastSent)(() => ran += 2)
    assert(!clock.onPong(-5), "GrimAC's ids are not ours")
    assert(!clock.onPong(0), "zero is foreign (GrimAC/other plugins), never ShardCompat's either")
    assert(!clock.onPong(-32768), "negative shorts are foreign")
    assert(!clock.onPong(70000), "ids wider than a short are not ours")
    assertEquals(clock.lastReceived, 0, "foreign pongs must not advance the shared watermark")
    assertEquals(ran.toList, List.empty[Int], "foreign pongs must not run shared-clock tasks")
    assert(clock.onPong(a))
    assertEquals(ran.toList, List(1))
    assert(clock.onPong(b))
    assertEquals(ran.toList, List(1, 2))

  test("combat needs a hit: a close target without attacks is never combat"):
    val h = Harness()
    for _ <- 0 until 150 do h.tick()
    h.spawnTarget(2, 2.5)
    for _ <- 0 until 100 do h.tick()
    val hist = h.sim.history
    assert((hist.oldestSeq until hist.nextSeq).forall(seq => hist(Schema.Gate, seq) == 0f))
    assert((hist.oldestSeq until hist.nextSeq).forall(seq => hist(Schema.MaskTgt, seq) == 0f))

  test("combat starts at a hit and lasts combatTicks after the last hit"):
    val cfg = SimConfig(calmMinTicks = 40, calmMaxTicks = 200)
    val h = Harness(cfg)
    for _ <- 0 until 150 do h.tick()
    h.spawnTarget(2, 2.5)
    h.tick(attack = 2)
    val hist = h.sim.history
    val hit = hist.nextSeq - 1
    assertEquals(hist(Schema.Gate, hit), 1f)
    for _ <- 0 until cfg.combatTicks do h.tick()
    assertEquals(hist(Schema.Gate, hit + cfg.combatTicks), 1f)
    h.tick()
    assertEquals(hist(Schema.Gate, hit + cfg.combatTicks + 1), 0f)

  test("Handoff preserves mouse calibration, engagement and in-progress calm track across servers"):
    val cfg = SimConfig(calmMinTicks = 40, calmMaxTicks = 200)
    val h1 = Harness(cfg)
    h1.sim.combat.engagement = 3
    for _ <- 0 until 285 do h1.tick()
    assert(RotationSystem.calibrated(h1.sim.rotation), "source sim should be calibrated")
    assertEquals(h1.sim.calm.length, 25)

    val snapshot = Handoff.encode(h1.sim)
    val h2 = Harness(cfg)
    assert(!RotationSystem.calibrated(h2.sim.rotation))
    assert(Handoff.restore(h2.sim, snapshot))
    assert(RotationSystem.calibrated(h2.sim.rotation), "restored sim is immediately calibrated")
    assertEquals(h2.sim.combat.engagement, 3)
    assertEquals(h2.sim.calm.length, 25)

    for _ <- 0 until (cfg.bufferTicks + 15) do h2.tick()
    assertEquals(h2.sim.calm.length, 40)
    WindowSystem.flushCalm(h2.sim)
    val calm = h2.windows.filter(_.kind == WindowKind.Calm)
    assertEquals(calm.size, 1)
    assertEquals(calm.head.rows, 40)
    assertEquals(calm.head.engagement, 3)

  test("a tick closed only by CLIENT_TICK_END carries the NoMoveTick observation marker"):
    // The marker never participates in any validity mask.
    assertEquals(
      (Reason.MasksRot | Reason.MasksInp | Reason.MasksTgt) & Reason.NoMoveTick,
      0,
    )
    val h = Harness()
    h.sim.onFlying(hasPos = true, 0, 64, 0, hasRot = true, 0.5f, 0f, onGround = true)
    h.sim.onTickEnd()
    h.sim.onTickEnd()
    val hist = h.sim.history
    assertEquals(hist(Schema.Reasons, hist.nextSeq - 2).toInt & Reason.NoMoveTick, 0)
    assertNotEquals(hist(Schema.Reasons, hist.nextSeq - 1).toInt & Reason.NoMoveTick, 0)

    // Without tick-end packets the row only exists because a movement packet closed the tick.
    val legacy = PlayerSim(ClientProfile.Modern.copy(tickEnd = false, realInput = false), SimConfig(), _ => ())
    legacy.onFlying(hasPos = true, 0, 64, 0, hasRot = true, 0.5f, 0f, onGround = true)
    legacy.onTickEnd()
    assertEquals(legacy.history(Schema.Reasons, legacy.history.nextSeq - 1).toInt & Reason.NoMoveTick, 0)

  test("rows of clients without PLAYER_INPUT carry the LegacyInput reason"):
    // Inferred inputs are an observation marker, not a validity mask: the values stay
    // usable and the model decides how to weigh them (the exact keys remain unmasked).
    assertEquals((Reason.MasksRot | Reason.MasksInp | Reason.MasksTgt) & Reason.LegacyInput, 0)

    val legacy = PlayerSim(ClientProfile.Modern.copy(tickEnd = false, realInput = false), SimConfig(), _ => ())
    legacy.onFlying(hasPos = true, 0, 64, 0, hasRot = true, 0.5f, 0f, onGround = true)
    assertNotEquals(legacy.history(Schema.Reasons, legacy.history.nextSeq - 1).toInt & Reason.LegacyInput, 0)

    val modern = PlayerSim(ClientProfile.Modern, SimConfig(), _ => ())
    modern.onFlying(hasPos = true, 0, 64, 0, hasRot = true, 0.5f, 0f, onGround = true)
    modern.onInput(true, false, false, false, false, false, false)
    modern.onTickEnd()
    assertEquals(modern.history(Schema.Reasons, modern.history.nextSeq - 1).toInt & Reason.LegacyInput, 0)

  test("the Attack reason marks attack input even without a matched target"):
    assertEquals((Reason.MasksRot | Reason.MasksInp | Reason.MasksTgt) & Reason.Attack, 0)
    val h = Harness()
    h.tick(attack = 7) // no entity 7 spawned: the attack column stays 0, the input bit is set
    val hist = h.sim.history
    assertEquals(hist(Schema.Attack, hist.nextSeq - 1), 0f)
    assertEquals(hist(Schema.Reasons, hist.nextSeq - 1).toInt & Reason.Attack, Reason.Attack)
    h.tick()
    assertEquals(hist(Schema.Reasons, hist.nextSeq - 1).toInt & Reason.Attack, 0)
    h.spawnTarget(7, 2.5)
    h.tick(attack = 7)
    assertEquals(hist(Schema.Attack, hist.nextSeq - 1), 1f)
    assertEquals(hist(Schema.Reasons, hist.nextSeq - 1).toInt & Reason.Attack, Reason.Attack)

```

`core/src/test/scala/eigen/core/UtilSuite.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core

import scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer

import eigen.core.aim.{AxisEstimator, RunningMode}
import eigen.core.geom.Angles
import eigen.core.latency.TransactionClock
import eigen.core.util.IntMap

class UtilSuite extends munit.FunSuite:

  test("wrapDegrees keeps angles in [-180, 180)"):
    assertEqualsDouble(Angles.wrapDegrees(190), -170, 1e-9)
    assertEqualsDouble(Angles.wrapDegrees(-190), 170, 1e-9)
    assertEqualsDouble(Angles.wrapDegrees(180), -180, 1e-9)
    assertEqualsDouble(Angles.turn(179, -179), 2, 1e-9)

  test("IntMap behaves like a map under random inserts and removals"):
    val map = IntMap[String]()
    val ref = scala.collection.mutable.HashMap[Int, String]()
    val rnd = scala.util.Random(7)
    for _ <- 0 until 20_000 do
      val k = rnd.nextInt(512) - 256
      if rnd.nextInt(3) == 0 then assertEquals(Option(map.remove(k)), ref.remove(k))
      else
        map(k) = k.toString
        ref(k) = k.toString
      assertEquals(map.size, ref.size)
    for k <- -256 until 256 do assertEquals(Option(map.getOrNull(k)), ref.get(k))
    var seen = 0
    map.foreachValue(_ => seen += 1)
    assertEquals(seen, ref.size)

  test("RunningMode tracks the most frequent bucket over a sliding window"):
    val mode = RunningMode(5)
    Seq(1.0, 1.0005, 2.0, 1.0, 3.0).foreach(mode.add(_, 1e-3))
    assertEqualsDouble(mode.mode, 1.0, 1e-9)
    assertEquals(mode.modeCount, 3)
    Seq(2.0, 2.0, 2.0).foreach(mode.add(_, 1e-3)) // evicts 1.0, 1.0005 and the first 2.0
    assertEqualsDouble(mode.mode, 2.0, 1e-9)
    assertEquals(mode.modeCount, 3)

  /** Rotation step of the client for a sensitivity in [0, 1]. */
  private def step(sensitivity: Double): Double =
    val f = sensitivity * 0.6 + 0.2
    f * f * f * 8 * 0.15

  test("AxisEstimator recovers the mouse step and whole-count dots"):
    val est = AxisEstimator()
    val s = step(0.5)
    val rnd = scala.util.Random(3)
    for _ <- 0 until 200 do
      val counts = (rnd.nextInt(30) + 1) * (if rnd.nextBoolean() then 1 else -1)
      est.observe(counts * s, math.ulp(100f).toDouble)
    assert(est.converged)
    assertEqualsDouble(est.currentStep, s, 1e-6)
    val dots = est.dots(7 * s)
    assertEqualsDouble(dots, 7.0, 1e-6)

  test("AxisEstimator starts over when the sensitivity changes"):
    val est = AxisEstimator()
    val rnd = scala.util.Random(5)
    def feed(s: Double, n: Int): Unit =
      for _ <- 0 until n do est.observe((rnd.nextInt(25) + 1) * s, 1e-6)
    feed(step(0.5), 200)
    assertEqualsDouble(est.currentStep, step(0.5), 1e-6)
    feed(step(0.83), 400)
    assert(est.converged)
    assertEqualsDouble(est.currentStep, step(0.83), 1e-6)

  test("TransactionClock runs all <= done tasks even when two listeners interleave [t, t+1, t, t+1]"):
    val clock = TransactionClock()
    val id1 = clock.nextId().toInt
    val id2 = clock.nextId().toInt
    clock.onPingSent(id1)
    clock.onPingSent(id2)
    assertEquals(clock.lastSent, 2)

    val order = ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
    // Listener A schedules first and second transaction tasks (e.g. Eigen on ENTITY_RELATIVE_MOVE)
    clock.at(1)(() => order += "eigen-t1")
    clock.at(2)(() => order += "eigen-t2")
    // Listener B schedules first and second transaction tasks (e.g. ShardCompat on ENTITY_RELATIVE_MOVE)
    clock.at(1)(() => order += "shard-t1")
    clock.at(2)(() => order += "shard-t2")

    assert(clock.onPong(id1))
    assertEquals(clock.lastReceived, 1)
    assertEquals(order.toList, List("eigen-t1", "shard-t1"))

    assert(clock.onPong(id2))
    assertEquals(clock.lastReceived, 2)
    assertEquals(order.toList, List("eigen-t1", "shard-t1", "eigen-t2", "shard-t2"))

  test("TransactionClock ignores foreign <= 0 ids (GrimAC) and unanswered ids"):
    val clock = TransactionClock()
    clock.onPingSent(0)
    clock.onPingSent(-1)
    clock.onPingSent(-32768)
    assertEquals(clock.lastSent, 0)
    assert(!clock.onPong(0))
    assert(!clock.onPong(-1))
    assert(!clock.onPong(42))

```

`core/src/test/scala/eigen/core/WireSuite.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.core

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
import java.nio.file.{Files, Path, Paths}
import java.util.HexFormat

import eigen.core.history.{Window, WindowKind}
import eigen.core.schema.{Manifest, Schema}
import eigen.core.wire.{ByteSink, FrameCodec, MessageType, Messages}

/**
 * Byte-level checks of the wire format, plus golden fixtures shared with the backend: the
 * backend's test suite decodes the hex files in `docs/fixtures` and must agree with what is asserted here.
 * Regenerate them with `EIGEN_WRITE_FIXTURES=1 sbt core/testFull` after a deliberate change.
 */
class WireSuite extends munit.FunSuite:

  private def window(kind: WindowKind, layoutCombat: Boolean, rows: Int, seed: Int): Window =
    val layout = if layoutCombat then Schema.Combat else Schema.Calm
    val data = Array.tabulate(layout.cols.length, rows): (c, r) =>
      layout.cols(c).tpe match
        case eigen.core.schema.ColType.F32 => (seed + c) * 0.25f + r
        case eigen.core.schema.ColType.I8 => ((c + r) % 3 - 1).toFloat
        case _ => ((seed + c + r) % 2).toFloat
    val w = Window(kind, layout, epoch = 2, engagement = if kind == WindowKind.Calm then 0 else 1, firstTick = 1000 + seed, rows, anchor = if kind == WindowKind.Calm then -1 else 1, data)
    w.handle = 7
    w.session = 3
    w.purpose = Window.PurposeCollect
    w.id = seed
    w

  private val sample = Seq(window(WindowKind.Attack, layoutCombat = true, rows = 3, seed = 1), window(WindowKind.Calm, layoutCombat = false, rows = 2, seed = 2))

  test("the manifest hash is the CRC-32 of the canonical JSON"):
    val crc = java.util.zip.CRC32()
    crc.update(Manifest.json.getBytes(UTF_8))
    assertEquals(Manifest.hash, f"${crc.getValue}%08x")
    assert(!Manifest.json.contains(' '), "canonical JSON has no whitespace")
    assert(Manifest.json.startsWith("{\"version\":1,\"layouts\":[{\"id\":1,\"name\":\"combat\""))

  test("a WINDOWS frame has the documented header and exact size"):
    val frame = FrameCodec(compressAbove = Int.MaxValue).encodeWindows(41, sample)
    assertEquals(frame(0) & 0xff, MessageType.Windows)
    assertEquals(frame(1).toInt, 0)
    assertEquals(ByteSink.readU32(frame, 4), 41L)
    assertEquals(ByteSink.readU16(frame, 8), 2)
    val expected = FrameCodec.HeaderBytes + 2 + sample.map(_.wireBytes).sum
    assertEquals(frame.length, expected)
    // First window header, right after the u16 count.
    val w = 10
    assertEquals(ByteSink.readU32(frame, w), 7L) // handle
    assertEquals(ByteSink.readU32(frame, w + 4), 1L) // window id
    assertEquals(ByteSink.readU32(frame, w + 8), 3L) // session
    assertEquals(frame(w + 12) & 0xff, WindowKind.Attack.code)
    assertEquals(frame(w + 13) & 0xff, Window.PurposeCollect)
    assertEquals(frame(w + 14) & 0xff, Schema.Combat.id)
    assertEquals(ByteSink.readU32(frame, w + 16), 2L) // epoch
    assertEquals(ByteSink.readU32(frame, w + 20), 1L) // engagement
    assertEquals(ByteSink.readU32(frame, w + 24), 1001L) // first tick
    assertEquals(ByteSink.readU16(frame, w + 28), 3) // rows
    assertEquals(ByteSink.readI16(frame, w + 30), 1) // anchor
    // First column (dots_yaw, f32) of the first window, row 0.
    assertEquals(ByteSink.readF32(frame, w + Window.HeaderBytes), 0.25f)

  test("compressed frames decode back to the same payload"):
    val plain = FrameCodec(compressAbove = Int.MaxValue).encodeWindows(5, sample)
    val packed = FrameCodec(compressAbove = 0).encodeWindows(5, sample)
    assertEquals(packed(1).toInt, 1)
    val decoded = FrameCodec.decode(packed, 1 << 20).toOption.get
    assertEquals(decoded.payload.toSeq, plain.drop(FrameCodec.HeaderBytes).toSeq)
    assert(FrameCodec.decode(packed, 16).isLeft, "size cap applies to the inflated payload")

  test("HELLO embeds the manifest verbatim"):
    val hello = String(Messages.hello("00112233aabbccdd", "eigen test", "Paper test"), UTF_8)
    assert(hello.contains("\"schema\":" + Manifest.json + ","))
    assert(hello.contains("\"schema_hash\":\"" + Manifest.hash + "\""))

  test("golden fixtures match the encoder"):
    val fixtures = Seq(
      "windows_v1.hex" -> FrameCodec(compressAbove = Int.MaxValue).encodeWindows(41, sample),
      "windows_v1_deflate.hex" -> FrameCodec(compressAbove = 0).encodeWindows(42, sample),
      "hello_v1.hex" -> FrameCodec().encode(MessageType.Hello, 0, Messages.hello("00112233aabbccdd", "eigen test", "Paper test")),
    )
    val dir = fixtureDir
    for (name, bytes) <- fixtures do
      val file = dir.resolve(name)
      val hex = HexFormat.of().formatHex(bytes)
      if sys.env.contains("EIGEN_WRITE_FIXTURES") then Files.writeString(file, hex + "\n")
      else assertEquals(Files.readString(file).trim, hex, s"$name is stale; regenerate it")

  private def fixtureDir: Path =
    var dir = Paths.get("").toAbsolutePath
    while dir != null && !Files.isDirectory(dir.resolve("docs")) do dir = dir.getParent
    val out = dir.resolve("docs/fixtures")
    Files.createDirectories(out)
    out

```
