`docs/protocol.md`:

```md
# Eigen stream protocol, version 1

This document is the contract between the Eigen server plugin (client) and the
Eigen backend (server). Both sides must implement exactly what is written here;
when the code and this document disagree, the document wins and the code is a bug.

## 1. Transport

- One WebSocket connection per Minecraft server process, over TLS (`wss://`).
  Plain `ws://` is accepted only for loopback development.
- Endpoint: `GET /v1/stream`.
- Authentication happens during the HTTP upgrade, before the socket is accepted:
  `Authorization: Bearer <token>`. A missing, malformed, unknown or revoked token
  is answered with HTTP `401` and the upgrade is refused.
- The request also carries `Eigen-Protocol: 1`. Any other value is answered with
  HTTP `426`.
- Every protocol message is one WebSocket **binary** message. Text messages are a
  protocol error. WebSocket ping/pong is used for liveness: the server pings every
  15 s and closes the connection after 45 s without a pong.

### 1.1 Tokens

```
eig_live_<30 base62 chars><6 base62 chars>
```

- The 30-character body is drawn from a CSPRNG (about 178 bits).
- The last 6 characters are CRC-32 (IEEE) of the body, written in base62
  (`0-9A-Za-z`), left-padded with `0`. The checksum only proves well-formedness;
  it is checked before any database lookup so that garbage never reaches storage.
- The server stores only `sha256(token)` (lowercase hex) and the last four
  characters for display. The token itself is shown once, when issued.
- Regex for secret scanners: `\beig_live_[0-9A-Za-z]{36}\b`.

## 2. Frame

All integers are little-endian. Every message starts with an 8-byte header:

| Offset | Size | Field | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | 1 | `type` | Message type, see §3 |
| 1 | 1 | `flags` | bit 0: payload is raw DEFLATE (RFC 1951) compressed; other bits must be 0 |
| 2 | 2 | `reserved` | Must be 0 |
| 4 | 4 | `seq` | Client sequence number (u32), see §2.1; 0 where not applicable |
| 8 | … | `payload` | Type-specific |

A frame larger than `max_frame_bytes` (announced in `WELCOME`, after decompression)
is a fatal protocol error.

### 2.1 Sequencing and acknowledgement

- `stream_id` is a random 64-bit value the client generates once per process start
  and sends in `HELLO`. It survives reconnects of the same process.
- Every client→server message except `HELLO` carries a `seq`. The first one is 1;
  each next one is exactly the previous plus 1, across reconnects of the stream.
- The server applies messages strictly in `seq` order. A message whose `seq` is
  not greater than the last applied one is a duplicate: it is acknowledged and
  ignored. A gap (`seq` greater than last applied + 1) is a fatal protocol error.
- `ACK` carries the highest `seq` that is durably applied (cumulative). The server
  persists this value per `stream_id`, so it survives server restarts.
- The client keeps every unacknowledged message in a bounded resend buffer. After a
  reconnect, `WELCOME.resume_from` tells it the last applied `seq`; it drops
  everything up to that and resends the rest in order before sending new messages.
- If the resend buffer overflows, the client drops the oldest **window** messages
  first and never drops control messages; it then starts a new `stream_id`
  (the server treats it as a new stream).

## 3. Message types

| Type | Name | Direction | Payload |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x01` | `HELLO` | C→S | JSON |
| `0x02` | `PLAYER_JOIN` | C→S | JSON |
| `0x03` | `PLAYER_LEAVE` | C→S | JSON |
| `0x04` | `COLLECT_START` | C→S | JSON |
| `0x05` | `COLLECT_STOP` | C→S | JSON |
| `0x06` | `MARK` | C→S | JSON |
| `0x10` | `WINDOWS` | C→S | binary, §5 |
| `0x81` | `WELCOME` | S→C | JSON |
| `0x82` | `ACK` | S→C | binary: `u32 seq` |
| `0x83` | `VERDICT` | S→C | JSON |
| `0x8F` | `ERROR` | S→C | JSON |

JSON payloads are UTF-8 objects. Unknown fields are ignored by the receiver.

### 3.1 `HELLO` (first message after the upgrade, `seq = 0`)

```json
{
  "protocol": 1,
  "stream_id": "9f86d081884c7d65",
  "plugin": "eigen 0.1.0",
  "platform": "Paper 26.2",
  "schema": { "...": "manifest, §4" },
  "schema_hash": "<crc32 of the schema bytes>"
}
```

`schema_hash` is the CRC-32 of the UTF-8 bytes of the canonical manifest JSON (§4),
lowercase hex, 8 digits.

### 3.2 `WELCOME`

```json
{
  "protocol": 1,
  "resume_from": 0,
  "max_frame_bytes": 4194304,
  "server_time_ms": 1792000000000,
  "inference": false
}
```

`inference: true` means the server runs a model and the client should mark windows
with the `infer` purpose (§5). The client sends nothing but `HELLO` until it has
received `WELCOME`.

### 3.3 Player and dataset messages

```json
// PLAYER_JOIN
{ "handle": 7, "player": "3b1f…(64 hex)", "client_protocol": 771, "brand": "fabric" }
// PLAYER_LEAVE
{ "handle": 7 }
// COLLECT_START
{ "session": 3, "session_uuid": "6f0c…", "handle": 7, "label": "cheat",
  "tags": { "client": "x", "module": "killaura" }, "note": "", "started_at_ms": 0 }
// COLLECT_STOP
{ "session": 3, "stopped_at_ms": 0 }
// MARK
{ "session": 3, "tick": 18234, "state": "on", "note": "" }
```

- `handle` is a u32 the client assigns to a player for the lifetime of the stream.
- `player` is a pseudonymous player id: `hmac_sha256(server_salt, uuid_bytes)` in
  lowercase hex. The raw UUID and the player name never leave the Minecraft server.
- `session` is a u32 the client assigns to a dataset session for the lifetime of the
  stream; `session_uuid` is its globally unique id.
- `label` is `legit`, `cheat` or `unknown`. `tags` is free-form string→string.
- `MARK` records a segment boundary inside a session (for example, the tester turned
  a cheat module on). `tick` is the client tick index (§5.2) of the boundary.

### 3.4 `VERDICT`

```json
{ "handle": 7, "window": 912, "model": "aim-v3", "scores": { "aimbot": 0.93, "legit": 0.07 } }
```

Scores are calibrated probabilities. The client decides what to do with them.

### 3.5 `ERROR`

```json
{ "code": "PROTOCOL", "message": "seq gap: expected 41, got 43", "fatal": true }
```

Codes: `UNAUTHORIZED`, `PROTOCOL`, `SCHEMA`, `OVERLOADED`, `INTERNAL`.
After a fatal error the server closes the connection.

## 4. Schema manifest

The manifest describes the column layouts used in `WINDOWS`. It is generated from
the plugin's schema code and is the single source of truth for column order.

```json
{
  "version": 1,
  "layouts": [
    { "id": 1, "name": "combat", "columns": [ ["dots_yaw", "f32", "rot"], ["strafe", "i8", "inp"] ] },
    { "id": 2, "name": "calm", "columns": [ ["dots_yaw", "f32", "rot"] ] }
  ]
}
```

- Each column is `[name, type, stream]`.
- Types and their sizes: `u8` 1, `i8` 1, `u16` 2, `i16` 2, `u32` 4, `f32` 4.
- Streams: `rot` (mouse), `inp` (keys and clicks), `tgt` (target geometry),
  `mask` (per-stream validity, 1 = valid), `meta` (context that must not be used as
  model input: gate state, mask reasons, uncertainty, item and target kinds).
- Canonical JSON: keys in the order shown, no whitespace.

## 5. `WINDOWS` payload

```
u16  count
repeat count times:
  u32  handle        player handle (§3.3)
  u32  window        window id, unique and increasing within the stream
  u32  session       dataset session (§3.3), 0 if the window is not collected
  u8   kind          1 = attack, 2 = engage, 3 = calm
  u8   purpose       bit 0 = collect (persist to the dataset), bit 1 = infer
  u8   layout        layout id from the manifest
  u8   reserved      0
  u32  epoch         continuity id of the player's tick timeline, §5.3
  u32  engagement    engagement id, §5.3
  u32  first_tick    client tick index of row 0
  u16  rows          number of rows (1..4096)
  i16  anchor        row index of the anchor event, -1 for calm windows
  columns            for each column of the layout, in manifest order:
                     rows values of that column, little-endian, packed
```

The size of a window is fully determined by its header and the manifest. A payload
whose length does not match is a fatal protocol error.

### 5.1 Window kinds

- `attack`: fixed-length window around a melee hit on a target (`anchor` = hit row).
- `engage`: fixed-length window around the first tick of an engagement, the moment
  a target first becomes hittable after a calm period (`anchor` = that row).
- `calm`: a contiguous run of non-combat ticks, variable length. Calm windows use
  the low-dimensional `calm` layout.

### 5.2 Ticks

`first_tick` and `MARK.tick` count client ticks, as delimited by the client's
`CLIENT_TICK_END` packet, starting from 0 when the player joins. Rows of a window are
consecutive client ticks. A world change or respawn starts a new epoch (§5.3); windows
never span it.

### 5.3 Linking combat and calm data

Training pairs a player's combat windows with that same player's calm behaviour
(the calm data is the per-player baseline). The client therefore labels every window
with enough context to rebuild the timeline exactly:

- `epoch` (u32, starts at 1 per handle) increases whenever the player's tick timeline
  is broken: join, respawn, world change. Windows of the same handle and epoch lie on
  one continuous tick axis and can be ordered by `first_tick`.
- `engagement` (u32, starts at 1 per handle, 0 = none yet) increases each time the
  player enters combat after a calm period, that is, at every `engage` anchor.
  - `attack` and `engage` windows carry the engagement they belong to.
  - `calm` windows carry the most recent engagement that ended before the segment
    started (0 if the player has not fought yet). The counter is per handle and keeps
    increasing across epochs.

So for engagement `E` of a player, the calm segments before it are the calm windows
with the same handle and epoch and `engagement < E`, and the ones right after it carry
`engagement = E`. Across sessions and reconnects, the stable pseudonymous `player` id
(§3.3) links a player's data.

```

`paper/src/main/resources/config.yml`:

```yml
# Eigen configuration.

backend:
  # Stream data to the Eigen backend. Required for inference and for server-side collection.
  enabled: false
  url: "wss://eigen.example.com/v1/stream"
  # Issued by the backend operator. Never share it; it identifies this server.
  token: ""
  # Bytes of unacknowledged data kept for resending after a reconnect.
  resend-buffer-mb: 64

privacy:
  # Secret used to pseudonymise player ids before they leave this server. Generated on first
  # start. Changing it makes the same player look like a new one to the backend.
  salt: ""

collect:
  # Where collected sessions go: server, local or both. Local sessions are written to
  # plugins/Eigen/collect/ in the same format the backend stores.
  sink: server
  # Automatically start a self-service legit recording for every non-exempt player on join.
  auto-legit: false
  # Optional 100%-equivalent Shard 2 dataset collector for head-to-head benchmarks.
  # Disabled by default; when false, zero Shard listeners or buffers are allocated.
  shard-compat: false
  shard-pre-window: 128
  shard-post-window: 128

garnet:
  # Share mouse calibration, calm baselines, alerts and violation counts across servers via Garnet.
  enabled: false
  uri: "redis://127.0.0.1:6379"
  # Logical name of this Paper instance in cross-server alerts.
  server: "default"
  # Seconds to retain per-player handoff state and violation counts after disconnect/update.
  ttl-seconds: 600

detection:
  # Targets that open combat: players, or living (players and mobs).
  targets: players
  gate:
    # Combat starts with a hit and lasts this many ticks after the last one.
    combat-ticks: 60
    # Calm ticks closer than this to combat on either side are neither combat nor calm.
    buffer-ticks: 60
  windows:
    attack-pre: 96
    attack-post: 32
    attack-min-gap: 20
    engage-pre: 32
    engage-post: 96
  calm:
    min-ticks: 100
    max-ticks: 1200
    min-active: 0.25
  # Players connecting through Geyser/Floodgate play with touch or a controller.
  exempt-bedrock: true

alerts:
  # Minimum score of a non-legit label for a staff alert.
  threshold: 0.9
```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/Eigen.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.PacketEvents
import io.papermc.paper.threadedregions.scheduler.ScheduledTask
import org.bukkit.Bukkit
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin

import eigen.commands.CommandTree
import eigen.paper.alert.Alerts
import eigen.paper.cmd.EigenCommands
import eigen.paper.collect.{Collector, LocalSink}
import eigen.paper.config.Settings
import eigen.paper.garnet.GarnetBridge
import eigen.paper.net.{Inbound, Outbound, Players}
import eigen.paper.stream.StreamClient
import eigen.shard.ShardCompat

/**
 * The plugin's object graph. Built at compile time by MacWire's `autowire` in [[Eigen.wire]]:
 * every service receives its dependencies through its constructor, with no runtime container.
 */
final class Eigen(
    plugin: JavaPlugin,
    stream: StreamClient,
    local: LocalSink,
    shardCompat: ShardCompat,
    garnet: GarnetBridge,
    players: Players,
    collector: Collector,
    alerts: Alerts,
    inbound: Inbound,
    outbound: Outbound,
    commands: EigenCommands,
):
  private var heartbeat: ScheduledTask | Null = null

  def start(): Unit =
    players.onAnnounced = collector.maybeAutoStart
    stream.onVerdict = alerts.onVerdict
    garnet.start(alerts.onRemoteAlert)
    stream.start()
    shardCompat.bindTransactionSource(user => { val p = players.get(user); if p == null then null else p.shardTransactions })
    shardCompat.start()
    val events = PacketEvents.getAPI.getEventManager
    events.registerListener(inbound)
    events.registerListener(outbound)
    CommandTree.register(plugin, commands.spec)
    // One transaction per server tick keeps acknowledgements flowing while nothing else is sent.
    heartbeat = Bukkit.getGlobalRegionScheduler.runAtFixedRate(plugin, _ => players.all.foreach(_.sendTransaction()), 1L, 1L)

  def stop(): Unit =
    val h = heartbeat
    if h != null then h.cancel()
    val events = PacketEvents.getAPI.getEventManager
    events.unregisterListener(inbound)
    events.unregisterListener(outbound)
    collector.stopAll()
    shardCompat.stop()
    stream.stop()
    garnet.stop()
    local.shutdown()

object Eigen:
  import com.softwaremill.macwire.autowire

  def wire(plugin: JavaPlugin, settings: Settings): Eigen =
    val stream = StreamClient(
      settings.backend,
      s"eigen ${plugin.getPluginMeta.getVersion}",
      s"${Bukkit.getName} ${Bukkit.getMinecraftVersion}",
      plugin.getLogger,
    )
    val collectRoot = plugin.getDataFolder.toPath.resolve("collect")
    val local = LocalSink(collectRoot, plugin.getLogger)
    val shardCompat = ShardCompat.load(plugin, collectRoot.resolve("shard"), settings.shardCompat, plugin.getLogger)
    val garnet = GarnetBridge(settings, plugin.getLogger)
    autowire[Eigen](plugin, settings, stream, local, shardCompat, garnet)
```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/EigenPlugin.scala`:

/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors

 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper

import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin

import eigen.core.schema.Manifest
import eigen.paper.config.Settings

final class EigenPlugin extends JavaPlugin:
  private var eigen: Eigen | Null = null

  override def onEnable(): Unit =
    Settings.load(this) match
      case Left(problem) =>
        getLogger.severe(s"Invalid configuration: $problem. Eigen is disabled.")
        getServer.getPluginManager.disablePlugin(this)
      case Right(settings) =>
        val e = Eigen.wire(this, settings)
        e.start()
        eigen = e
        getLogger.info(s"Eigen ${getPluginMeta.getVersion} enabled (schema ${Manifest.hash})")

  override def onDisable(): Unit =
    val e = eigen
    if e != null then e.stop()
    eigen = null

```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/alert/Alerts.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.alert

import java.util.UUID
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap

import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*

import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component
import net.kyori.adventure.text.format.NamedTextColor
import org.bukkit.Bukkit
import org.bukkit.entity.Player
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin

import eigen.core.wire.Messages
import eigen.paper.config.Settings
import eigen.paper.garnet.{AlertBroadcast, GarnetBridge}
import eigen.paper.net.Players

/** Staff notifications for backend verdicts. Staff opt in with `/eigen alerts`. */
final class Alerts(settings: Settings, players: Players, garnet: GarnetBridge, plugin: JavaPlugin):
  private val listening = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet[UUID]()

  /** Toggles alerts for `staff`; returns whether they are now on. */
  def toggle(staff: Player): Boolean =
    if listening.remove(staff.getUniqueId) then false
    else
      listening.add(staff.getUniqueId)
      true

  /** Handles a verdict from the stream thread. */
  def onVerdict(v: Messages.Verdict): Unit =
    val suspect = v.scores.iterator.filter((label, _) => label != "legit").maxByOption(_._2)
    val p = players.byHandleOrNull(v.handle)
    suspect.foreach: (label, score) =>
      if score >= settings.alertThreshold && p != null then
        val playerName = p.name
        garnet.recordViolation(playerName, label, score, v.model): status =>
          val message = formatAlert(None, playerName, label, score, v.model, status.vl)
          Bukkit.getGlobalRegionScheduler.execute(plugin, () => broadcast(message))

  /** Handles an alert published by another Paper instance over Garnet Pub/Sub. */
  def onRemoteAlert(a: AlertBroadcast): Unit =
    val message = formatAlert(Some(a.server), a.player, a.label, a.score, a.model, a.vl)
    Bukkit.getGlobalRegionScheduler.execute(plugin, () => broadcast(message))

  private def formatAlert(
      remoteServer: Option[String],
      player: String,
      label: String,
      score: Double,
      model: String,
      vl: Long,
  ): Component =
    val prefix = remoteServer.fold("[Eigen] ")(srv => s"[Eigen:$srv] ")
    Component
      .text(prefix, NamedTextColor.DARK_AQUA)
      .append(Component.text(player, NamedTextColor.WHITE))
      .append(Component.text(f" $label ${score * 100}%.1f%% ($model) ", NamedTextColor.GRAY))
      .append(Component.text(s"[x$vl]", NamedTextColor.AQUA))

  private def broadcast(message: Component): Unit =
    Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers.asScala
      .filter(s => listening.contains(s.getUniqueId) && s.hasPermission("eigen.alerts"))
      .foreach(_.sendMessage(message))
    plugin.getComponentLogger.info(message)
```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/cmd/EigenCommands.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.cmd

import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component
import net.kyori.adventure.text.format.NamedTextColor
import org.bukkit.entity.Player

import eigen.commands.{Arg, CommandSpec, Ctx}
import eigen.commands.CommandTree.*
import eigen.core.sim.RotationSystem
import eigen.paper.alert.Alerts
import eigen.paper.collect.{CollectSession, Collector, Label}
import eigen.paper.garnet.GarnetBridge
import eigen.paper.net.Players
import eigen.paper.stream.StreamClient

/** The `/eigen` command tree. */
final class EigenCommands(
    players: Players,
    collector: Collector,
    stream: StreamClient,
    alerts: Alerts,
    garnet: GarnetBridge,
):
  private val Target = Arg.player("player")
  private val PlayerName = Arg.word("player")
  private val LabelArg = Arg.oneOf("label", Label.values.map(_.wire).toSeq*)
  private val Details = Arg.text("details")
  private val Toggle = Arg.oneOf("state", "on", "off")
  private val Note = Arg.text("note")

  val spec: CommandSpec = command("eigen", "Eigen anti-cheat", "ei"):
    requiresAny("eigen.command", "eigen.record")

    literal("status"):
      requires("eigen.command")
      run("connection and collection overview")(status)

    literal("alerts"):
      requires("eigen.alerts")
      run("toggle staff alerts"): ctx =>
        ctx.raw.getSource.getSender match
          case staff: Player => ctx.reply(if alerts.toggle(staff) then "Alerts on." else "Alerts off.")
          case _ => ctx.fail("Only players receive alerts.")

    literal("vl"):
      requires("eigen.alerts")
      arg(PlayerName):
        run("inspect violation count of a player")(vl)

    literal("debug"):
      requires("eigen.admin")
      arg(Target):
        run("live simulation state of a player")(debug)

    literal("exempt"):
      requires("eigen.admin")
      arg(Target):
        run("toggle streaming for a player"): ctx =>
          val p = tracked(ctx, ctx(Target))
          p.exempt = !p.exempt
          ctx.reply(s"${p.name} is ${if p.exempt then "exempt" else "no longer exempt"}.")

    literal("collect"):
      requires("eigen.collect")
      literal("start"):
        arg(Target):
          arg(LabelArg):
            run("record a labelled session")(ctx => start(ctx, ""))
            arg(Details):
              run("…with key=value tags and a note")(ctx => start(ctx, ctx(Details)))
      literal("stop"):
        arg(Target):
          run("finish a recording"): ctx =>
            val target = ctx(Target)
            collector.stop(target.getUniqueId).fold(ctx.fail, s => ctx.reply(s"Stopped ${describe(s)}."))
      literal("mark"):
        arg(Target):
          arg(Toggle):
            run("mark a cheat toggle inside a recording")(ctx => mark(ctx, ""))
            arg(Note):
              run("…with a note")(ctx => mark(ctx, ctx(Note)))
      literal("list"):
        run("recordings in progress"): ctx =>
          val active = collector.active.toSeq
          if active.isEmpty then ctx.reply("No recordings in progress.")
          else active.foreach(s => ctx.reply(describe(s)))

    literal("record"):
      requires("eigen.record")
      literal("start"):
        run("record your own gameplay as legit")(ctx => recordStart(ctx, ""))
        arg(Note):
          run("…with an optional note")(ctx => recordStart(ctx, ctx(Note)))
      literal("stop"):
        run("stop your own legit recording")(recordStop)
      literal("status"):
        run("check your calibration and recording progress")(recordStatus)

    literal("help"):
      run(ctx => help(ctx))

  private def help(ctx: Ctx): Unit = spec.usage.foreach(line => ctx.reply(line))

  private def status(ctx: Ctx): Unit =
    val tracked = players.all.size
    ctx.reply(header("Eigen"))
    ctx.reply(s"Backend: ${stream.state.toString.toLowerCase}${if stream.inference then ", inference on" else ""}")
    if garnet.enabled then
      ctx.reply(s"Garnet: ${if garnet.connected then "connected" else "connecting/offline"}")
    ctx.reply(s"Players tracked: $tracked, recordings: ${collector.active.size}")
    if stream.enabled then ctx.reply(s"Queued windows: ${stream.backlog}, dropped: ${stream.dropped}")

  private def vl(ctx: Ctx): Unit =
    val name = ctx(PlayerName)
    garnet.queryViolation(name):
      case Some(st) =>
        ctx.reply(
          f"${st.player}: VL x${st.vl} — last ${st.label} ${st.score * 100}%.1f%% (${st.model}) on ${st.server}"
        )
      case None =>
        ctx.reply(s"No violations recorded for $name.")

  private def recordStart(ctx: Ctx, note: String): Unit =
    val self = senderPlayer(ctx)
    collector
      .start(self.getUniqueId, self.getName, Label.Legit, Map("source" -> "self"), note.trim, selfService = true)
      .fold(ctx.fail, s => ctx.reply(s"Started self-recording (${describe(s)})."))

  private def recordStop(ctx: Ctx): Unit =
    val self = senderPlayer(ctx)
    collector.stopSelf(self.getUniqueId).fold(ctx.fail, s => ctx.reply(s"Stopped self-recording (${describe(s)})."))

  private def recordStatus(ctx: Ctx): Unit =
    val self = senderPlayer(ctx)
    val p = tracked(ctx, self)
    p.post: () =>
      val calibrated = RotationSystem.calibrated(p.sim.rotation)
      val gate = if p.sim.combat.gate then "combat" else "calm"
      val rec = Option(p.session).fold("not recording")(s => s"recording (${s.windows.get} windows)")
      ctx.reply(s"Calibration: ${if calibrated then "ready" else "warming up"}, gate: $gate, status: $rec")

  private def senderPlayer(ctx: Ctx): Player =
    ctx.raw.getSource.getSender match
      case player: Player => player
      case _ => ctx.fail("Only players can use /eigen record.")

  private def debug(ctx: Ctx): Unit =
    val p = tracked(ctx, ctx(Target))
    // Read on the player's network thread so the snapshot is consistent.
    p.post: () =>
      val s = p.sim
      val r = s.rotation
      val c = s.combat
      ctx.reply(header(p.name))
      val lines = Seq(
        s"client protocol ${p.profile.protocol}, tick-end ${p.profile.tickEnd}, brand ${p.brand}",
        f"rtt ${p.clock.rtt}%.0f ms, tick ${s.tick}, epoch ${s.epoch}, engagement ${c.engagement}",
        f"mouse step yaw ${r.yawAxis.currentStep}%.5f (${if r.yawAxis.converged then "ok" else "calibrating"}), pitch ${r.pitchAxis.currentStep}%.5f",
        s"gate ${if c.gate then "combat" else "calm"}, entities ${s.entities.size}",
        f"aim ${c.aimYaw}%.2f/${c.aimPitch}%.2f, distance ${c.distance}%.2f, inflation ${c.inflation}%.2f",
        s"recording: ${Option(p.session).map(describe).getOrElse("no")}, exempt ${p.exempt}",
      )
      lines.foreach(l => ctx.reply(l))

  private def start(ctx: Ctx, details: String): Unit =
    val target = ctx(Target)
    val label = Label.parse(ctx(LabelArg)).getOrElse(ctx.fail("Unknown label."))
    val (tagTokens, words) = details.split("\\s+").filter(_.nonEmpty).partition(_.contains('='))
    val tags = tagTokens.map(_.split("=", 2)).collect { case Array(k, v) if k.nonEmpty => k -> v }.toMap
    collector.start(target, label, tags, words.mkString(" ")).fold(ctx.fail, s => ctx.reply(s"Recording ${describe(s)}."))

  private def mark(ctx: Ctx, note: String): Unit =
    val target = ctx(Target)
    val on = ctx(Toggle) == "on"
    collector.mark(target.getUniqueId, on, note).fold(ctx.fail, _ => ctx.reply(s"Marked ${target.getName}: ${if on then "on" else "off"}."))

  private def tracked(ctx: Ctx, player: Player) =
    val p = players.get(player.getUniqueId)
    if p == null then ctx.fail(s"${player.getName} is not tracked.") else p

  private def describe(s: CollectSession): String =
    val tags = if s.tags.isEmpty then "" else s.tags.map((k, v) => s"$k=$v").mkString(" [", ", ", "]")
    s"${s.playerName} as ${s.label.wire}$tags, ${s.windows.get} windows"

  private def header(title: String): Component =
    Component.text(s"— $title —", NamedTextColor.DARK_AQUA)
```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/collect/CollectSession.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.collect

import java.util.UUID
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger

/** Labels a collection session can carry (protocol §3.3). */
enum Label(val wire: String):
  case Legit extends Label("legit")
  case Cheat extends Label("cheat")
  case Unknown extends Label("unknown")

object Label:
  def parse(s: String): Option[Label] = values.find(_.wire == s.toLowerCase)

/** One recording of one player, from `collect start` to `collect stop`. */
final class CollectSession(
    val id: Int,
    val uuid: UUID,
    val handle: Int,
    val playerName: String,
    val player: String,
    val label: Label,
    val tags: Map[String, String],
    val note: String,
    val startedAt: Long,
    val selfService: Boolean = false,
):
  val windows = AtomicInteger()
  @volatile var cheatActive: Boolean = label == Label.Cheat
```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/collect/Collector.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.collect

import java.util.UUID
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger

import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*

import org.bukkit.entity.Player

import eigen.core.sim.WindowSystem
import eigen.core.wire.Messages
import eigen.paper.config.Settings
import eigen.paper.net.{EigenPlayer, Players}
import eigen.paper.stream.StreamClient
import eigen.shard.ShardCompat

/**
 * Dataset collection: starts and stops labelled recordings of players and records segment
 * marks. Called from commands on the server thread; changes that touch a player's simulation
 * are posted to its network thread.
 */
final class Collector(settings: Settings, players: Players, stream: StreamClient, local: LocalSink, shardCompat: ShardCompat):
  private val sessions = ConcurrentHashMap[UUID, CollectSession]()
  private val ids = AtomicInteger(0)

  def active: Iterable[CollectSession] = sessions.values().asScala
  def get(uuid: UUID): CollectSession | Null = sessions.get(uuid)

  def start(target: Player, label: Label, tags: Map[String, String], note: String): Either[String, CollectSession] =
    start(target.getUniqueId, target.getName, label, tags, note, selfService = false)

  def start(
      targetUuid: UUID,
      targetName: String,
      label: Label,
      tags: Map[String, String],
      note: String,
      selfService: Boolean,
  ): Either[String, CollectSession] =
    val p = players.get(targetUuid)
    if p == null then Left(s"$targetName is not tracked yet; try again in a moment.")
    else if p.exempt then Left(s"$targetName is exempt from collection.")
    else if settings.sink.server && !stream.enabled && !settings.sink.local then Left("The backend is disabled and collect.sink is server.")
    else
      val s = CollectSession(
        id = ids.incrementAndGet(),
        uuid = UUID.randomUUID(),
        handle = p.handle,
        playerName = targetName,
        player = p.pseudonym,
        label = label,
        tags = tags,
        note = note,
        startedAt = System.currentTimeMillis(),
        selfService = selfService,
      )
      if sessions.putIfAbsent(targetUuid, s) != null then Left(s"$targetName is already being recorded.")
      else
        if settings.sink.server then
          stream.sessionStarted(Messages.CollectStart(s.id, s.uuid.toString, s.handle, label.wire, tags, note, s.startedAt))
        val eigenDir = if settings.sink.local then
          local.start(s)
          local.sessionDir(s)
        else null
        if shardCompat.enabled then
          val details =
            val tagPart = tags.map((k, v) => s"$k=$v").mkString(" ")
            Seq(tagPart, note).filter(_.nonEmpty).mkString(" ")
          shardCompat.startSession(targetUuid, targetName, label.wire, details, s.startedAt, eigenDir)
        // The session becomes visible to the simulation only after the start was queued, so no
        // window can reference a session the backend has not heard of.
        p.post(() => p.session = s)
        Right(s)

  /** Automatically starts a legit self-service session on join when `collect.auto-legit` is enabled. */
  def maybeAutoStart(p: EigenPlayer): Unit =
    if settings.autoLegit && !p.exempt && (settings.sink.local || (settings.sink.server && stream.enabled)) then
      val _ = start(p.uuid, p.name, Label.Legit, Map("source" -> "auto"), "", selfService = true)

  def stop(target: UUID): Either[String, CollectSession] = finish(target, direct = false)

  /** Stops a self-service session started via `/eigen record start` or `collect.auto-legit`. */
  def stopSelf(target: UUID): Either[String, CollectSession] =
    val s = sessions.get(target)
    if s == null then Left("You do not have an active recording.")
    else if !s.selfService then Left("Your recording was started by staff and cannot be stopped with /eigen record stop.")
    else finish(target, direct = false)

  /**
   * Ends a recording. Normally this runs on the player's network thread, after its last window;
   * when the plugin is stopping or the player is leaving, it runs on the calling thread instead,
   * because no further packets (and so no mailbox drains) will come.
   */
  private def finish(target: UUID, direct: Boolean): Either[String, CollectSession] =
    val s = sessions.remove(target)
    if s == null then Left("No recording in progress for that player.")
    else
      val stoppedAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
      val p = players.get(target)
      val close: Runnable = () =>
        if p != null then
          // Flush while the session is still attached, so the last calm segment is recorded.
          WindowSystem.flushCalm(p.sim)
          p.session = null
        if settings.sink.server then stream.sessionStopped(Messages.CollectStop(s.id, stoppedAt))
        if settings.sink.local then local.stop(s, stoppedAt)
        if shardCompat.enabled then shardCompat.stopSession(target)
      if p == null || direct then close.run() else p.post(close)
      Right(s)

  /** Marks a segment boundary at the player's current client tick (e.g. a cheat toggled). */
  def mark(target: UUID, on: Boolean, note: String): Either[String, CollectSession] =
    val s = sessions.get(target)
    val p = players.get(target)
    if s == null || p == null then Left("No recording in progress for that player.")
    else
      p.post: () =>
        val tick = p.sim.tick
        s.cheatActive = on
        if settings.sink.server then stream.mark(Messages.Mark(s.id, tick, if on then "on" else "off", note))
        if settings.sink.local then local.mark(s, tick, on, note)
      Right(s)

  /** Stops every recording; used when the plugin disables. */
  def stopAll(): Unit = sessions.keys().asScala.toSeq.foreach(u => { val _ = finish(u, direct = true) })

  /** A player left: finish their recording now, while the player is still tracked. */
  def onQuit(uuid: UUID): Unit = { val _ = finish(uuid, direct = true) }
```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/collect/LocalSink.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.collect

import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.nio.ByteOrder
import java.nio.channels.FileChannel
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
import java.nio.file.{Files, Path, StandardOpenOption}
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter
import java.time.{Instant, ZoneOffset}
import java.util.concurrent.{ExecutorService, Executors, TimeUnit}
import java.util.logging.{Level, Logger}

import scala.collection.mutable

import com.github.plokhotnyuk.jsoniter_scala.core.*
import com.github.plokhotnyuk.jsoniter_scala.macros.*

import eigen.core.history.Window
import eigen.core.schema.Manifest
import eigen.core.wire.FrameCodec

/**
 * Writes collected sessions to disk, in the layout the backend stores them:
 *
 * {{{
 * collect/<label>_<player>_<utc time>_<id>/
 *   manifest.json   schema manifest (protocol §4)
 *   session.json    session metadata, rewritten when the session stops
 *   windows.bin     records of: u32 length, then one window exactly as in WINDOWS (§5)
 *   marks.jsonl     segment boundaries recorded with `collect mark`
 * }}}
 *
 * All file work happens on one background thread; the network thread only enqueues.
 */
final class LocalSink(root: Path, log: Logger):
  import LocalSink.*

  private val io: ExecutorService = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(r =>
    val t = Thread(r, "eigen-collect-io")
    t.setDaemon(true)
    t,
  )
  private val codec = FrameCodec()
  private val open = mutable.HashMap[Int, Open]()

  private final class Open(val dir: Path, val windows: FileChannel):
    var nextId = 1

  /** Computes the local session directory path for `s`. */
  def sessionDir(s: CollectSession): Path =
    val time = Stamp.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(s.startedAt))
    root.resolve(s"${s.label.wire}_${safe(s.playerName)}_${time}_${s.uuid.toString.take(8)}")

  def start(s: CollectSession): Unit = io.execute: () =>
    attempt(s, "open"):
      val dir = sessionDir(s)
      Files.createDirectories(dir)
      Files.write(dir.resolve("manifest.json"), Manifest.bytes)
      writeMeta(dir, s, stoppedAt = 0L)
      val channel = FileChannel.open(dir.resolve("windows.bin"), StandardOpenOption.CREATE, StandardOpenOption.WRITE, StandardOpenOption.APPEND)
      open(s.id) = Open(dir, channel)

  def write(s: CollectSession, w: Window): Unit = io.execute: () =>
    open.get(s.id).foreach: o =>
      attempt(s, "write"):
        val record = codec.windowRecord(w, Window.PurposeCollect, o.nextId)
        o.nextId += 1
        val len = ByteBuffer.allocate(4).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN).putInt(record.length).flip()
        o.windows.write(Array(len, ByteBuffer.wrap(record)))

  def mark(s: CollectSession, tick: Int, on: Boolean, note: String): Unit = io.execute: () =>
    open.get(s.id).foreach: o =>
      attempt(s, "mark"):
        val line = writeToString(MarkLine(tick, if on then "on" else "off", note, System.currentTimeMillis())) + "\n"
        Files.write(o.dir.resolve("marks.jsonl"), line.getBytes(UTF_8), StandardOpenOption.CREATE, StandardOpenOption.APPEND)

  def stop(s: CollectSession, stoppedAt: Long): Unit = io.execute: () =>
    open.remove(s.id).foreach: o =>
      attempt(s, "close"):
        o.windows.force(true)
        o.windows.close()
        writeMeta(o.dir, s, stoppedAt)

  /** Finishes queued writes; used on plugin disable. */
  def shutdown(): Unit =
    io.shutdown()
    if !io.awaitTermination(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS) then log.warning("Collection writes did not finish in time")

  private def writeMeta(dir: Path, s: CollectSession, stoppedAt: Long): Unit =
    val meta = SessionMeta(
      sessionUuid = s.uuid.toString,
      player = s.player,
      playerName = s.playerName,
      label = s.label.wire,
      tags = s.tags,
      note = s.note,
      startedAtMs = s.startedAt,
      stoppedAtMs = stoppedAt,
      windows = s.windows.get,
      schemaHash = Manifest.hash,
    )
    val tmp = dir.resolve("session.json.tmp")
    Files.write(tmp, writeToArray(meta, WriterConfig.withIndentionStep(2)))
    Files.move(tmp, dir.resolve("session.json"), java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING, java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE)

  private inline def attempt(s: CollectSession, what: String)(body: => Unit): Unit =
    try body
    catch case e: java.io.IOException => log.log(Level.SEVERE, s"Collection session ${s.uuid}: $what failed", e)

object LocalSink:
  private val Stamp = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd-HHmmss").withZone(ZoneOffset.UTC)

  private def safe(s: String): String = s.replaceAll("[^A-Za-z0-9_-]", "-")

  private final case class SessionMeta(
      sessionUuid: String,
      player: String,
      playerName: String,
      label: String,
      tags: Map[String, String],
      note: String,
      startedAtMs: Long,
      stoppedAtMs: Long,
      windows: Int,
      schemaHash: String,
  )
  private final case class MarkLine(tick: Int, state: String, note: String, atMs: Long)

  private given JsonValueCodec[SessionMeta] =
    JsonCodecMaker.make(CodecMakerConfig.withFieldNameMapper(JsonCodecMaker.enforce_snake_case).withTransientEmpty(false))
  private given JsonValueCodec[MarkLine] =
    JsonCodecMaker.make(CodecMakerConfig.withFieldNameMapper(JsonCodecMaker.enforce_snake_case))

```

/*

 */

package eigen.paper.config

import org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection

import eigen.config.Source

/** Presents a Bukkit configuration section as a platform-free [[Source]]. */
final class BukkitSource(section: ConfigurationSection) extends Source:
  def value(key: String): Option[Any] =
    Option(section.get(key)).filterNot(_.isInstanceOf[ConfigurationSection])

  def section(key: String): Option[Source] =
    Option(section.getConfigurationSection(key)).map(BukkitSource(_))

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/config/Settings.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.config

import java.net.URI
import java.security.SecureRandom
import java.util.HexFormat

import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin

import eigen.config.Read
import eigen.core.sim.SimConfig
import eigen.shard.ShardCompatConfig

enum CollectSink:
  case Server, Local, Both

  def server: Boolean = this != Local
  def local: Boolean = this != Server

final case class BackendSettings(enabled: Boolean, url: URI, token: String, resendBufferBytes: Long)

final case class GarnetSettings(enabled: Boolean, uri: URI, server: String, ttlSeconds: Int)

final case class Settings(
    backend: BackendSettings,
    salt: Array[Byte],
    sink: CollectSink,
    autoLegit: Boolean,
    shardCompat: ShardCompatConfig,
    garnet: GarnetSettings,
    sim: SimConfig,
    exemptBedrock: Boolean,
    alertThreshold: Double,
)

/**
 * The plugin's configuration. Every key is declared once in [[Settings.schema]] with its type,
 * default and check; the bundled `config.yml` documents the same keys and must read back to the
 * same defaults (see the settings test).
 */
object Settings:

  private val defaults = SimConfig()
  private val DefaultEndpoint = URI.create("wss://eigen.example.com/v1/stream")
  private val DefaultGarnetUri = URI.create("redis://127.0.0.1:6379")
  private val LoopbackHosts = Set("localhost", "127.0.0.1", "[::1]", "::1")
  private val SaltHexDigits = 64

  /** Reads `config.yml` from the plugin, writing defaults and a fresh privacy salt on first start. */
  def load(plugin: JavaPlugin): Either[String, Settings] =
    plugin.saveDefaultConfig()
    plugin.reloadConfig()
    ensureSalt(plugin)
    schema.from(BukkitSource(plugin.getConfig))

  private val backendSchema: Read[BackendSettings] =
    for
      enabled <- Read.bool("enabled", default = false)
      url <- Read.key("url", DefaultEndpoint)(endpoint)
      token <- Read.text("token", "").map(_.trim)
      resendMb <- Read.int("resend-buffer-mb", default = 64, min = 1)
    yield BackendSettings(enabled, url, token, resendMb.toLong * 1024 * 1024)

  private val sinkSchema: Read[CollectSink] =
    Read.choice("sink", "server")("server" -> CollectSink.Server, "local" -> CollectSink.Local, "both" -> CollectSink.Both)

  private val collectSchema: Read[(CollectSink, Boolean, ShardCompatConfig)] =
    for
      sink <- sinkSchema
      autoLegit <- Read.bool("auto-legit", default = false)
      shardCompat <- Read.bool("shard-compat", default = false)
      shardPre <- Read.int("shard-pre-window", default = 128, min = 1)
      shardPost <- Read.int("shard-post-window", default = 128, min = 1)
    yield (sink, autoLegit, ShardCompatConfig(shardCompat, shardPre, shardPost))

  private val garnetSchema: Read[GarnetSettings] =
    for
      enabled <- Read.bool("enabled", default = false)
      uri <- Read.key("uri", DefaultGarnetUri)(garnetUri)
      server <- Read.text("server", "default").map(_.trim)
      _ <- Read.ensure(server.nonEmpty, "server", "must not be empty")
      ttl <- Read.int("ttl-seconds", default = 600, min = 10)
    yield GarnetSettings(enabled, uri, server, ttl)

  private final case class Detection(sim: SimConfig, exemptBedrock: Boolean)

  private val detectionSchema: Read[Detection] =
    for
      living <- Read.choice("targets", "players")("players" -> false, "living" -> true)
      gate <- Read.section("gate"):
        for
          combat <- Read.int("combat-ticks", defaults.combatTicks, min = 1)
          buffer <- Read.int("buffer-ticks", defaults.bufferTicks, min = 0)
        yield (combat, buffer)
      windows <- Read.section("windows"):
        for
          attackPre <- Read.int("attack-pre", defaults.attackPre, min = 0)
          attackPost <- Read.int("attack-post", defaults.attackPost, min = 1)
          attackGap <- Read.int("attack-min-gap", defaults.attackMinGap, min = 0)
          enginePre <- Read.int("engage-pre", defaults.engagePre, min = 0)
          enginePost <- Read.int("engage-post", defaults.engagePost, min = 1)
        yield (attackPre, attackPost, attackGap, enginePre, enginePost)
      calm <- Read.section("calm"):
        for
          minTicks <- Read.int("min-ticks", defaults.calmMinTicks, min = 1)
          maxTicks <- Read.int("max-ticks", defaults.calmMaxTicks, min = 1)
          _ <- Read.ensure(maxTicks >= minTicks, "max-ticks", "must be at least min-ticks")
          minActive <- Read.double("min-active", defaults.calmMinActive.toDouble)
        yield (minTicks, maxTicks, minActive.toFloat)
      exempt <- Read.bool("exempt-bedrock", default = true)
    yield
      val (combat, buffer) = gate
      val (attackPre, attackPost, attackGap, enginePre, enginePost) = windows
      val (minTicks, maxTicks, minActive) = calm
      Detection(
        SimConfig(
          targetLiving = living,
          combatTicks = combat,
          bufferTicks = buffer,
          attackPre = attackPre,
          attackPost = attackPost,
          attackMinGap = attackGap,
          engagePre = enginePre,
          engagePost = enginePost,
          calmMinTicks = minTicks,
          calmMaxTicks = maxTicks,
          calmMinActive = minActive,
        ),
        exempt,
      )

  /** The whole configuration, assembled after its parts so their initialisation order is safe. */
  val schema: Read[Settings] =
    for
      backend <- Read.section("backend")(backendSchema)
      salt <- Read.section("privacy")(Read.key("salt", Array.empty[Byte])(hexSalt))
      collect <- Read.section("collect")(collectSchema)
      garnet <- Read.section("garnet")(garnetSchema)
      detection <- Read.section("detection")(detectionSchema)
      threshold <- Read.section("alerts")(Read.double("threshold", 0.9))
    yield
      val (sink, autoLegit, shardCompat) = collect
      Settings(backend, salt, sink, autoLegit, shardCompat, garnet, detection.sim, detection.exemptBedrock, threshold)

  /** A Garnet/Redis endpoint: `redis://` or `rediss://`. */
  private def garnetUri(raw: Any): Either[String, URI] = raw match
    case text: String =>
      try
        val uri = URI(text)
        uri.getScheme match
          case "redis" | "rediss" if uri.getHost != null && uri.getHost.nonEmpty => Right(uri)
          case "redis" | "rediss" => Left(s"missing host in '$text'")
          case _ => Left(s"must be redis:// or rediss://, got '$text'")
      catch case e: IllegalArgumentException => Left(s"not a URI: ${e.getMessage}")
    case other => Left(s"must be text, got ${other.getClass.getSimpleName}")

  /** A backend endpoint: `wss://` anywhere, `ws://` only for loopback development. */
  private def endpoint(raw: Any): Either[String, URI] = raw match
    case text: String =>
      try
        val uri = URI(text)
        val loopback = uri.getHost != null && LoopbackHosts.contains(uri.getHost)
        uri.getScheme match
          case "wss" => Right(uri)
          case "ws" if loopback => Right(uri)
          case "ws" => Left("plaintext ws:// is allowed only for loopback hosts; use wss://")
          case _ => Left(s"must be ws:// or wss://, got '$text'")
      catch case e: IllegalArgumentException => Left(s"not a URI: ${e.getMessage}")
    case other => Left(s"must be text, got ${other.getClass.getSimpleName}")

  /** The privacy salt, hex-encoded. The plugin writes one on first start. */
  private def hexSalt(raw: Any): Either[String, Array[Byte]] = raw match
    case text: String if text.length >= SaltHexDigits && text.forall(Character.digit(_, 16) >= 0) =>
      Right(HexFormat.of.parseHex(text))
    case _ => Left(s"must be at least $SaltHexDigits hex digits; delete it to generate a new one")

  private def ensureSalt(plugin: JavaPlugin): Unit =
    val config = plugin.getConfig
    val salt = config.getString("privacy.salt", "")
    if salt == null || salt.length < SaltHexDigits then
      val bytes = new Array[Byte](SaltHexDigits / 2)
      SecureRandom().nextBytes(bytes)
      config.set("privacy.salt", HexFormat.of().formatHex(bytes))
      plugin.saveConfig()
```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/garnet/GarnetBridge.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.garnet

import java.time.Duration
import java.util.Base64
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap
import java.util.logging.{Level, Logger}

import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*

import com.github.plokhotnyuk.jsoniter_scala.core.*
import com.github.plokhotnyuk.jsoniter_scala.macros.*
import io.lettuce.core.{ClientOptions, RedisClient, RedisURI}
import io.lettuce.core.api.StatefulRedisConnection
import io.lettuce.core.pubsub.{RedisPubSubAdapter, StatefulRedisPubSubConnection}

import eigen.paper.config.{GarnetSettings, Settings}

final case class ViolationStatus(
    player: String,
    vl: Long,
    label: String,
    score: Double,
    model: String,
    server: String,
    updatedAt: Long,
)

final case class AlertBroadcast(
    server: String,
    player: String,
    label: String,
    score: Double,
    model: String,
    vl: Long,
    ts: Long,
)

object AlertBroadcast:
  given JsonValueCodec[AlertBroadcast] = JsonCodecMaker.make

/**
 * Microsoft Garnet (Redis RESP) integration for multi-instance deployments:
 *   - Cross-server player state handoff (`eigen:handoff:<pseudonym>`) preserving mouse sensitivity
 *     calibration, engagement counter, and in-progress calm track across lobby/arena switches.
 *   - Shared violation counters (`eigen:vl:<player_lower>`) and cross-server staff alert Pub/Sub
 *     (`eigen:alerts`).
 *
 * Falls back transparently to local in-memory state when `garnet.enabled` is `false`.
 */
final class GarnetBridge(settings: Settings, log: Logger):
  private val cfg: GarnetSettings = settings.garnet
  private val localVl = ConcurrentHashMap[String, ViolationStatus]()

  @volatile private var client: RedisClient | Null = null
  @volatile private var conn: StatefulRedisConnection[String, String] | Null = null
  @volatile private var pubSub: StatefulRedisPubSubConnection[String, String] | Null = null

  private val ChannelAlerts = "eigen:alerts"
  private def handoffKey(pseudonym: String): String = s"eigen:handoff:$pseudonym"
  private def vlKey(playerName: String): String = s"eigen:vl:${playerName.toLowerCase}"

  def enabled: Boolean = cfg.enabled
  def connected: Boolean =
    val c = conn
    c != null && c.isOpen

  def start(onRemoteAlert: AlertBroadcast => Unit): Unit =
    if !cfg.enabled then return
    Thread
      .ofPlatform()
      .daemon(true)
      .name("eigen-garnet-init")
      .start: () =>
        try
          val uri = RedisURI.create(cfg.uri)
          uri.setTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
          val rc = RedisClient.create(uri)
          rc.setOptions(ClientOptions.builder().autoReconnect(true).build())
          val mainConn = rc.connect()
          val subConn = rc.connectPubSub()
          subConn.addListener(
            new RedisPubSubAdapter[String, String]:
              override def message(channel: String, message: String): Unit =
                if channel == ChannelAlerts then
                  try
                    val alert = readFromString[AlertBroadcast](message)
                    if alert.server != cfg.server then onRemoteAlert(alert)
                  catch case e: Exception => log.log(Level.FINE, "Malformed alert on eigen:alerts", e)
          )
          subConn.async().subscribe(ChannelAlerts)
          client = rc
          conn = mainConn
          pubSub = subConn
          log.info(s"Garnet connected (${cfg.uri}, server=${cfg.server})")
        catch
          case e: Exception =>
            log.log(Level.WARNING, s"Could not connect to Garnet at ${cfg.uri}; running in local-only mode", e)

  def stop(): Unit =
    try
      val ps = pubSub
      pubSub = null
      if ps != null then ps.close()
    catch case _: Exception => ()
    try
      val c = conn
      conn = null
      if c != null then c.close()
    catch case _: Exception => ()
    try
      val rc = client
      client = null
      if rc != null then rc.shutdown(Duration.ZERO, Duration.ofSeconds(2))
    catch case _: Exception => ()

  /** Saves a player's serialized handoff snapshot asynchronously with the configured TTL. */
  def saveHandoff(pseudonym: String, payload: Array[Byte]): Unit =
    val c = conn
    if c != null && c.isOpen then
      val encoded = Base64.getEncoder.encodeToString(payload)
      c.async()
        .setex(handoffKey(pseudonym), cfg.ttlSeconds.toLong, encoded)
        .exceptionally: err =>
          log.log(Level.FINE, "Failed to save handoff to Garnet", err)
          null

  /** Fetches a player's serialized handoff snapshot asynchronously, if present. */
  def loadHandoff(pseudonym: String)(onLoaded: Array[Byte] => Unit): Unit =
    val c = conn
    if c != null && c.isOpen then
      c.async()
        .get(handoffKey(pseudonym))
        .thenAccept: encoded =>
          if encoded != null && encoded.nonEmpty then
            try
              val bytes = Base64.getDecoder.decode(encoded)
              onLoaded(bytes)
            catch case _: IllegalArgumentException => ()
        .exceptionally: err =>
          log.log(Level.FINE, "Failed to load handoff from Garnet", err)
          null

  /** Increments a player's violation count, publishes a cross-server alert, and returns the updated VL. */
  def recordViolation(playerName: String, label: String, score: Double, model: String)(onRecorded: ViolationStatus => Unit): Unit =
    val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
    val key = playerName.toLowerCase
    val localUpdated = localVl.compute(
      key,
      (_, prev) =>
        val nextVl = if prev == null then 1L else prev.vl + 1L
        ViolationStatus(playerName, nextVl, label, score, model, cfg.server, now),
    )
    val c = conn
    if c == null || !c.isOpen then onRecorded(localUpdated)
    else
      val rKey = vlKey(playerName)
      val async = c.async()
      val fields = Map(
        "player" -> playerName,
        "label" -> label,
        "score" -> score.toString,
        "model" -> model,
        "server" -> cfg.server,
        "updatedAt" -> now.toString,
      ).asJava
      async
        .hincrby(rKey, "vl", 1L)
        .thenCompose: vl =>
          val status = ViolationStatus(playerName, vl.longValue(), label, score, model, cfg.server, now)
          localVl.put(key, status)
          val _ = async.hset(rKey, fields)
          val _ = async.expire(rKey, cfg.ttlSeconds.toLong)
          val payload = writeToString(AlertBroadcast(cfg.server, playerName, label, score, model, status.vl, now))
          async.publish(ChannelAlerts, payload).thenApply(_ => status)
        .whenComplete: (status, err) =>
          if err != null || status == null then
            log.log(Level.FINE, "Failed to record violation in Garnet", err)
            onRecorded(localUpdated)
          else onRecorded(status)

  /** Looks up the latest violation status for `playerName` from Garnet or local state. */
  def queryViolation(playerName: String)(onResult: Option[ViolationStatus] => Unit): Unit =
    val key = playerName.toLowerCase
    val fallback = Option(localVl.get(key))
    val c = conn
    if c == null || !c.isOpen then onResult(fallback)
    else
      c.async()
        .hgetall(vlKey(playerName))
        .whenComplete: (map, err) =>
          if err != null || map == null || map.isEmpty then onResult(fallback)
          else
            val m = map.asScala
            val parsed =
              for
                vlStr <- m.get("vl")
                vl <- vlStr.toLongOption
                label <- m.get("label")
                scoreStr <- m.get("score")
                score <- scoreStr.toDoubleOption
              yield ViolationStatus(
                player = m.getOrElse("player", playerName),
                vl = vl,
                label = label,
                score = score,
                model = m.getOrElse("model", "unknown"),
                server = m.getOrElse("server", cfg.server),
                updatedAt = m.get("updatedAt").flatMap(_.toLongOption).getOrElse(0L),
              )
            onResult(parsed.orElse(fallback))

```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/net/EigenPlayer.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.net

import java.util.UUID
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketSendEvent
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.ConnectionState
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.entity.`type`.EntityType
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.player.User

import eigen.core.entity.ClientProfile
import eigen.core.history.Window
import eigen.core.latency.TransactionClock
import eigen.core.sim.{PlayerSim, SimConfig}
import eigen.core.util.IntMap
import eigen.paper.collect.CollectSession
import eigen.shard.ShardTransactionSource

/** Adapter-side facts about a tracked entity that the core simulation does not need. */
final class EntityMeta(val tpe: EntityType):
  var crouching = false
  var lowPose = false
  var scale = 1f

/**
 * Everything the plugin keeps for one connection. The simulation and all fields without
 * `@volatile` belong to the connection's network thread; other threads hand work over with
 * [[post]], which runs it there before the next packet is processed.
 */
final class EigenPlayer(
    val user: User,
    val uuid: UUID,
    val handle: Int,
    val profile: ClientProfile,
    val pseudonym: String,
    config: SimConfig,
    windows: (EigenPlayer, Window) => Unit,
):
  val clock = TransactionClock()
  val sim = PlayerSim(profile, config, w => windows(this, w))

  /**
   * ShardCompat's view of this player's clock: the single transaction source for both.
   * Sends go through [[sendTransaction]]/[[bracket]] so `lastSent` advances synchronously
   * via the outbound listener, exactly as for Eigen's own brackets.
   */
  // Lazy so the disabled-by-default ShardCompat costs nothing per player until bound.
  lazy val shardTransactions: ShardTransactionSource = new ShardTransactionSource:
    def lastSent: Int = clock.lastSent
    def lastReceived: Int = clock.lastReceived
    def rttMillis: Double = clock.rtt
    def sendTransaction(): Unit = EigenPlayer.this.sendTransaction()
    override def sendAfter(event: PacketSendEvent): Unit = EigenPlayer.this.sendAfter(event)
    override def preTransaction(event: PacketSendEvent): Int = EigenPlayer.this.preTransaction(event)
    def bracket(event: PacketSendEvent): Int = EigenPlayer.this.bracket(event)
    def schedule(transaction: Int, task: Runnable): Unit = clock.at(transaction)(task)
  val entityMeta = IntMap[EntityMeta](64)

  /** Collection session in progress; set and cleared on the network thread only. */
  var session: CollectSession | Null = null

  @volatile var name: String = user.getName
  @volatile var brand: String = "unknown"
  @volatile var exempt = false
  /** True once `PLAYER_JOIN` was announced to the backend. */
  @volatile var announced = false

  // Send-path state: the last transaction each entity's movement/despawn was ordered with, and
  // the last outbound event already bracketed so Eigen and ShardCompat share one ping pair per packet.
  private val lastMove = IntMap[LastMove](64)
  private val despawned = IntMap[java.lang.Boolean](16)
  private var despawnedAtSent: Int = 0
  private var lastPreEvent: PacketSendEvent | Null = null
  private var lastPreId: Int = 0
  private var lastBracketEvent: PacketSendEvent | Null = null
  private var lastBracketId: Int = 0
  private var lastAfterEvent: PacketSendEvent | Null = null

  private val mailbox = ConcurrentLinkedQueue[Runnable]()

  /** Runs `task` on this player's network thread before its next packet. */
  def post(task: Runnable): Unit = mailbox.add(task)

  /** Called on the network thread at the start of every received packet. */
  def drain(): Unit =
    if !mailbox.isEmpty then
      var t = mailbox.poll()
      while t != null do
        t.run()
        t = mailbox.poll()
    clock.runDue()

  /**
   * Sends one of our transactions, if the connection is in the play state.
   * Dispatches through [[ServerProtocol.current]] (`PING` on 1.17+, window-0 `WINDOW_CONFIRMATION`
   * on `< 1.17`) without per-call version branching.
   */
  def sendTransaction(): Unit =
    if user.getEncoderState == ConnectionState.PLAY then
      ServerProtocol.current.sendTransaction(user, clock.nextId())

  /**
   * Schedules a single trailing transaction after `event` is sent (e.g. `JOIN_GAME`).
   * Idempotent for a given `PacketSendEvent` across Eigen and ShardCompat.
   */
  def sendAfter(event: PacketSendEvent): Unit =
    if event eq null then sendTransaction()
    else if !(event eq lastAfterEvent) then
      lastAfterEvent = event
      event.getTasksAfterSend.add(() => sendTransaction())

  /**
   * Transaction before `event` (without a trailing transaction). Shared across listeners firing
   * on the same `PacketSendEvent` so Eigen and ShardCompat send only one ping.
   */
  def preTransaction(event: PacketSendEvent): Int =
    if (event ne null) && (event eq lastPreEvent) then lastPreId
    else
      sendTransaction()
      val id = clock.lastSent
      if event ne null then
        lastPreEvent = event
        lastPreId = id
      id

  /**
   * Transaction before the packet being sent; another goes out right after it.
   * Idempotent for a given `PacketSendEvent` so Eigen and ShardCompat share the same bracket.
   */
  def bracket(event: PacketSendEvent): Int =
    if (event ne null) && (event eq lastBracketEvent) then lastBracketId
    else
      sendTransaction()
      val id = clock.lastSent
      if event ne null then
        lastBracketEvent = event
        lastBracketId = id
        sendAfter(event)
      id

  /**
   * Transaction that orders a movement of `entityId`. Two moves of one entity must not share a
   * transaction, or the second would be applied before the first settled.
   */
  def movementTransaction(entityId: Int): Int =
    val last = lastMove.getOrNull(entityId)
    if last != null && last.transaction == clock.lastSent then sendTransaction()
    val id = clock.lastSent
    if last != null then last.transaction = id else lastMove(entityId) = LastMove(id)
    id

  /**
   * Transaction that orders a spawn of `entityId`: if `entityId` was despawned at the current
   * `lastSent`, forces a fresh transaction so `destroyTrans + 1` (which removes the old entity)
   * does not erase the newly spawned entity.
   */
  def spawnTransaction(entityId: Int): Int =
    lastMove.remove(entityId)
    if despawned.getOrNull(entityId) != null then
      if clock.lastSent <= despawnedAtSent then sendTransaction()
      despawned.clear()
    clock.lastSent

  /** Marks `entityId` despawned at the current `lastSent` and clears its movement state. */
  def noteDespawned(entityId: Int): Unit =
    lastMove.remove(entityId)
    despawned(entityId) = java.lang.Boolean.TRUE
    despawnedAtSent = clock.lastSent

  private final class LastMove(var transaction: Int)

  /** Bucketed transaction round-trip time, for the `ping_bucket` meta column. */
  def pingBucket: Int =
    val rtt = clock.rtt
    if rtt < 50 then 0 else if rtt < 100 then 1 else if rtt < 150 then 2 else if rtt < 250 then 3 else 4

```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/net/EntityCatalog.scala`:

/*

package eigen.paper.net

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.entity.`type`.{EntityType, EntityTypes}

import eigen.core.entity.{ClientProfile, EntityKind, TrackedEntity}

/**
 * Hitbox sizes and interpolation steps per entity type, as the client uses them. Sizes follow
 * vanilla's entity dimensions; derived from Shard's BoundingBoxSize (GPL-3.0).
 */
object EntityCatalog:

  private final case class Dims(width: Float, height: Float)

  private val dims: Map[String, Dims] =
    def d(w: Float, h: Float, names: String*) = names.map(_ -> Dims(w, h))
    Seq(
      d(0.6f, 1.8f, "player"),
      d(0.6f, 1.95f, "zombie", "zombie_villager", "husk", "drowned", "skeleton", "stray", "bogged",
        "piglin", "piglin_brute", "zombified_piglin", "villager", "wandering_trader", "vindicator",
        "pillager", "evoker", "illusioner", "witch"),
      d(0.6f, 1.8f, "blaze"),
      d(0.6f, 2.9f, "enderman"),
      d(0.6f, 1.77f, "breeze"),
      d(0.7f, 2.4f, "wither_skeleton"),
      d(1.4f, 0.9f, "spider"),
      d(0.7f, 0.5f, "cave_spider"),
      d(0.6f, 1.7f, "creeper"),
      d(1.4f, 2.7f, "iron_golem"),
      d(0.7f, 1.9f, "snow_golem"),
      d(1.95f, 2.2f, "ravager"),
      d(4.0f, 4.0f, "ghast"),
      d(4.0f, 4.5f, "happy_ghast"),
      d(16.0f, 8.0f, "ender_dragon"),
      d(0.9f, 3.5f, "wither"),
      d(0.9f, 2.9f, "warden"),
      d(0.85f, 0.85f, "guardian"),
      d(1.9975f, 1.9975f, "elder_guardian"),
      d(0.6f, 0.85f, "wolf"),
      d(0.9f, 1.4f, "cow", "mooshroom"),
      d(0.9f, 1.3f, "sheep", "goat"),
      d(0.9f, 0.9f, "pig"),
      d(0.9f, 1.875f, "llama", "trader_llama"),
      d(0.9f, 0.6f, "dolphin"),
      d(0.9f, 1.7f, "strider"),
      d(1.3964844f, 1.6f, "horse", "donkey", "mule", "skeleton_horse", "zombie_horse"),
      d(1.3964844f, 1.4f, "hoglin", "zoglin"),
      d(1.3f, 1.25f, "panda"),
      d(0.4f, 0.7f, "chicken"),
      d(0.4f, 0.3f, "endermite", "silverfish"),
      d(0.4f, 0.8f, "vex"),
      d(0.4f, 0.5f, "rabbit"),
      d(0.7f, 0.6f, "bee"),
      d(0.5f, 0.9f, "bat", "parrot"),
      d(0.5f, 0.3f, "cod"),
      d(0.5f, 0.4f, "tropical_fish"),
      d(0.75f, 0.42f, "axolotl"),
      d(0.5f, 0.55f, "frog"),
      d(0.5f, 1.975f, "armor_stand"),
      d(2.0f, 2.0f, "end_crystal"),
      d(0.9f, 0.5f, "phantom"),
    ).flatten.toMap

  private val Fallback = Dims(0.6f, 1.95f)
  private val Boat = Dims(1.375f, 0.5625f)

  private def name(t: EntityType): String = t.getName.getKey

  def kind(t: EntityType): EntityKind =
    if t == EntityTypes.PLAYER then EntityKind.Player
    else if t == EntityTypes.END_CRYSTAL then EntityKind.Crystal
    else if EntityTypes.isTypeInstanceOf(t, EntityTypes.LIVINGENTITY) then EntityKind.Living
    else EntityKind.Other

  /** Interpolation steps the client uses for this type. */
  def steps(t: EntityType, modernBoats: Boolean): Int =
    if EntityTypes.isTypeInstanceOf(t, EntityTypes.BOAT) then
      if modernBoats then TrackedEntity.LivingSteps else TrackedEntity.LegacyBoatSteps
    else if EntityTypes.isTypeInstanceOf(t, EntityTypes.MINECART_ABSTRACT) then TrackedEntity.MinecartSteps
    else if t == EntityTypes.SHULKER then 1
    else if EntityTypes.isTypeInstanceOf(t, EntityTypes.LIVINGENTITY) then TrackedEntity.LivingSteps
    else 1

  /**
   * Applies the hitbox size for the entity's current state. `crouching` and `scale` come from
   * metadata and attributes; `profile` decides how this client sizes a crouching player.
   */
  def resize(e: TrackedEntity, t: EntityType, profile: ClientProfile, crouching: Boolean, lowPose: Boolean, scale: Float): Unit =
    val base =
      if EntityTypes.isTypeInstanceOf(t, EntityTypes.BOAT) then Boat
      else dims.getOrElse(name(t), Fallback)
    val height =
      if t != EntityTypes.PLAYER then base.height
      else if lowPose then 0.6f
      else if crouching then profile.crouchHeight
      else base.height
    e.width = base.width * scale
    e.height = height * scale
