`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/net/Inbound.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.net

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.{PacketListenerAbstract, PacketListenerPriority, PacketReceiveEvent, UserDisconnectEvent, UserLoginEvent}
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.packettype.PacketType
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.player.{DiggingAction, InteractionHand}
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.configuration.client.WrapperConfigClientPluginMessage
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.client.*

import eigen.paper.collect.Collector

/**
 * Client → server packets. Runs on the connection's network thread at the lowest priority, so
 * it sees every packet the client sent even if another plugin later cancels it. Only our own
 * transaction answers are cancelled.
 *
 * Dispatch is an O(1) array lookup indexed by `PacketType.Play.Client.ordinal` via [[PacketTable]],
 * with the server-version-specific transaction packet (`PONG` on 1.17+, `WINDOW_CONFIRMATION` on
 * `< 1.17`) bound once at construction time through [[ServerProtocol]].
 */
final class Inbound(players: Players, collector: Collector) extends PacketListenerAbstract(PacketListenerPriority.LOWEST):
  import Inbound.Handler
  import PacketType.Play.Client as C

  private val protocol: ServerProtocol = ServerProtocol.current
  private val flyingHandler: Handler = (p, event) =>
    val f = WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying(event)
    val loc = f.getLocation
    prepareTick(p)
    p.sim.onFlying(f.hasPositionChanged, loc.getX, loc.getY, loc.getZ, f.hasRotationChanged, loc.getYaw, loc.getPitch, f.isOnGround)

  private val table: PacketTable[C, Handler] = PacketTable.build(C.values)(
    protocol.inboundTransactionType -> ((p, event) =>
      val id = protocol.readAckId(event)
      if id != 0 && p.clock.onPong(id) then event.setCancelled(true)
    ),
    C.CLIENT_TICK_END -> ((p, _) => { prepareTick(p); p.sim.onTickEnd() }),
    C.PLAYER_INPUT -> ((p, event) =>
      val i = WrapperPlayClientPlayerInput(event)
      p.sim.onInput(i.isForward, i.isBackward, i.isLeft, i.isRight, i.isJump, i.isShift, i.isSprint)
    ),
    C.INTERACT_ENTITY -> ((p, event) =>
      val i = WrapperPlayClientInteractEntity(event)
      if i.getAction == WrapperPlayClientInteractEntity.InteractAction.ATTACK then p.sim.onAttack(i.getEntityId)
    ),
    C.ATTACK -> ((p, event) => p.sim.onAttack(WrapperPlayClientAttack(event).getEntityId)),
    C.ANIMATION -> ((p, event) =>
      if WrapperPlayClientAnimation(event).getHand == InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND then p.sim.onSwing()
    ),
    C.USE_ITEM -> ((p, _) => p.sim.onUse()),
    C.PLAYER_BLOCK_PLACEMENT -> ((p, _) => p.sim.onPlace()),
    C.PLAYER_DIGGING -> ((p, event) =>
      if WrapperPlayClientPlayerDigging(event).getAction == DiggingAction.START_DIGGING then p.sim.onDig()
    ),
    C.HELD_ITEM_CHANGE -> ((p, event) => p.sim.onSlot(WrapperPlayClientHeldItemChange(event).getSlot)),
    C.ENTITY_ACTION -> ((p, event) => entityAction(p, WrapperPlayClientEntityAction(event).getAction)),
    C.CLICK_WINDOW -> ((p, _) => p.sim.onGui(true)),
    C.CLOSE_WINDOW -> ((p, _) => p.sim.onGui(false)),
    C.PLUGIN_MESSAGE -> ((_, event) =>
      val m = WrapperPlayClientPluginMessage(event)
      brand(event, m.getChannelName, m.getData)
    ),
    C.PLAYER_FLYING -> flyingHandler,
    C.PLAYER_POSITION -> flyingHandler,
    C.PLAYER_ROTATION -> flyingHandler,
    C.PLAYER_POSITION_AND_ROTATION -> flyingHandler,
  )

  override def onUserLogin(event: UserLoginEvent): Unit =
    val p = players.get(event.getUser)
    if p != null then
      val bukkit: org.bukkit.entity.Player = event.getPlayer
      players.announce(p, if bukkit != null then bukkit.getName else event.getUser.getName)

  override def onUserDisconnect(event: UserDisconnectEvent): Unit =
    // Finish any recording while the player is still tracked; the disconnect removes it.
    collector.onQuit(event.getUser.getUUID)
    val _ = players.disconnect(event.getUser)

  override def onPacketReceive(event: PacketReceiveEvent): Unit =
    val tpe = event.getPacketType
    if tpe == PacketType.Configuration.Client.PLUGIN_MESSAGE then
      val message = WrapperConfigClientPluginMessage(event)
      brand(event, message.getChannelName, message.getData)
    else
      val p = players.get(event.getUser)
      if p != null then
        p.drain()
        val h = table.get(tpe)
        if h != null then h.handle(p, event)

  private def entityAction(p: EigenPlayer, action: WrapperPlayClientEntityAction.Action): Unit =
    import WrapperPlayClientEntityAction.Action
    action match
      case Action.START_SPRINTING => p.sim.onSprint(true)
      case Action.STOP_SPRINTING => p.sim.onSprint(false)
      case Action.START_SNEAKING => p.sim.onSneak(true)
      case Action.STOP_SNEAKING => p.sim.onSneak(false)
      case _ => ()

  /** Per-tick inputs the simulation reads from outside. */
  private def prepareTick(p: EigenPlayer): Unit =
    p.sim.windowsWanted = players.wantsWindows(p)
    p.sim.pingBucket = p.pingBucket

  /** A brand is a length-prefixed string; brands are short, so the prefix is a single byte. */
  private def brand(event: PacketReceiveEvent, channel: String, data: Array[Byte]): Unit =
    if (channel == "minecraft:brand" || channel == "MC|Brand") && data.length > 1 && data.length < 256 then
      val text = String(data, 1, data.length - 1, UTF_8).replace(" (Velocity)", "")
      players.rememberBrand(event.getUser, text.take(64))

object Inbound:
  @FunctionalInterface
  trait Handler:
    def handle(p: EigenPlayer, event: PacketReceiveEvent): Unit

```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/net/Outbound.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.net

import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.{PacketListenerAbstract, PacketListenerPriority, PacketSendEvent}
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.entity.data.EntityData
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.entity.pose.EntityPose
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.entity.`type`.{EntityType, EntityTypes}
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.item.ItemStack
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.packettype.PacketType
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.player.ClientVersion
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.teleport.RelativeFlag
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.util.Vector3d
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.server.*

import eigen.core.entity.TrackedEntity
import eigen.core.sim.{ItemKind, PendingRotation, PendingTeleport}

/**
 * Server → client packets. Runs at the highest priority, ignoring cancelled sends so the
 * simulation only sees packets that really leave the server. Anything that changes simulation
 * state is queued on the player's [[eigen.core.latency.TransactionClock]] and therefore runs on
 * the receive path once the client has acknowledged the surrounding transaction.
 *
 * Dispatch is an O(1) array lookup indexed by `PacketType.Play.Server.ordinal` via [[PacketTable]],
 * with the server-version-specific transaction packet (`PING` on 1.17+, `WINDOW_CONFIRMATION` on
 * `< 1.17`) and living-entity metadata index resolved once at construction time through [[ServerProtocol]].
 */
final class Outbound(players: Players) extends PacketListenerAbstract(PacketListenerPriority.HIGHEST):
  import Outbound.*
  import PacketType.Play.Server as S

  private val protocol: ServerProtocol = ServerProtocol.current
  private val livingIdx: Int = protocol.livingFlagsIndex

  private val table: PacketTable[S, Handler] = PacketTable.build(S.values)(
    protocol.outboundTransactionType -> ((p, event) =>
      val id = protocol.readSentId(event)
      if id != 0 then p.clock.onPingSent(id)
    ),
    S.SPAWN_ENTITY -> ((p, event) =>
      val w = WrapperPlayServerSpawnEntity(event)
      spawn(p, w.getEntityId, w.getEntityType, w.getPosition)
    ),
    S.SPAWN_LIVING_ENTITY -> ((p, event) =>
      val w = WrapperPlayServerSpawnLivingEntity(event)
      spawn(p, w.getEntityId, w.getEntityType, w.getPosition)
    ),
    S.SPAWN_PLAYER -> ((p, event) =>
      val w = WrapperPlayServerSpawnPlayer(event)
      spawn(p, w.getEntityId, EntityTypes.PLAYER, w.getPosition)
    ),
    S.DESTROY_ENTITIES -> ((p, event) => destroy(p, WrapperPlayServerDestroyEntities(event).getEntityIds)),
    S.ENTITY_RELATIVE_MOVE -> ((p, event) =>
      val w = WrapperPlayServerEntityRelativeMove(event)
      move(p, w.getEntityId, relative = true, hasPos = true, w.getDeltaX, w.getDeltaY, w.getDeltaZ)
    ),
    S.ENTITY_RELATIVE_MOVE_AND_ROTATION -> ((p, event) =>
      val w = WrapperPlayServerEntityRelativeMoveAndRotation(event)
      move(p, w.getEntityId, relative = true, hasPos = true, w.getDeltaX, w.getDeltaY, w.getDeltaZ)
    ),
    S.ENTITY_TELEPORT -> ((p, event) =>
      val w = WrapperPlayServerEntityTeleport(event)
      absolute(p, w.getEntityId, w.getValues.getPosition)
    ),
    S.ENTITY_POSITION_SYNC -> ((p, event) =>
      val w = WrapperPlayServerEntityPositionSync(event)
      absolute(p, w.getId, w.getValues.getPosition)
    ),
    S.ENTITY_ROTATION -> ((p, event) =>
      move(p, WrapperPlayServerEntityRotation(event).getEntityId, relative = true, hasPos = false, 0, 0, 0)
    ),
    S.ENTITY_STATUS -> ((p, event) => deathStatus(p, event, WrapperPlayServerEntityStatus(event))),
    S.ENTITY_METADATA -> ((p, event) => metadata(p, WrapperPlayServerEntityMetadata(event))),
    S.UPDATE_ATTRIBUTES -> ((p, event) => attributes(p, event, WrapperPlayServerUpdateAttributes(event))),
    S.ENTITY_VELOCITY -> ((p, event) =>
      if WrapperPlayServerEntityVelocity(event).getEntityId == p.sim.selfId then knockback(p, event)
    ),
    S.EXPLOSION -> ((p, event) =>
      val motion = WrapperPlayServerExplosion(event).getKnockback
      if motion != null && (motion.getX != 0 || motion.getY != 0 || motion.getZ != 0) then knockback(p, event)
    ),
    S.PLAYER_POSITION_AND_LOOK -> ((p, event) => teleport(p, event, WrapperPlayServerPlayerPositionAndLook(event))),
    S.PLAYER_ROTATION -> ((p, event) => serverRotation(p, event, WrapperPlayServerPlayerRotation(event))),
    S.JOIN_GAME -> ((p, event) => joinGame(p, event, WrapperPlayServerJoinGame(event).getEntityId)),
    S.RESPAWN -> ((p, event) =>
      p.clock.at(p.bracket(event))(() => { p.sim.breakEpoch(); p.entityMeta.clear() })
    ),
    S.SET_PASSENGERS -> ((p, event) =>
      val w = WrapperPlayServerSetPassengers(event)
      val vehicle = w.getEntityId
      val riding = w.getPassengers.contains(p.sim.selfId)
      p.clock.at(p.bracket(event))(() => vehicleChange(p, vehicle, riding))
    ),
    S.OPEN_WINDOW -> ((p, _) => p.clock.at(p.clock.lastSent)(() => p.sim.onGui(true))),
    S.CLOSE_WINDOW -> ((p, _) => p.clock.at(p.clock.lastSent)(() => p.sim.onGui(false))),
    S.HELD_ITEM_CHANGE -> ((p, event) =>
      val slot = WrapperPlayServerHeldItemChange(event).getSlot
      p.clock.at(p.clock.lastSent)(() => p.sim.onSlot(slot))
    ),
    S.SET_SLOT -> ((p, event) =>
      val w = WrapperPlayServerSetSlot(event)
      if w.getWindowId == 0 then inventorySlot(p, w.getSlot, w.getItem)
    ),
    S.WINDOW_ITEMS -> ((p, event) =>
      val w = WrapperPlayServerWindowItems(event)
      if w.getWindowId == 0 then
        val items = w.getItems.asScala.toIndexedSeq
        for slot <- FirstHotbarSlot to OffhandSlot if slot < items.size do inventorySlot(p, slot, items(slot))
    ),
    S.SET_PLAYER_INVENTORY -> ((p, event) =>
      val w = WrapperPlayServerSetPlayerInventory(event)
      // Player inventory indices: 0..8 hotbar, 40 offhand.
      val slot = w.getSlot
      if slot >= 0 && slot < 9 then inventorySlot(p, FirstHotbarSlot + slot, w.getStack)
      else if slot == PlayerOffhandIndex then inventorySlot(p, OffhandSlot, w.getStack)
    ),
  )

  override def onPacketSend(event: PacketSendEvent): Unit =
    if event.isCancelled then return
    val tpe = event.getPacketType
    if tpe == PacketType.Login.Server.LOGIN_SUCCESS then
      val user = event.getUser
      event.getTasksAfterSend.add(() => { val _ = players.connect(user) })
    else
      val p = players.get(event.getUser)
      if p != null then
        val h = table.get(tpe)
        if h != null then h.handle(p, event)

  private def deathStatus(p: EigenPlayer, event: PacketSendEvent, w: WrapperPlayServerEntityStatus): Unit =
    if w.getStatus == DeathStatus then
      val id = w.getEntityId
      p.clock.at(p.bracket(event)): () =>
        val e = p.sim.entity(id)
        if e != null then e.dead = true

  private def knockback(p: EigenPlayer, event: PacketSendEvent): Unit =
    p.clock.at(p.bracket(event))(() => p.sim.onKnockback())

  private def teleport(p: EigenPlayer, event: PacketSendEvent, w: WrapperPlayServerPlayerPositionAndLook): Unit =
    val f = w.getRelativeFlags
    val pending = PendingTeleport(
      w.getX, w.getY, w.getZ, w.getYaw, w.getPitch,
      f.has(RelativeFlag.X), f.has(RelativeFlag.Y), f.has(RelativeFlag.Z), f.has(RelativeFlag.YAW), f.has(RelativeFlag.PITCH),
    )
    p.clock.at(p.bracket(event))(() => p.sim.expectTeleport(pending))

  private def serverRotation(p: EigenPlayer, event: PacketSendEvent, w: WrapperPlayServerPlayerRotation): Unit =
    val pitch = if w.isRelativePitch then w.getPitch else math.max(-90f, math.min(90f, w.getPitch % 360f))
    val rotation = PendingRotation(w.getYaw, pitch, w.isRelativeYaw, w.isRelativePitch)
    p.clock.at(p.preTransaction(event))(() => p.sim.expectRotation(rotation))

  private def joinGame(p: EigenPlayer, event: PacketSendEvent, self: Int): Unit =
    p.clock.at(p.clock.lastSent)(() => { p.sim.breakEpoch(); p.sim.selfId = self; p.entityMeta.clear() })
    p.sendAfter(event)

  private def spawn(p: EigenPlayer, id: Int, tpe: EntityType, pos: Vector3d): Unit =
    if id < 0 then return
    val t = p.spawnTransaction(id)
    val modernBoats = p.user.getClientVersion.isNewerThanOrEquals(ClientVersion.V_1_21_2)
    val e = TrackedEntity(id, EntityCatalog.kind(tpe), EntityCatalog.steps(tpe, modernBoats), p.profile)
    e.spawnAt(pos.getX, pos.getY, pos.getZ)
    val meta = EntityMeta(tpe)
    EntityCatalog.resize(e, tpe, p.profile, crouching = false, lowPose = false, scale = 1f)
    p.clock.at(t)(() => { p.entityMeta(id) = meta; p.sim.addEntity(e) })

  private def destroy(p: EigenPlayer, ids: Array[Int]): Unit =
    // Transaction bookkeeping is touched only from the sending thread.
    ids.foreach(p.noteDespawned)
    val t = p.clock.lastSent
    p.clock.at(t): () =>
      ids.foreach: id =>
        val e = p.sim.entity(id)
        if e != null then e.dead = true
    p.clock.at(t + 1): () =>
      ids.foreach: id =>
        p.sim.removeEntity(id)
        p.entityMeta.remove(id)

  private def absolute(p: EigenPlayer, id: Int, pos: Vector3d): Unit =
    move(p, id, relative = false, hasPos = true, pos.getX, pos.getY, pos.getZ)

  private def move(p: EigenPlayer, id: Int, relative: Boolean, hasPos: Boolean, x: Double, y: Double, z: Double): Unit =
    if id == p.sim.selfId then return
    val t = p.movementTransaction(id)
    p.clock.at(t): () =>
      val e = p.sim.entity(id)
      if e != null then e.onFirstTransaction(relative, hasPos, x, y, z)
    p.clock.at(t + 1): () =>
      val e = p.sim.entity(id)
      if e != null then e.onSecondTransaction()

  private def metadata(p: EigenPlayer, w: WrapperPlayServerEntityMetadata): Unit =
    val id = w.getEntityId
    val self = id == p.sim.selfId
    val entries = w.getEntityMetadata
    val n = entries.size
    // Almost every metadata packet carries nothing the simulation tracks; filtering before
    // scheduling keeps those packets free of collection conversion, task and closure.
    var i = 0
    var relevant = false
    while !relevant && i < n do
      relevant = isRelevantMeta(self, entries.get(i))
      i += 1
    if !relevant then return
    val kept = new java.util.ArrayList[EntityData[?]](2)
    i = 0
    while i < n do
      val d = entries.get(i)
      if isRelevantMeta(self, d) then kept.add(d)
      i += 1
    p.clock.at(p.clock.lastSent): () =>
      var j = 0
      while j < kept.size do
        applyMeta(p, id, self, kept.get(j))
        j += 1

  private def isRelevantMeta(self: Boolean, d: EntityData[?]): Boolean =
    val v = d.getValue
    if v.isInstanceOf[java.lang.Byte] then self && (d.getIndex == 0 || d.getIndex == livingIdx)
    else v.isInstanceOf[EntityPose] && !self

  private def applyMeta(p: EigenPlayer, id: Int, self: Boolean, d: EntityData[?]): Unit =
    (d.getIndex, d.getValue) match
      case (0, flags: java.lang.Byte) if self =>
        p.sim.onSelfFlags(gliding = (flags & 0x80) != 0, swimming = (flags & 0x10) != 0)
      case (i, flags: java.lang.Byte) if self && i == livingIdx =>
        p.sim.onSelfLivingFlags(riptide = (flags & 0x04) != 0)
      case (_, pose: EntityPose) if !self =>
        val meta = p.entityMeta.getOrNull(id)
        val e = p.sim.entity(id)
        if meta != null && e != null then
          meta.crouching = pose == EntityPose.CROUCHING
          meta.lowPose = pose == EntityPose.SWIMMING || pose == EntityPose.FALL_FLYING || pose == EntityPose.SPIN_ATTACK
          EntityCatalog.resize(e, meta.tpe, p.profile, meta.crouching, meta.lowPose, meta.scale)
      case _ => ()

  private def attributes(p: EigenPlayer, event: PacketSendEvent, w: WrapperPlayServerUpdateAttributes): Unit =
    val id = w.getEntityId
    val self = id == p.sim.selfId
    val props = w.getProperties
    val n = props.size
    // Most attribute packets (armour, luck, ...) touch nothing tracked here; skipping them
    // also skips the bracket transactions a self packet would otherwise cost.
    var i = 0
    var relevant = false
    while !relevant && i < n do
      relevant = isRelevantAttribute(self, props.get(i).getAttribute.getName.getKey)
      i += 1
    if !relevant then return
    val values = new java.util.ArrayList[(String, Double)](2)
    i = 0
    while i < n do
      val prop = props.get(i)
      val key = prop.getAttribute.getName.getKey
      if isRelevantAttribute(self, key) then values.add((key, prop.calcValue()))
      i += 1
    val t = if self then p.bracket(event) else p.clock.lastSent
    p.clock.at(t): () =>
      var j = 0
      while j < values.size do
        val (key, value) = values.get(j)
        applyAttribute(p, id, self, key, value)
        j += 1

  private def isRelevantAttribute(self: Boolean, key: String): Boolean =
    key.endsWith("scale") || (self && (key.endsWith("attack_speed") || key.endsWith("entity_interaction_range")))

  private def applyAttribute(p: EigenPlayer, id: Int, self: Boolean, key: String, value: Double): Unit =
    if key.endsWith("scale") then
      if self then p.sim.onSelfScale(value.toFloat)
      else
        val meta = p.entityMeta.getOrNull(id)
        val e = p.sim.entity(id)
        if meta != null && e != null then
          meta.scale = value.toFloat
          EntityCatalog.resize(e, meta.tpe, p.profile, meta.crouching, meta.lowPose, meta.scale)
    else if self && key.endsWith("attack_speed") then p.sim.onAttackSpeed(value)
    else if self && key.endsWith("entity_interaction_range") then p.sim.onInteractionRange(value)

  private def vehicleChange(p: EigenPlayer, vehicle: Int, riding: Boolean): Unit =
    if riding then p.sim.onVehicle(true)
    else if p.sim.body.inVehicle && p.sim.entity(vehicle) != null then p.sim.onVehicle(false)

  /** Stores an item in the simulation's inventory mirror; `slot` is a window-0 slot index. */
  private def inventorySlot(p: EigenPlayer, slot: Int, item: ItemStack): Unit =
    val kind = if item == null || item.isEmpty then ItemKind.Empty else ItemKind.of(item.getType.getName.toString)
    p.clock.at(p.clock.lastSent): () =>
      if slot >= FirstHotbarSlot && slot < OffhandSlot then p.sim.inventory.hotbar(slot - FirstHotbarSlot) = kind
      else if slot == OffhandSlot then p.sim.inventory.offhand = kind

object Outbound:
  @FunctionalInterface
  trait Handler:
    def handle(p: EigenPlayer, event: PacketSendEvent): Unit

  private inline val DeathStatus = 3
  /** Window-0 slot of the first hotbar slot; the hotbar spans nine slots. */
  private inline val FirstHotbarSlot = 36
  /** Window-0 offhand slot. */
  private inline val OffhandSlot = 45
  /** Player-inventory index of the offhand, as used by `SET_PLAYER_INVENTORY`. */
  private inline val PlayerOffhandIndex = 40

```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/net/Players.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.net

import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.util.{HexFormat, UUID}
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger

import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.player.User
import javax.crypto.Mac
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec

import eigen.core.history.Window
import eigen.core.sim.{Handoff, WindowSystem}
import eigen.core.wire.Messages
import eigen.paper.collect.LocalSink
import eigen.paper.config.Settings
import eigen.paper.garnet.GarnetBridge
import eigen.paper.stream.StreamClient

/**
 * Registry of connected players and the router for the windows their simulations produce.
 * Lookups are concurrent; each [[EigenPlayer]] is otherwise owned by its network thread.
 */
final class Players(settings: Settings, stream: StreamClient, local: LocalSink, garnet: GarnetBridge):
  private val byUser = ConcurrentHashMap[User, EigenPlayer]()
  private val byUuid = ConcurrentHashMap[UUID, EigenPlayer]()
  private val byHandle = ConcurrentHashMap[Int, EigenPlayer]()
  private val pendingBrands = ConcurrentHashMap[User, String]()
  private val handles = AtomicInteger(0)
  private val macSpec = SecretKeySpec(settings.salt, "HmacSHA256")

  @volatile var onAnnounced: EigenPlayer => Unit = _ => ()

  def get(user: User): EigenPlayer | Null = byUser.get(user)
  def get(uuid: UUID): EigenPlayer | Null = byUuid.get(uuid)
  def byHandleOrNull(handle: Int): EigenPlayer | Null = byHandle.get(handle)
  def all: Iterable[EigenPlayer] = byUser.values().asScala

  /** Creates the player's state when its connection enters the game. */
  def connect(user: User): EigenPlayer =
    val p = byUser.computeIfAbsent(user, newPlayer)
    // The name is known now; announcing here (not only on the login event) makes it reliable.
    announce(p, user.getName)
    p

  private def newPlayer(user: User): EigenPlayer =
    val p = EigenPlayer(user, user.getUUID, handles.incrementAndGet(), Profiles.of(user.getClientVersion), pseudonymOf(user.getUUID), settings.sim, route)
    val brand = pendingBrands.remove(user)
    if brand != null then p.brand = brand
    p.exempt = settings.exemptBedrock && isBedrock(user.getUUID)
    byUuid.put(p.uuid, p)
    byHandle.put(p.handle, p)
    p

  /** Announces the player to the backend once its name and brand are known. */
  def announce(p: EigenPlayer, name: String): Unit =
    p.name = name
    if !p.announced then
      val pseudo = p.pseudonym
      stream.playerJoined(Messages.PlayerJoin(p.handle, pseudo, p.profile.protocol, p.brand))
      p.announced = true
      if !p.exempt && garnet.enabled then
        garnet.loadHandoff(pseudo): bytes =>
          p.post(() => { val _ = Handoff.restore(p.sim, bytes) })
      onAnnounced(p)

  def disconnect(user: User): EigenPlayer | Null =
    pendingBrands.remove(user)
    val p = byUser.remove(user)
    if p != null then
      // No further packets arrive, so the mailbox is drained here: every task a command
      // posted (a session start or stop) still runs on the connection's own thread.
      p.drain()
      if !p.exempt then
        if wantsWindows(p) && p.sim.calm.length >= settings.sim.calmMinTicks then
          WindowSystem.flushCalm(p.sim)
        if garnet.enabled then
          garnet.saveHandoff(p.pseudonym, Handoff.encode(p.sim))
      byUuid.remove(p.uuid, p)
      byHandle.remove(p.handle, p)
      if p.announced then stream.playerLeft(p.handle)
    p

  def rememberBrand(user: User, brand: String): Unit =
    val p = byUser.get(user)
    if p != null then p.brand = brand else pendingBrands.put(user, brand)

  /** Whether windows are wanted for this player right now (checked every tick). */
  def wantsWindows(p: EigenPlayer): Boolean =
    p.announced && !p.exempt && (p.session != null || (stream.enabled && stream.inference))

  /** Routes a finished window: to the backend for collection and/or inference, and to disk. */
  private def route(p: EigenPlayer, w: Window): Unit =
    val s = p.session
    val collect = s != null
    val infer = stream.enabled && stream.inference
    w.handle = p.handle
    if collect then
      w.session = s.nn.id
      s.nn.windows.incrementAndGet()
      if settings.sink.local then local.write(s.nn, w)
    val purpose =
      (if collect && settings.sink.server then Window.PurposeCollect else 0) |
        (if infer then Window.PurposeInfer else 0)
    if purpose != 0 then
      w.purpose = purpose
      stream.window(w)

  /** Stable pseudonymous id: HMAC-SHA256 of the UUID under this server's secret salt. */
  private def pseudonymOf(uuid: UUID): String =
    val mac = Mac.getInstance("HmacSHA256")
    mac.init(macSpec)
    val bytes = ByteBuffer.allocate(16).putLong(uuid.getMostSignificantBits).putLong(uuid.getLeastSignificantBits).array()
    HexFormat.of().formatHex(mac.doFinal(bytes))

  /** Floodgate gives Bedrock players UUIDs with all-zero high bits. */
  private def isBedrock(uuid: UUID): Boolean = uuid.getMostSignificantBits == 0L
```

```scala
/*

package eigen.paper.net

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.player.ClientVersion
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.player.ClientVersion.*

import eigen.core.entity.ClientProfile

/** Maps a client's protocol version to the behaviour the simulation must mirror. */
object Profiles:

  def of(v: ClientVersion): ClientProfile =
    inline def atLeast(o: ClientVersion) = v.isNewerThanOrEquals(o)
    inline def below(o: ClientVersion) = v.isOlderThan(o)
    val tickEnd = atLeast(V_1_21_2)
    ClientProfile(
      protocol = v.getProtocolVersion,
      tickEnd = tickEnd,
      realInput = tickEnd,
      skipsIdleTicks = !tickEnd && atLeast(V_1_9),
      duplicateFlying = atLeast(V_1_17) && below(V_1_21),
      floorPacking = below(V_1_19_3),
      coarsePositions = below(V_1_9),
      rotationFreezesInterpolation =
        (v.isNewerThan(V_1_14_4) && below(V_1_20_2)) || (v.isNewerThan(V_1_21_4) && below(V_1_21_9)),
      doubleIndexTrig = atLeast(V_1_21_11),
      lookCandidates = if below(V_1_9) then 2 else 3,
      sneakEyeHeight = if below(V_1_14) then 1.54 else 1.27,
      crouchHeight = if below(V_1_14) then 1.8f else 1.5f,
      movementThreshold = if below(V_1_18_2) then 0.03 else 0.0002,
    )
```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/net/ServerProtocol.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Portions of this file are derived from the following projects, also licensed under
 * the GNU General Public License version 3:
 *   GrimAC - https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim
 *            Copyright (c) 2021-2026 GrimAC, DefineOutside and contributors
 *   Shard  - https://github.com/KaelusAI/Shard
 *            Copyright (C) 2026 KaelusAI
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.net

import scala.reflect.ClassTag

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.PacketEvents
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.{PacketReceiveEvent, PacketSendEvent}
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.manager.server.ServerVersion
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.packettype.{PacketType, PacketTypeCommon}
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.player.User
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.client.{WrapperPlayClientPong, WrapperPlayClientWindowConfirmation}
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.server.{WrapperPlayServerPing, WrapperPlayServerWindowConfirmation}

/**
 * Version-specialized protocol and transaction codec resolved once for the server process, plus
 * O(1) ordinal-indexed packet dispatch tables for `PacketType.Play`.
 *
 * Why this exists:
 *   - Scala 3 pattern matching on Java enums (`PacketType.Play.Client` / `Server`) compiles to a
 *     linear `if (tpe == X)` chain rather than a bytecode `tableswitch` (to preserve binary
 *     compatibility across enum edits). Indexing a dense array by `Enum.ordinal` turns every
 *     packet receive/send into a single bounds-checked array read + monomorphic/bimorphic call.
 *   - Server-version differences (`1.17+` `PING`/`PONG` vs `< 1.17` window-0 `WINDOW_CONFIRMATION`,
 *     living-entity metadata index, etc.) are bound once into a sealed [[ServerProtocol]]
 *     singleton so the hot packet path executes zero version branches.
 */
sealed trait ServerProtocol:
  /** Index of the living-entity flags byte in `ENTITY_METADATA`. */
  val livingFlagsIndex: Int

  /** Outbound packet type carrying server → client transactions on this server version. */
  val outboundTransactionType: PacketType.Play.Server

  /** Inbound packet type carrying client → server transaction answers on this server version. */
  val inboundTransactionType: PacketType.Play.Client

  /** Sends one transaction with wire `id` to `user`. */
  def sendTransaction(user: User, id: Short): Unit

  /** Extracts the wire transaction id from an outbound transaction packet, or `0` if unrelated. */
  def readSentId(event: PacketSendEvent): Int

  /** Extracts the wire transaction id from an inbound transaction answer, or `0` if unrelated. */
  def readAckId(event: PacketReceiveEvent): Int

object ServerProtocol:

  /** 1.17+ servers: `PLAY/PING` and `PLAY/PONG` with 32-bit id (we use positive 15-bit shorts). */
  case object ModernPing extends ServerProtocol:
    val livingFlagsIndex: Int = 8
    val outboundTransactionType: PacketType.Play.Server = PacketType.Play.Server.PING
    val inboundTransactionType: PacketType.Play.Client = PacketType.Play.Client.PONG

    def sendTransaction(user: User, id: Short): Unit =
      user.sendPacket(WrapperPlayServerPing(id.toInt))

    def readSentId(event: PacketSendEvent): Int =
      WrapperPlayServerPing(event).getId

    def readAckId(event: PacketReceiveEvent): Int =
      WrapperPlayClientPong(event).getId

  /** `< 1.17` servers: window-0 `WINDOW_CONFIRMATION` (`accepted = false` on send). */
  final class LegacyWindowConfirmation(val livingFlagsIndex: Int) extends ServerProtocol:
    val outboundTransactionType: PacketType.Play.Server = PacketType.Play.Server.WINDOW_CONFIRMATION
    val inboundTransactionType: PacketType.Play.Client = PacketType.Play.Client.WINDOW_CONFIRMATION

    def sendTransaction(user: User, id: Short): Unit =
      user.sendPacket(WrapperPlayServerWindowConfirmation(0, id, false))

    def readSentId(event: PacketSendEvent): Int =
      val w = WrapperPlayServerWindowConfirmation(event)
      if w.getWindowId == 0 && !w.isAccepted then w.getActionId.toInt else 0

    def readAckId(event: PacketReceiveEvent): Int =
      val w = WrapperPlayClientWindowConfirmation(event)
      if w.getWindowId == 0 then w.getActionId.toInt else 0

  /** Resolves the protocol strategy for a given `ServerVersion`. */
  def forVersion(server: ServerVersion): ServerProtocol =
    if server.isNewerThanOrEquals(ServerVersion.V_1_17) then ModernPing
    else
      val livingIdx =
        if server.isNewerThanOrEquals(ServerVersion.V_1_14) then 7
        else if server.isNewerThanOrEquals(ServerVersion.V_1_10) then 6
        else 5
      LegacyWindowConfirmation(livingIdx)

  /** Process-wide protocol strategy resolved from PacketEvents (defaults to [[ModernPing]] in unit tests). */
  lazy val current: ServerProtocol =
    try forVersion(PacketEvents.getAPI.getServerManager.getVersion)
    catch case _: Throwable => ModernPing

/**
 * Dense O(1) dispatch table indexed by `Enum.ordinal`, replacing linear `match` chains over
 * `PacketType.Play.Client` and `PacketType.Play.Server` with a single array lookup.
 */
final class PacketTable[K <: Enum[K], H >: Null <: AnyRef](
    private val handlers: Array[H | Null],
    private val runtimeClass: Class[K],
):
  /** Returns the handler registered for `tpe`, or `null` if `tpe` is not of `K` or unhandled. */
  inline def get(tpe: PacketTypeCommon): H | Null =
    if runtimeClass.isInstance(tpe) then
      val ord = tpe.asInstanceOf[K].ordinal
      if ord >= 0 && ord < handlers.length then handlers(ord) else null
    else null

object PacketTable:
  def build[K <: Enum[K]: ClassTag, H >: Null <: AnyRef: ClassTag](
      universe: Array[K]
  )(
      entries: (K, H)*
  ): PacketTable[K, H] =
    val cls = summon[ClassTag[K]].runtimeClass.asInstanceOf[Class[K]]
    val table = new Array[H | Null](universe.length)
    var i = 0
    while i < entries.length do
      val (k, h) = entries(i)
      table(k.ordinal) = h
      i += 1
    PacketTable(table, cls)

```

`paper/src/main/scala/eigen/paper/stream/StreamClient.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.stream

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.net.http.{HttpClient, WebSocket}
import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.security.SecureRandom
import java.time.Duration
import java.util.ArrayDeque
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.{AtomicInteger, AtomicLong}
import java.util.concurrent.{CompletionStage, ConcurrentHashMap, ConcurrentLinkedQueue, Executors, ScheduledExecutorService, TimeUnit}
import java.util.logging.{Level, Logger}

import scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer
import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*

import com.github.plokhotnyuk.jsoniter_scala.core.JsonValueCodec

import eigen.core.history.Window
import eigen.core.wire.{ByteSink, FrameCodec, MessageType, Messages}
import eigen.paper.config.BackendSettings

/** Connection state, for status output. */
enum StreamState:
  case Disabled, Disconnected, Connecting, Handshaking, Ready

/**
 * The plugin's side of the backend stream (protocol v1).
 *
 * Producers on any thread hand messages to a lock-free inbox. A single "eigen-stream" thread
 * owns everything else: it turns the inbox into numbered frames, keeps them until the server
 * acknowledges them, (re)connects with backoff and resends what the server has not applied.
 */
final class StreamClient(settings: BackendSettings, plugin: String, platform: String, log: Logger):
  import StreamClient.*

  private sealed trait Out
  private final case class Control(tpe: Int, body: Array[Byte]) extends Out
  private final case class Win(w: Window) extends Out
  private final class Frame(val seq: Long, val bytes: Array[Byte], val windows: Boolean)

  private val exec: ScheduledExecutorService = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor(r =>
    val t = Thread(r, "eigen-stream")
    t.setDaemon(true)
    t,
  )
  private lazy val http = HttpClient.newBuilder().connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10)).build()

  private val inbox = ConcurrentLinkedQueue[Out]()
  private val queuedWindows = AtomicInteger()
  private val droppedWindows = AtomicLong()

  // Snapshot of live state, replayed at the start of a new stream.
  private val players = ConcurrentHashMap[Int, Messages.PlayerJoin]()
  private val sessions = ConcurrentHashMap[Int, Messages.CollectStart]()

  // ---- owned by the stream thread ----
  private val codec = FrameCodec()
  private var streamId = newStreamId()
  private var nextSeq = 1L
  private var nextWindow = 1L
  private val unacked = ArrayDeque[Frame]()
  private var unackedBytes = 0L
  private var transmittedThrough = 0L
  private var socket: WebSocket | Null = null
  private var maxFrameBytes = 4 * 1024 * 1024
  private var backoffMs = MinBackoffMs
  private var reconnectAt = 0L
  private var handshakeDeadline = 0L

  @volatile private var unackedNow = 0
  @volatile private var stateNow: StreamState = if settings.enabled then StreamState.Disconnected else StreamState.Disabled
  @volatile private var inferenceNow = false
  @volatile var onVerdict: Messages.Verdict => Unit = _ => ()

  def state: StreamState = stateNow
  def enabled: Boolean = settings.enabled
  /** The server runs a model; windows should carry the `infer` purpose. */
  def inference: Boolean = inferenceNow
  def dropped: Long = droppedWindows.get
  def backlog: Int = queuedWindows.get
  /** Frames sent or waiting that the backend has not acknowledged yet. */
  def unacknowledged: Int = unackedNow

  def start(): Unit =
    if settings.enabled then
      if settings.token.isEmpty then log.warning("backend.token is empty; the backend will refuse the stream")
      exec.scheduleWithFixedDelay(() => guarded(pump()), PumpMs, PumpMs, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)

  def stop(): Unit =
    exec.execute: () =>
      // Send what is queued (e.g. the final CollectStop) before closing, if the stream is up.
      frameInbox()
      if stateNow == StreamState.Ready then transmit()
      val s = socket
      if s != null then s.sendClose(WebSocket.NORMAL_CLOSURE, "shutdown")
      socket = null
    exec.shutdown()
    exec.awaitTermination(15, TimeUnit.SECONDS)

  // ---- producer API, any thread ----

  def playerJoined(m: Messages.PlayerJoin): Unit =
    players.put(m.handle, m)
    control(MessageType.PlayerJoin, m)

  def playerLeft(handle: Int): Unit =
    players.remove(handle)
    control(MessageType.PlayerLeave, Messages.PlayerLeave(handle))

  def sessionStarted(m: Messages.CollectStart): Unit =
    sessions.put(m.session, m)
    control(MessageType.CollectStart, m)

  def sessionStopped(m: Messages.CollectStop): Unit =
    sessions.remove(m.session)
    control(MessageType.CollectStop, m)

  def mark(m: Messages.Mark): Unit = control(MessageType.Mark, m)

  /** Queues a window; drops it when the backlog is full rather than growing without bound. */
  def window(w: Window): Unit =
    if !settings.enabled then return
    if queuedWindows.incrementAndGet() > MaxQueuedWindows then
      queuedWindows.decrementAndGet()
      droppedWindows.incrementAndGet()
    else inbox.add(Win(w))

  private def control[A: JsonValueCodec](tpe: Int, m: A): Unit =
    if settings.enabled then inbox.add(Control(tpe, Messages.encode(m)))

  // ---- stream thread ----

  private def pump(): Unit =
    frameInbox()
    if unackedBytes > settings.resendBufferBytes then resetStream("resend buffer overflow")
    if stateNow == StreamState.Handshaking && System.currentTimeMillis() >= handshakeDeadline then
      disconnected("no WELCOME from backend")
    stateNow match
      case StreamState.Ready => transmit()
      case StreamState.Disconnected if System.currentTimeMillis() >= reconnectAt => connect()
      case _ => ()

  /** Turns queued messages into numbered frames, batching windows between control messages. */
  private def frameInbox(): Unit =
    val batch = ArrayBuffer[Window]()
    var batchBytes = 0
    def flushBatch(): Unit =
      if batch.nonEmpty then
        batch.foreach: w =>
          w.id = (nextWindow & 0xffffffffL).toInt
          nextWindow += 1
        add(codec.encodeWindows(nextSeq, batch), windows = true)
        batch.clear()
        batchBytes = 0
    var out = inbox.poll()
    while out != null do
      out match
        case Control(tpe, body) =>
          flushBatch()
          add(codec.encode(tpe, nextSeq, body), windows = false)
        case Win(w) =>
          queuedWindows.decrementAndGet()
          if batchBytes + w.wireBytes > BatchBytes then flushBatch()
          batch += w
          batchBytes += w.wireBytes
      out = inbox.poll()
    flushBatch()

  private def add(bytes: Array[Byte], windows: Boolean): Unit =
    unacked.addLast(Frame(nextSeq, bytes, windows))
    unackedBytes += bytes.length
    unackedNow = unacked.size
    nextSeq += 1

  private def transmit(): Unit =
    val s = socket
    if s == null then return
    val it = unacked.iterator()
    while it.hasNext do
      val f = it.next()
      if f.seq > transmittedThrough then
        if !send(s, f.bytes) then return
        transmittedThrough = f.seq

  private def send(s: WebSocket, bytes: Array[Byte]): Boolean =
    try
      s.sendBinary(ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes), true).get(SendTimeoutSec, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
      true
    catch
      case e: Exception =>
        disconnected(s"send failed: ${rootMessage(e)}")
        false

  private def connect(): Unit =
    stateNow = StreamState.Connecting
    val listener = Listener()
    http
      .newWebSocketBuilder()
      .header("Authorization", s"Bearer ${settings.token}")
      .header("Eigen-Protocol", "1")
      .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
      .buildAsync(settings.url, listener)
      .whenComplete: (ws, error) =>
        exec.execute: () =>
          if error != null then disconnected(s"connect failed: ${rootMessage(error)}")
          else
            socket = ws
            stateNow = StreamState.Handshaking
            handshakeDeadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + HandshakeTimeoutMs
            val _ = send(ws, codec.encode(MessageType.Hello, 0, Messages.hello(streamId, plugin, platform)))

  private def onMessage(bytes: Array[Byte]): Unit =
    FrameCodec.decode(bytes, maxFrameBytes) match
      case Left(problem) => disconnected(s"bad frame from server: $problem")
      case Right(frame) =>
        frame.tpe match
          case MessageType.Welcome => Messages.decode[Messages.Welcome](frame.payload).fold(p => disconnected(s"bad WELCOME: $p"), welcome)
          case MessageType.Ack if frame.payload.length >= 4 => acknowledge(ByteSink.readU32(frame.payload, 0))
          case MessageType.Verdict =>
            Messages.decode[Messages.Verdict](frame.payload).foreach(v => guarded(onVerdict(v)))
          case MessageType.Error =>
            Messages.decode[Messages.ServerError](frame.payload).foreach: e =>
              log.warning(s"Backend error ${e.code}: ${e.message}")
              if e.code == "UNAUTHORIZED" then backoffMs = MaxBackoffMs
              if e.fatal then disconnected(s"backend closed the stream (${e.code})")
          case other => log.fine(f"Ignoring unknown message type 0x$other%02x")

  private def welcome(w: Messages.Welcome): Unit =
    if w.protocol != 1 then
      disconnected(s"backend speaks protocol ${w.protocol}")
      return
    maxFrameBytes = w.maxFrameBytes
    inferenceNow = w.inference
    if w.resumeFrom >= nextSeq then
      // The server has applied more than we ever sent on this stream: it is not ours to resume.
      resetStream("backend is ahead of this stream")
      return
    acknowledge(w.resumeFrom)
    transmittedThrough = w.resumeFrom
    stateNow = StreamState.Ready
    backoffMs = MinBackoffMs
    log.info(s"Connected to the Eigen backend (resuming after #${w.resumeFrom}, inference ${if w.inference then "on" else "off"})")
    transmit()

  private def acknowledge(seq: Long): Unit =
    while !unacked.isEmpty && unacked.peekFirst().seq <= seq do
      unackedBytes -= unacked.pollFirst().bytes.length
    unackedNow = unacked.size

  /** Starts a fresh stream, replaying live players and sessions so references stay valid. */
  private def resetStream(reason: String): Unit =
    val windows = unacked.asScala.count(_.windows)
    log.warning(s"Starting a new backend stream ($reason); dropping $windows unacknowledged window batches")
    streamId = newStreamId()
    nextSeq = 1
    unacked.clear()
    unackedBytes = 0
    unackedNow = 0
    transmittedThrough = 0
    players.values().asScala.toSeq.sortBy(_.handle).foreach(m => add(codec.encode(MessageType.PlayerJoin, nextSeq, Messages.encode(m)), windows = false))
    sessions.values().asScala.toSeq.sortBy(_.session).foreach(m => add(codec.encode(MessageType.CollectStart, nextSeq, Messages.encode(m)), windows = false))
    backoffMs = MinBackoffMs
    disconnected("new stream")

  private def disconnected(reason: String): Unit =
    val s = socket
    socket = null
    if s != null then s.abort()
    if stateNow != StreamState.Disconnected then log.warning(s"Backend stream down: $reason; retrying in ${backoffMs / 1000}s")
    stateNow = StreamState.Disconnected
    inferenceNow = false
    reconnectAt = System.currentTimeMillis() + backoffMs + (math.random() * backoffMs * 0.2).toLong
    backoffMs = math.min(MaxBackoffMs, backoffMs * 2)

  private def guarded(body: => Unit): Unit =
    try body
    catch case e: Exception => log.log(Level.SEVERE, "Backend stream task failed", e)

  /** Receives frames on the HTTP client's threads and hands complete messages to the stream thread. */
  private final class Listener extends WebSocket.Listener:
    private val buffer = ByteArrayOutputStream()

    override def onOpen(ws: WebSocket): Unit = ws.request(1)

    override def onBinary(ws: WebSocket, data: ByteBuffer, last: Boolean): CompletionStage[?] | Null =
      val chunk = new Array[Byte](data.remaining())
      data.get(chunk)
      buffer.write(chunk)
      if buffer.size() > maxFrameBytes + 64 * 1024 then exec.execute(() => disconnected("oversized frame from server"))
      else if last then
        val message = buffer.toByteArray
        buffer.reset()
        exec.execute(() => guarded(if socket == ws then onMessage(message)))
      ws.request(1)
      null

    override def onText(ws: WebSocket, data: CharSequence, last: Boolean): CompletionStage[?] | Null =
      exec.execute(() => if socket == ws then disconnected("text frame from server"))
      null

    override def onClose(ws: WebSocket, code: Int, reason: String): CompletionStage[?] | Null =
      exec.execute(() => if socket == ws then disconnected(s"closed by backend ($code $reason)"))
      null

    override def onError(ws: WebSocket, error: Throwable): Unit =
      exec.execute(() => if socket == ws then disconnected(rootMessage(error)))

object StreamClient:
  private inline val PumpMs = 50L
  private inline val HandshakeTimeoutMs = 10_000L
  private inline val SendTimeoutSec = 10L
  private inline val MinBackoffMs = 1000L
  private inline val MaxBackoffMs = 300_000L
  private inline val BatchBytes = 512 * 1024
  private inline val MaxQueuedWindows = 20_000

  private val random = SecureRandom()

  private def newStreamId(): String = f"${random.nextLong()}%016x"

  private def rootMessage(e: Throwable): String =
    var c = e
    while c.getCause != null && c.getCause != c do c = c.getCause
    s"${c.getClass.getSimpleName}: ${c.getMessage}"

```

`paper/src/test/scala/eigen/paper/StreamLiveSuite.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper

import java.net.URI
import java.util.logging.Logger

import eigen.core.history.{Window, WindowKind}
import eigen.core.schema.Schema
import eigen.core.wire.Messages
import eigen.paper.config.BackendSettings
import eigen.paper.stream.{StreamClient, StreamState}

/**
 * End-to-end check against a running backend. Skipped unless `EIGEN_TEST_BACKEND`
 * (e.g. `ws://127.0.0.1:8787/v1/stream`) and `EIGEN_TEST_TOKEN` are set.
 */
class StreamLiveSuite extends munit.FunSuite:
  override val munitTimeout = scala.concurrent.duration.Duration(60, "s")

  private val backend = sys.env.get("EIGEN_TEST_BACKEND")
  private val token = sys.env.get("EIGEN_TEST_TOKEN")

  private def window(kind: WindowKind, rows: Int): Window =
    val layout = if kind == WindowKind.Calm then Schema.Calm else Schema.Combat
    val data = Array.tabulate(layout.cols.length, rows)((c, r) => if layout.cols(c).tpe.size == 4 then c + r * 0.5f else (r % 2).toFloat)
    Window(kind, layout, epoch = 1, engagement = if kind == WindowKind.Calm then 0 else 1, firstTick = 500, rows, anchor = if kind == WindowKind.Calm then -1 else rows / 2, data)

  private def awaitUntil(what: String)(cond: => Boolean): Unit =
    val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 20_000
    while !cond && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline do Thread.sleep(50)
    assert(cond, s"timed out waiting for $what")

  test("streams players, a session and windows, and every frame is acknowledged"):
    assume(backend.isDefined && token.isDefined, "EIGEN_TEST_BACKEND / EIGEN_TEST_TOKEN not set")
    val client = StreamClient(BackendSettings(enabled = true, URI(backend.get), token.get, 64L << 20), "eigen test", "munit", Logger.getLogger("test"))
    client.start()
    try
      awaitUntil("connection")(client.state == StreamState.Ready)
      val player = "ab" * 32
      client.playerJoined(Messages.PlayerJoin(1, player, 773, "vanilla"))
      client.sessionStarted(Messages.CollectStart(1, java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString, 1, "legit", Map("client" -> "vanilla"), "live test", System.currentTimeMillis()))
      for i <- 0 until 50 do
        val w = window(if i % 5 == 0 then WindowKind.Calm else WindowKind.Attack, if i % 5 == 0 then 300 else 40)
        w.handle = 1
        w.session = 1
        w.purpose = Window.PurposeCollect
        client.window(w)
      client.mark(Messages.Mark(1, 520, "on", "toggle"))
      client.sessionStopped(Messages.CollectStop(1, System.currentTimeMillis()))
      client.playerLeft(1)
      awaitUntil("acknowledgements")(client.backlog == 0 && client.unacknowledged == 0)
      assertEquals(client.state, StreamState.Ready)
      assertEquals(client.dropped, 0L)
    finally client.stop()

```

`paper/src/test/scala/eigen/paper/config/SettingsSuite.scala`:

```scala
/*
 * This file is part of Eigen.
 * Copyright (C) 2026 Eigen contributors
 *
 * Eigen is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify it under the terms of the
 * GNU General Public License as published by the Free Software Foundation, version 3.
 *
 * Eigen is distributed in the hope that it will be useful, but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without
 * even the implied warranty of MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE. See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along with Eigen. If not,
 * see <https://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */

package eigen.paper.config

import java.io.InputStreamReader
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.manager.server.ServerVersion
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.packettype.PacketType
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration

import eigen.config.Source
import eigen.paper.net.{PacketTable, ServerProtocol}

class SettingsSuite extends munit.FunSuite:

  private val salt = "ab" * 32

  /** The bundled `config.yml`, read exactly as Bukkit reads it, with a salt set as on first start. */
  private def bundled(): YamlConfiguration =
    val stream = getClass.getResourceAsStream("/config.yml")
    val config = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(InputStreamReader(stream, UTF_8))
    config.set("privacy.salt", salt)
    config

  private def settingsFrom(entries: Map[String, Any]): Either[String, Settings] =
    Settings.schema.from(Source.of(entries + ("privacy" -> Map("salt" -> salt))))

  test("the bundled config.yml reads back to the schema defaults"):
    val fromEmpty = settingsFrom(Map.empty)
    val fromBundled = Settings.schema.from(BukkitSource(bundled()))
    assert(fromEmpty.isRight && fromBundled.isRight, s"$fromEmpty / $fromBundled")
    assertEquals(fromBundled.map(_.backend), fromEmpty.map(_.backend))
    assertEquals(fromBundled.map(_.sim), fromEmpty.map(_.sim))
    assertEquals(fromBundled.map(_.sink), fromEmpty.map(_.sink))
    assertEquals(fromBundled.map(_.autoLegit), fromEmpty.map(_.autoLegit))
    assertEquals(fromBundled.map(_.shardCompat), fromEmpty.map(_.shardCompat))
    assertEquals(fromBundled.map(_.garnet), fromEmpty.map(_.garnet))
    assertEquals(fromBundled.map(_.exemptBedrock), fromEmpty.map(_.exemptBedrock))
    assertEquals(fromBundled.map(_.alertThreshold), fromEmpty.map(_.alertThreshold))
    assertEquals(fromBundled.map(_.sim.attackPre + fromBundled.toOption.get.sim.attackPost), Right(128))
    assertEquals(fromBundled.map(_.sim.engagePre + fromBundled.toOption.get.sim.engagePost), Right(128))

  test("a remote plaintext endpoint is refused, naming the key"):
    val result = settingsFrom(Map("backend" -> Map("url" -> "ws://eigen.example.com/v1/stream")))
    assertEquals(result.left.toOption, Some("backend.url: plaintext ws:// is allowed only for loopback hosts; use wss://"))

  test("loopback plaintext is allowed for local development"):
    assert(settingsFrom(Map("backend" -> Map("url" -> "ws://127.0.0.1:8787/v1/stream"))).isRight)

  test("an unknown sink names the allowed values"):
    val result = settingsFrom(Map("collect" -> Map("sink" -> "cloud")))
    assertEquals(result.left.toOption, Some("collect.sink: must be one of server, local, both, got 'cloud'"))

  test("a calm segment cannot be configured to end before it starts"):
    val result = settingsFrom(Map("detection" -> Map("calm" -> Map("min-ticks" -> 300, "max-ticks" -> 100))))
    assertEquals(result.left.toOption, Some("detection.calm.max-ticks: must be at least min-ticks"))

  test("the living-target switch maps to the simulation config"):
    val living = settingsFrom(Map("detection" -> Map("targets" -> "living")))
    assertEquals(living.map(_.sim.targetLiving), Right(true))

  test("a salt that is too short is rejected with a hint"):
    val result = Settings.schema.from(Source.of(Map("privacy" -> Map("salt" -> "abc"))))
    assertEquals(result.left.toOption, Some("privacy.salt: must be at least 64 hex digits; delete it to generate a new one"))

  test("garnet uri must use redis:// or rediss://"):
    val bad = settingsFrom(Map("garnet" -> Map("uri" -> "http://127.0.0.1:6379")))
    assertEquals(bad.left.toOption, Some("garnet.uri: must be redis:// or rediss://, got 'http://127.0.0.1:6379'"))
    val ok = settingsFrom(Map("garnet" -> Map("enabled" -> true, "uri" -> "rediss://garnet.internal:6379", "server" -> "duels-1")))
    assert(ok.isRight)
    assertEquals(ok.map(_.garnet.server), Right("duels-1"))

  test("collect.auto-legit enables automatic legit session recording"):
    val enabled = settingsFrom(Map("collect" -> Map("auto-legit" -> true)))
    assertEquals(enabled.map(_.autoLegit), Right(true))

  test("collect.shard-compat is disabled by default and configurable"):
    val defaultCfg = settingsFrom(Map.empty)
    assertEquals(defaultCfg.map(_.shardCompat.enabled), Right(false))
    val enabled = settingsFrom(Map("collect" -> Map("shard-compat" -> true, "shard-pre-window" -> 64, "shard-post-window" -> 96)))
    assertEquals(enabled.map(_.shardCompat.enabled), Right(true))
    assertEquals(enabled.map(_.shardCompat.preWindow), Right(64))
    assertEquals(enabled.map(_.shardCompat.postWindow), Right(96))

  test("ServerProtocol selects ModernPing on 1.17+ and LegacyWindowConfirmation on < 1.17"):
    val modern = ServerProtocol.forVersion(ServerVersion.V_1_21_4)
    assertEquals(modern, ServerProtocol.ModernPing)
    assertEquals(modern.livingFlagsIndex, 8)
    assertEquals(modern.outboundTransactionType, PacketType.Play.Server.PING)
    assertEquals(modern.inboundTransactionType, PacketType.Play.Client.PONG)

    val v116 = ServerProtocol.forVersion(ServerVersion.V_1_16_5)
    assertEquals(v116.livingFlagsIndex, 7)
    assertEquals(v116.outboundTransactionType, PacketType.Play.Server.WINDOW_CONFIRMATION)
    assertEquals(v116.inboundTransactionType, PacketType.Play.Client.WINDOW_CONFIRMATION)

    val v112 = ServerProtocol.forVersion(ServerVersion.V_1_12_2)
    assertEquals(v112.livingFlagsIndex, 6)
    assertEquals(v112.outboundTransactionType, PacketType.Play.Server.WINDOW_CONFIRMATION)

    val v18 = ServerProtocol.forVersion(ServerVersion.V_1_8_8)
    assertEquals(v18.livingFlagsIndex, 5)
    assertEquals(v18.outboundTransactionType, PacketType.Play.Server.WINDOW_CONFIRMATION)

  test("PacketTable dispatches in O(1) by enum ordinal and returns null for unrelated packet types"):
    import PacketType.Play.Client as C
    val table = PacketTable.build[C, String](C.values)(
      C.PONG -> "pong",
      C.ATTACK -> "attack",
      C.CLIENT_TICK_END -> "tick_end",
    )
    assertEquals(table.get(C.PONG), "pong")
    assertEquals(table.get(C.ATTACK), "attack")
    assertEquals(table.get(C.CLIENT_TICK_END), "tick_end")
    assertEquals(table.get(C.ANIMATION), null)
    assertEquals(table.get(PacketType.Play.Server.PING), null)
    assertEquals(table.get(PacketType.Configuration.Client.PLUGIN_MESSAGE), null)
```

```scala
/*
```
